# Waves UI consistency audit — 2026-09-07

**Scope.** Every React surface in `client/src` (admin portal, tech portal, customer portal + public token pages), read against the pinned worktree `~/wt-ui-audit` at `d77f6ac58` (origin/main, 2026-09-07 05:00 ET). Brief: Adam, 2026-09-07 08:01Z ("go through our React templates … UI consistency, bugs, mobile/desktop renders, fonts, style, colors").

**Method.** Phase 1 discovery ran 31 read-only agents in two workflow runs — 18 surface readers (one per page family, full-file reads with line citations) and 13 cross-cutting scanners (tokens/style, glass census, duplication, dead code, a11y, responsive, overlays, forms, tables/lists, states, perf/arch, navigation map, dev harness). Their 638 raw findings were merged and de-duplicated (same file, line ±3, same category) to 613, then every P0, every P1 and every P2 flagged `safe_to_autofix` (250 findings in 136 files) went through a refute-by-default verification pass (21 skeptic agents, each re-reading the cited code). A rendered census of 45 admin routes at desktop 1440 and mobile 390 (a render-census JSON and 90 screenshots kept with the session artifacts — not tracked; every number it backs is reproduced in section B) backs the typography and responsive numbers with what actually paints. Customer surfaces were rendered through the fixture harnesses (`/preview-*.html`).

**Headline numbers (post-merge, pre-verification).**

| Severity | Count | Flagged safe to autofix |
|---|---|---|
| P0 | 1 | 1 |
| P1 | 63 | 31 |
| P2 | 348 | 186 |
| P3 | 201 | 132 |

Verified counts and the confirmed bug/responsive/a11y lists are in sections D–F. Section J lists what this lane fixed; K ranks the rest.

---

| Entry | File | Notes |

| App | `client/index.html` → `src/main.jsx` → `src/App.jsx` | 68 `lazyWithRetry` routes; `PageErrorBoundary` wraps every route and the admin layout; `/*` catch-all is the customer `PortalPage` (it also swallows unknown `/admin/*` and `/tech/*`). |
| Fixture harnesses | 9 `client/preview-*.html` → `src/dev-preview/*-main.jsx` | Render the REAL portal, estimate, secure-appointment, service-report, project-report, irrigation, tokens and completion pages with stubbed fetch; no server needed. Admin and tech pages have no harness and no dev auth bypass. |

| Shell | File | Owns |
|---|---|---|
| Admin | `components/AdminLayoutV2.jsx` (inline styles over `styles/theme-square.css` vars) | 220px sidebar, mobile 52px top bar (zIndex 90) + 56px bottom tab bar (95) + drawer (100); `.admin-main` is the only scroll container (`--admin-vh`); `useAdminViewport` writes `--admin-vh` / `--vv-offset-top` / `--keyboard-inset`. | Customer | `components/brand/WavesShell.jsx` (18 pages) and `pages/PortalPage.jsx`'s own app shell (sticky glass bar, 760px main, desktop section nav, mobile BottomNav + MoreSheet; no footer by owner D5) | Glass is mounted by `useGlassSurface(true)` (`glass/glass-engine.js`); `glass/glass-theme.css` then overrides authored radii/type with `!important`. |

## B. Design-system inventory (what ships today, with the inconsistency evidence)

### Typography

**Families.** Admin renders 100% Roboto: `index.css:187-206` forces `Roboto !important` on `html.admin-app *`, including `code`, `pre` and `[class*=font-mono]`, so the 133 `'JetBrains Mono'` literals, 230 `MONO` constants, 8 `ROBOTO` constant copies and 30 files with literal Roboto stacks are inert in admin; `tailwind.config.js` still declares Inter as `font-sans` (10 files use it; also inert). Three admin pages inject Google Fonts `<link>`s for DM Sans / Montserrat / Poppins that nothing renders (`PPCDashboardPage.jsx:559`, `SEODashboardPage.jsx:760`, `EstimatePage.jsx:811` — the last in a dead branch). Tech: Nunito Sans body + Montserrat headings, 'DM Sans' set in three surfaces but never loaded. Customer: the glass sheet's system stack; `public/fonts/fonts.css` still ships Instrument Serif and Nunito faces with zero references.

**Sizes.** The design skill's hard line is 14px minimum readable text; `docs/design/admin-ui-consistency-contract.md` sets body 13 / label 12 / caption 11. The code follows neither consistently:

| Portal | 9px | 10px | 11px | 12px | 13px | Total sub-14 literals |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Admin | 53 | 313 | 919 | 1,335 | 938 | 3,577 (top: SEOPage 315, SchedulePage 298, InventoryPage 178, TaxPage 171) |
| Tech | – | 10 | 63 | 84 | 80 | 237 |
| Customer | 9 | 34 | 72 | 50 | 52 | 220 (mostly print documents + un-gated components) |

Rendered census (45 admin routes × 2 viewports): 1,851 elements paint at 12px, 627 at 11px, 237 at 10px, 20 at 9px. Inventory renders 646 sub-14 text nodes on one screen; pricing-logic 330; dashboard 315. Tier 1 primitives themselves encode 11–13px (`ui/Button` sm `text-11`, md `text-12`; `ui/Table` TH `text-11`; `ui/Input` `md:text-13`). Four Tailwind size classes that do not exist emit no CSS and silently inherit: `text-10` (86 sites), `text-15` (22), `text-20` (13), `text-24` (5). **Owner ruling needed (see K): which floor governs Tier 1 — the contract's 11/12/13 or the 14px hard line.**

**Weights.** Contract: Tier 1 = 400/500 only. Literals in the tree: 700 × 1,548, 600 × 277, 800 × 36, 850 × 9, 900 × 4, plus fractional 620/640/650/750. Rendered: 807 elements at 700 and 450 at 600 across the admin census; `/admin/agents` alone paints 71 at 700, `/admin/pricing-logic` 43. Customer weights >700 outside the brand gate: `App.jsx` (850 ×7), `StationMapCard`, `NewsletterSignup`, `InstallPrompt` (800), `theme-brand.js BUTTON_BASE` 800.

**Scale sources (four).** Tailwind `fontSize` keys 11/12/13/14/16/18/22/28; `brand-tokens.css` `--text-xs…xl` (0 consumers); `theme-doc.js FS`; glass sheet h1 `clamp(32px,4vw,40px)` / h2 26 / h3 20 / eyebrow 14 (`!important`, overrides every authored heading on 18 shell pages).

### Color

| Portal | Hex literals | Top values |
|---|---|---|
| Admin | 2,203 | `#FFFFFF` 243, `#18181B` 210, `#E4E4E7` 158, `#71717A` 110, `#F4F4F5` 99, `#991B1B` 71 (SchedulePage 180, ProjectsPage 138) |
| Tech | 279 | `#ef4444` 31, `#f59e0b` 20, `#1e293b` 20, `#0ea5e9` 20 |
| Customer | 1,400 | `#E7E2D7` 155, `#04395E` 113, `#D8D0C0` 64 (PortalPage 284, ReportViewPage 102) |

Brand hex written outside theme files: `#04395E` in 34 files, `#0A7EC2` in 30, `#E7E2D7` in 17, `#C8312F` in 14, `#F0A500` in 15. Token sources are seven-way split (`tailwind.config.js`, `styles/design-tokens.css` [mostly dead], `styles/brand-tokens.css`, `styles/theme-square.css` [all 13 vars live], `theme-brand.js` [24 dead exports], `theme-customer.js` + `components/estimate/tokens.js`, `theme-doc.js`, `glass/glass-theme.css`). Muted text: `#71717A` on white ≈ 4.7:1 (passes), but `#A1A1AA` (28 sites) ≈ 2.5:1 and `#94A3B8` (46 sites) ≈ 2.9:1 fail AA; 642 sites pair `D.muted` with 11–12px text. Semantic colour outside the sanctioned exceptions: dashboard chart palette (documented), Customers V2 health/tier/stage (documented), dispatch time-grid `#3B82F6/#1E40AF` blocks (undocumented), `#DCFCE7/#166534` prepaid chips in 4 schedule files (undocumented), Tier 2 green/amber/red chips and status text everywhere.

### Spacing and page width

Shell padding: admin 28/24 desktop, 16 mobile; no shell-owned content cap. Pages cap themselves eight different ways: `max-w-[1300px]` class (8 pages), inline `maxWidth:1300` (13), 1500 (Contracts, AgentEstimate), 1320, 1200, 1100, 1000, `max-w-6xl`, `max-w-7xl`, and 10 route pages uncapped (Dashboard, Customers, Pipeline, Reviews, PPC, SEO, Blog, Referrals, Settings, Newsletter). Double padding layers: `EstimateToolViewV2` adds `px-4 md:px-7` inside the shell's 16/28; `CommercialProposalPage` `px-4`; five Tier 2 agents-hub tabs add 24px; `LawnProtocolCommandCenterPage` `px-4` when embedded; `AdminPriceChangePage` `p-6` inside the hub. Customer columns: 760 (documents/portal), 640 (flows), 480 (`/book`), 420 (`/rate`), 560 (pay-page error states only).

### Radius

Literal sites: admin 1,657 (0×25, 2×27, 3×27, 4×151, 5×14, 6×388, 8×574, 10×157, 12×170, 16×12, 20×16, 999×46, 9999×15), tech 173, customer 975 (8×329, 10×153, 12×77, 14×12, 16×33, 999×86, 50%×61). Six token definitions disagree: Tailwind xs3/sm4/md6/lg8; `design-tokens.css` sm4/md6/lg8/xl12; `brand-tokens.css` sm6/md8/lg8/xl8 (loaded last, so `--radius-md`=8 and `--radius-xl`=8 win → `BrandCard` renders r8 while `theme-doc RADIUS.card`=12 and `DocumentActionBar` is r16); `theme-doc RADIUS` tag6/input8/button10/card12/modal16; `theme-brand RADIUS` md6…3xl24/button12; admin contract controls 4 / cards 6 / hero 8. Rendered admin census: 4px 1,022 · 6px 363 · 3px 266 · 12px 215 · 8px 188 · 50% 81 · 9999 39 · 10px 39. Overlay panels alone use 4, 6, 10, 16 (admin), 8, 16, 24 (customer), 12–18 (tech).

### Shadows and blur

309 `box-shadow` usages with **146 distinct values**; the only tokens (`--shadow-soft`, `SHADOW.card`, `PORTAL_SHELL.shadowSoft`, `theme-brand SHADOWS`) have 3–4 consumers each and `theme-brand SHADOWS` has zero external importers. 86 `backdrop-filter` sites: `glass-theme.css` 25, `glass-components.css` 18, `PortalPage` 15, `CardPage` 4, plus one-offs in NotificationBell, InstallPrompt, PublicFunnelTracking, GlobalCommandPalette (admin), ReviewVelocityEngine (admin) and two Tailwind `backdrop-blur` in admin bars. Seventeen distinct blur/saturate pairs (canonical 32px/185% desktop, 20px mobile; soft 18/165; chip 18/170; accent 18/175; modal 36/190; scrim 8) — see G.

| `components/brand/BrandButton` | 3 pages (PayPageV2, StatementPayPage, ContractSignPage) | 48px, weight 600, auto-tags `data-glass-accent` / `chip`; `glass-theme.css:462` forces chip min-height 40 over its inline 48 |
| `components/Button.jsx` + `styles/buttons.css` (legacy `.btn`) | PublicBookingPage, RatePage, ReviewPage (unrouted), StickyBottomCTA (dead) | `nav`/`utility` variants and `[data-surface=admin]` block (DM Sans + teal) have 0 consumers |
| `theme-doc docButton()` | 6 files | customer document CTAs |

### Inputs and forms

`ui/Input` `h-11 text-16` on mobile → `md:h-9 md:text-13`; `Select` matches with an SVG caret; `Checkbox`/`Radio` 16px + 44px `u-touch-hit`; `Switch` is `role=switch`. `index.css:45-62` forces 16px on every input under `pointer: coarse` and 44px min-height on admin inputs. Adoption: 17 files use `Input`, 24 `Select`; **829 raw `<input>` in 137 files**; local input class constants in EstimateToolViewV2 (`INPUT_CLS` + `InputV2/SelectV2`), CustomersPageV2, NewsletterTabs, CustomerPropertiesPanelV2. Labels: 256 placeholder-only inputs (SchedulePage 39, InventoryPage 31), 672 `<label>` without `htmlFor` (some wrap the control and are fine; InventoryPage 31/0, CreateAppointmentModal quick-add, FieldLeadModal are verified sibling-label misses). Phone fields: 11 `type=tel`, 4 phone inputs with no `type=tel`. Money inputs: 47 `type=number` without `step`, 10 with `inputMode=decimal`. Error surfacing: **294 `window.alert()` and 142 `window.confirm()`** (SchedulePage 69, EstimatePage 40, TaxPage 20, EstimateToolViewV2 15 validation alerts); the good patterns exist (LoginPage `role=alert` + `aria-describedby`; PublicBookingPage phone `aria-invalid`; PayPageV2 `role=alert`). `BrandInput` has an `error` prop that only recolours the border and 0 consumers.

### Cards

`ui/Card` 33–51 files (CardFooter 0 consumers); `BrandCard` 7 files; `estimateCard()` 13 files; PortalPage 13 local card objects (10 byte-identical `#E7E2D7`/r8); six report-V2 folders each with an r16 `card` object and a 14/700/.08em `eyebrow` (vs `DOC_EYEBROW` 14/600/.06em); 8 more local card/panel styles (WeeklyWateringPlanCard, ProjectReportViewPage, LawnAssessmentPanel, PricingRealityCheckPage, EquipmentCalibrationPanel, AutoDispatchPage, WdoSignaturePad, AdminInvoicesPage). The glass sheet forces r12 on every `[data-glass]` regardless of the authored radius, so most inline card radii on customer pages are dead code.

### Navigation and tabs

One registry (`adminNavigation.js`) drives desktop sidebar, mobile tabs, More page and the role guard — good. Below it: `ui/Tabs` has 4 consumers (click-only tablist, no arrow keys, no `aria-controls`); 19 files map their own tab arrays with 0 `role=tab`; the marketing pages alone use **nine** leaf-tab styles (Blog/Social/Newsletter/Reviews share one byte-identical 36px uppercase row; SEOWorkspaceNav 12px pill strip; GBP 14px/700 grey pill; PPC slate segmented control; SEODashboard centered segmented; ContentRegistry 12px/700 buttons; Recruiting `Button` + `role=tab`; autonomous-content PillTab); the four ops pages (Inventory, Equipment, TimeTracking, Referrals) copy a fourth inline pill row instead of `AdminCommandHeader secondarySections`. Tab state is URL-backed in about half the pages (Blog, Newsletter, Knowledge, Equipment, Compliance, ServiceLibrary, hubs) and `useState`-only in the rest (SEOPage 16 leaves, SocialMedia 10, Reviews, Ads, GBP 9, TimeTracking, PricingStrategy) — refresh loses the tab and bell links cannot deep-link. Horizontal strips: three mechanisms (`HorizontalScroll.jsx`, `.u-scroll-strip`, `.tab-pill-scroll`).

### Tables and lists

`ui/Table` in 10 files; raw `<table>` 152 elements in 47 admin files (SEOPage 27, InventoryPage 14, TimeTracking 9, EquipmentMaintenance 7, WinLossSlicesCard 7), 0 in tech, 2 on customer surfaces. **`aria-sort` appears nowhere in client/src**; the two sortable headers are `<th onClick>` (ScheduleListView ×6) and a Name-only button (CustomersPageV2). Eight pagination implementations plus four load-more variants, all different labels/sizes; four lists hard-cap with no paging (CallLog 200 rows/365 days, Estimates `limit=all`, Tax mileage 200, Inventory products). Unwrapped tables that scroll the page sideways on phones: TimeTracking ×4, Banking, GBPManagement, ToolHealth, WinLossSlicesCard ×7, SourcePerformanceCard ×2, EmailAutomationsPanelV2; Compliance/Discounts `sTableWrap` uses `overflow: hidden` (clips). Nine per-file `thStyle/tdStyle` copies (th at 10, 11 or 12px, uppercase or not). No table has a stacked/card mobile treatment; number alignment is left in Inventory/TimeTracking/Tax, right in Banking/SEO, centred in CustomersPageV2.

| Side sheet | `ui/Sheet` (`layer` prop, 100dvh, insets) | 6 files (JobDrawer, TechDrawer, PayerDetailSheet, PhotoAssessmentDetailSheet, InsertLinkSheet…); hand-rolled: ScheduleCustomerSidebar 430px, TreatmentPlanPanel 760px, CustomerEstimatesPanel 480px, Customer 360 900px, CustomerSmsPanel 420px, VisitProtocol 600px, ReviewVelocityEngine drawer |
| Bottom sheet | none | Dispatch More sheet, QuickActionMenu, EstimatesPageV2 pickers ×3, MarkPrepaidModal, RainOutSheet, ServiceOutlineComposerModal, tech ×5, TechRecapCapture + SchedulePage duplicate chip sheet |
| Full-screen phone sheet | none | 12 `components/schedule/Mobile*Sheet.jsx` files (only one has `role=dialog`, none `aria-modal`, none focus trap, z ladder 100/105/110/115, header 56 vs 64px, four close idioms), MobileNewCustomerSheet (correct), CreateAppointmentModal (correct) |
| Customer confirm | `CustomerDialogHost` (alertdialog, `useLockBodyScroll`) | 7 consumers |

47 raw overlays in 25 files have no dialog role, no focus trap, no Escape; 25 distinct z-index values (50 → 99999) and 18 backdrop recipes are in use; scroll lock is `useLockBodyScroll` (iOS-safe) in 4 places, `body.style.overflow` (ignored by iOS Safari for touch) in the two `ui` primitives and 6 more, and nothing in every other overlay. Same task, different kind: money actions are a `ui/Dialog` (CancelPlan), a hand-rolled centre/full-screen modal (360 Refund/Prepay), a bottom sheet on mobile (MarkPrepaid) and a full-screen sheet (PrepaySwitch).

### Badges and status

`ui/Badge` 55–67 files (tones neutral/alert/strong; 9 call sites pass a `muted` tone that does not exist and render unstyled); 28 local Badge/Pill/Chip/Tag definitions, four byte-identical Tier 2 `Chip` copies (DataHygiene, ContentRegistry, PriceMatch, AgentDecisions) plus two tone-object variants (AgentOps, AgentShadowDrafts); report `StatusPill` duplicated in lawnV2 and treeShrubV2; EstimatesPageV2 `StatusPillV3` behind a per-user flag adds a third filter vocabulary. Colour-only status with no text/aria: NotificationBell unread dot, ActionInbox critical-vs-warning dot, CustomersPageV2 alert-stage dot.

### Loading, empty, error, toast

No Spinner / Skeleton / EmptyState / ErrorState / Toast primitive exists in `components/ui`. Loading copy: `Loading…` 90 sites + `Loading...` 43; admin list pages replace the whole table with a one-line text node (layout jump) — only AgentEstimatePage and IntelligenceBarShell use skeleton bars; customer token pages use six hand-rolled static skeleton components. Toasts: 15 fixed-position copies (top-right green 13px, bottom-right zinc, bottom-right green + shadow, top-center) in 37 files rendering `position:fixed`, only one with `role=status`; every one sits at `bottom:20` with no safe-area. Error handling: 116 silent `catch` sites in `pages/admin`; verified "error renders as empty" cases in DiscountsTabs stats, RevenuePage (dead), LawnAssessmentPanel customers, CallLogTabV2 search, EquipmentMaintenance lists, LeadsTabs ROI, CompliancePage lookup, SEODashboardPage ("No SEO data yet" on a 500), AdsPage/PPC ("Connect your Google Ads account" on any failure); three writes fail invisibly (InlineTechPicker, EquipmentMaintenance record + mileage). Native dialogs: 294 `alert()`, 142 `confirm()` (customer pages: 0).

### Z-index

45 distinct values; primitives sit at `z-[120]` (Dialog/Sheet) while pages override with 1000/1100/1120 (Customer 360), 1000 (EstimatesPageV2), 9998/9999 (palette, NotificationBell, InlineTechPicker), `99999 !important` (EstimatePage, EstimateToolViewV2). The one P0 of this audit is a direct consequence (CancelPlanDialog at 120 under the 360 at 1000).

## G. Waves glass UI specification

There is **one** glass system of record — `client/src/glass/glass-theme.css` tiers mounted by `useGlassSurface` — adopted by 24 customer routes and 60 files through `data-glass` markup. The fragmentation is at the edges: the estimate DOM walker (`EstimateGlassTheme.jsx`, re-runs `getComputedStyle` over `#root *` on every mutation), `.gc-*` classes that re-declare chip/accent material, ~20 inline frosted surfaces, six scrim recipes, CardPage's private dark glass, BiometricGate's copied scene, and four admin blur sites. This section is the standard those edges collapse into. Attribute names stay as they are so no consumer moves.

### Surfaces

| Tier | Attribute | Material | Radius | Min size | Used for |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Scene | `useGlassSurface(true)` | html background = 5 radial + 1 linear gradient, 5 orbs `filter: blur(70px)`, dot grid, fixed grain layer, pointer parallax | — | — | Every customer route; export `GLASS_SCENE_BACKGROUND` / `GLASS_SCENE_ORBS` from `glass-engine.js` so BiometricGate stops hand-copying them |
| Nav / shell | `data-glass="nav"` (new; today `""`, `"soft"`, `.gc-mbb` and inline recipes) | modal material at blur 22 / sat 180, `#fff .96` fallback, safe-area padding | 12 (999 with `data-glass-pill`) | — | Sticky header, fixed bottom tab bar, mobile book bar, InstallPrompt floating action |
| Card | `data-glass="card"` (or bare `data-glass`) | gradient `#fff .30→.08` over `rgba(244,239,224,.36)` warm tint; border `#fff .62`; blur 32 / sat 185 (20 below 640px); shadow `0 28px 90px navy .18 + 0 5px 18px navy .07` + 4 inset highlights; hover specular `::before`, sheen `::after` | 12 | — | Page sections; `BrandCard` is its component |
| Inner / raised | `data-glass="soft"` | gradient `.38→.15` over the warm tint; blur 18 / sat 165 (14 mobile); shadow `0 8px 26px navy .08` | 12 (10 when nested) | — | Tiles inside a card. **Rule:** when nested inside a `[data-glass]` parent, tint + border only — no second `backdrop-filter` (stacked blur is the single heaviest cost on ReportViewPage: 28 unconditional surfaces + 5 orbs) |
| Chip | `data-glass="chip"` + `data-glass-pill` | gradient `.34→.10` over `#fff .22`; blur 18 / sat 170 | 12 / 999 | 40 (44 pill) | Choice chips (frequency, filters); `BrandButton secondary` emits it; fold `.gc-freq-btn`, `.gc-tech-chip`, `.gc-scarcity` into it |
| Action | `data-glass-accent` (+ pill) | gold gradient `rgba(255,222,120,.6)→rgba(244,176,20,.45)` over `rgba(240,165,0,.38)`; border `rgba(255,238,180,.92)`; blur 18 / sat 175; ink `#04395E` | 10 (999 pill) | 44 | Every gold CTA; `BrandButton primary` emits it; fold `.gc-section-cta`, `.gc-mbb-btn`, `.gc-freq-btn[data-active]` |
| Modal | `data-glass="modal"` | gradient `.55→.28` over `#fff .50`; border `#fff .75`; blur 36 / sat 190; shadow `0 40px 120px navy .35` | 24 | — | Dialogs and sheets (CustomerDialogHost, AutopayCard, NotificationBell already comply) |
| Popover | `data-glass="modal" data-glass-popover` (new alias) | modal material, no lift | 12 | — | Property listbox, notification dropdown, account menu (today three ad-hoc radii 8/16/24) |
| Scrim | `data-glass-scrim` | radial `#fff .08` over `rgba(4,57,94,.38)`; **blur 8px made `!important`** and added to the fallback blocks | — | — | Every overlay backdrop; delete the six inline scrims (`slate .42/.48 + blur 5`, `slate .48 + blur 6`, `black .5`, `black .55 + blur 4`) |
| Input | `data-glass="input"` (new; promote the `.waves-ask-form input` recipe) | `#fff .8`, border navy `.16`, 48px, 16px text, italic 14px placeholder | 10 | 48 | `BrandInput` emits it |
| Clear | `data-glass-clear` | transparent | — | — | unchanged |

### Typography under glass (unchanged, now written down)

h1 `clamp(32px, 4vw, 40px)`, h2 26, h3 20, eyebrow 14 / 600 / .06em uppercase (`data-gt="eyebrow"`), body 16 minimum, labels 14 minimum, system font stack, ink `#04395E` (never remapped), muted `#475569` inside the glass scope. The sheet applies these with `!important`, so authoring a different heading size or card radius inline is dead code; write to the tokens instead.

### Interaction states

Hover: card specular only (no lift on mobile ≤640, already suppressed). Focus: `.waves-focus-ring` / `:focus-visible` 2px ring in navy — never `outline: none` without it (`glass-theme.css:502` is the one rule that drops the ring; 15 inline `outline:'none'` keys elsewhere). Active: accent darkens one step; chips flip to accent when selected. Disabled: 50% opacity, no blur change. Loading: `SkeletonCard` keeps the card's height (CLS fix pattern from EstimateViewPage).

### Where glass is NOT used

- **Admin and tech never carry `backdrop-filter`.** The two admin mobile bars (`StickyActionBar`, Customer 360 action bar) go solid; the two admin scrims (palette, ReviewVelocityEngine) align to the `ui` overlay (`bg-zinc-900/30`, no blur).
- Data-dense tables and forms inside modals sit on the opaque inner surface, not on nested glass.
- Print (`@media print`), `prefers-reduced-transparency`, `forced-colors` and `@supports not (backdrop-filter)` already provide solid fallbacks for card/soft/chip/accent — extend them to scrim, nav and input so no hand-rolled surface is left without one.
- Documents rendered for PDF (`?mode=pdf`, `EstimateProposalDocument`, `ServiceReportDocument`) stay non-glass by design.

### Enforcement

Extend `scripts/check-portal-brand.js` to `.css` under `client/src/glass` and `components/estimate/glass`, and to `components/NotificationBell.jsx`, `components/InstallPrompt.jsx`, `components/VanScene.jsx`, `components/analytics/`; forbid inline `backdropFilter` in customer files (the tiers own the material) and any `backdrop-filter` / `backdrop-blur` under `pages/admin`, `components/admin`, `pages/tech`, `components/tech`. Sequence: small safe folds (scene export, scrim authority, popover alias, input tier) → fold `.gc-*` into tiers → nested-blur cap (screenshot-verified on ReportViewPage) → retire the estimate walker → owner ruling on CardPage's dark glass.

## I. Screens and pages that need redesign (not just fixes)

Ranked by daily use and by how many independent readers landed on the same screen. Each entry says why a local fix will not do and what the redesign is.

1. **Customer 360 (`components/admin/Customer360ProfileV2.jsx`, 8,474 lines).** Six readers converged here. Two navigation systems live inside one panel (a 7-tab strip plus a 6-chip timeline filter and a decorative 3-step stepper on Contracts); on phones a top bar and a bottom bar both carry Text/Call. Five sub-modals are hand-rolled fixed overlays with no dialog role or focus trap, and Escape or a backdrop click inside any of them closes the whole profile (C360-06 / C360-02). The Health Radar plots hardcoded values while the real sub-scores sit in the payload; "Add Note" (header pill and ⋯ menu) lands on the SMS composer; the Comms tab duplicates a weaker composer than `CustomerSmsPanel` and never marks texts read; date columns render a day early for ET viewers; 36 raw inputs, 14 raw selects and 7 identical black pill buttons bypass the primitives; the Map view (Tier 2 dark) and Health view (third palette) render inside the Tier 1 page. **Redesign:** one navigation model (tabs only, timeline filter as a segmented control inside its tab), every sub-modal on `ui/Dialog` with `layer` (this lane's P0 PR proves the path), financial actions grouped under Billing, the SMS panel reused, real health data or no radar, and the profile moved onto `ui/Sheet` so it inherits focus/Escape semantics instead of a window listener.

2. **Pipeline → Estimator (`EstimatesPageV2.jsx` + `LeadsTabs.jsx` + `EstimateToolViewV2.jsx`).** The Leads tab is inline-styled with its own `C` palette inside the Tier 1 Pipeline page, so one page shows two visual systems (12px-radius cards / 9999 pills next to the 4px/6px contract). The mobile estimates list drops Follow Up, Mark lost, Schedule, proposal and preview actions that desktop rows carry (PE-01). "Convert to Customer" asks the operator to paste a customer UUID (PE-02) while mark-accepted and schedule-appointment already convert leads automatically. Lead prefill passes a street-only address and never carries `customer_id`, so information is lost between lead → estimate → customer. The estimator on a phone (the tech path) never collapses its eight `grid-cols-3` field groups. The entire `pages/admin/pipeline/*` Unified Pipeline directory (2,660 lines) has been unrouted since 2026-05-25. **Redesign:** one row-action model shared by desktop and mobile, lead → estimate handoff that carries the lead id and full address, remove or rebuild "Convert to Customer" (owner ruling), Leads tab migrated to Tier 1, estimator form grids responsive, and a ruling on the Unified Pipeline (delete or return).

3. **Communications hub (`CommunicationsPageV2.jsx` + Email tab).** The Email tab (`EmailPage` + `email/*`) is the last inline-styled Tier 2 island rendered inside a Tier 1 page, with its own `AdminCommandHeader`, stat bar, filter chips and modal. On phones the SMS composer card sits above the thread list (600px to scroll past), the Calls stat filters are hidden with no substitute, and conversation/log/search rows are click-only divs (102 such rows across the surface). Four different customer-search dropdowns, five `timeAgo` copies, and ET vs browser-local timestamps on the same page. **Redesign:** docked reply composer under the conversation (the `CustomerSmsPanel` pattern), Email tab to Tier 1, one search combobox, one time helper, keyboard-reachable rows.

4. **Schedule / Dispatch mobile sheets (`components/schedule/Mobile*Sheet.jsx`, 12 files).** Ten full-screen sheets hand-roll their shell with five header variants, four close idioms, a 100/105/110/115 z ladder, no `role=dialog`, no focus trap, no scroll lock; "Back" from Checkout lands on the list instead of the appointment. The cash numpad is decorative (the amount never reaches the server). **Redesign:** one `MobileSheetFrame` owning role/aria/focus/z and a 56px + safe-area header; Checkout → Payment → tender as a real stack with Back; owner rulings on the numpad and the legacy POS-placeholder rows.

5. **Dispatch board (`DispatchBoardLayout.jsx`).** Sized `h-[calc(100vh-64px)]` inside a shell with no 64px bar, so the board is always clipped and sits under the mobile tab bar; below `xl` drag-to-reassign has no drop target because the roster pane is `display:none`; Board is the default tab at every width though its header comment declares mobile out of scope. **Redesign:** `flex-1 min-h-0` against `--admin-vh`, a phone board that defaults to Schedule or offers a real reassign affordance.

6. **Admin ops Tier 2 pages (Inventory 5,845 lines, TimeTracking 5,402, Tax 5,245, SEO 8,402, Settings, Equipment, Compliance, Referrals).** Each carries its own `D` copy, `sCard/sBtn/sBadge/sInput/thS/tdS`, a hand-copied 36px uppercase leaf-tab row, a module-level `isMobile` snapshot (6 files), 10px table headers and hundreds of 11–13px runs, `alert()` outcomes and fixed toasts with no safe-area. **Redesign:** these are migration candidates, one page per PR, onto `AdminCommandHeader secondarySections` + `ui/Table` + `ui/State` + `useToast`; until then the shared `adminLegacy.js` styles stop the drift.

7. **Marketing hub (SEO, Blog, Social, Newsletter, Reviews, Ads/PPC, Knowledge, GBP).** Nine leaf-tab styles; sub-tab state lost on refresh in SEO (16 leaves), Social (10), Reviews, Ads, GBP (9); `AutonomousContentReviewPage` ships a forest-green/lime/cream brand hero and iPhone mockup inside `/admin`; SEO and PPC inject `<style>` `!important` overrides at 640px while the header switches at 768; PPC and SEODashboard duplicate views that also exist in Ads and SEO tabs; two pages inject Google Fonts nothing uses. **Redesign:** URL-backed tabs everywhere, one leaf-tab control, the autopilot hero on zinc, and an IA ruling on the duplicated dashboards.

8. **Tech portal home (`TechHomePage.jsx` + `VisitBriefPanel.jsx`).** The Next Stop action row packs up to six ~34px buttons with 12px labels in one non-wrapping row; in-brief actions are ~32px with an icon-only flag; five bottom sheets, the geofence prompt and the rain-out toast ignore safe areas; `100vh` inside a padded main adds blank scroll; no offline state anywhere; two incompatible API-base conventions. **Redesign:** one primary next action per stop (glanceability), 44px targets, a shared tech bottom-sheet with safe-area, a `Icon` set instead of emoji spans, and a documented offline stance.

9. **Customer portal app shell (`PortalPage.jsx`, 16,168 lines).** Every tab re-declares its own card/button/label styles (13 card objects, 5 button styles, 52 boxed section labels), ten hand-rolled dialog shells, two switch primitives, two icon-tile primitives, and inline radii/type that the glass sheet overrides anyway. The Visits tab prints invented per-visit treatment descriptions (CPA-001). **Redesign:** fold the local styles into `BrandCard`/`BrandButton`/`ShellIconTile`/`GoldSwitch`, one dialog shell, and real visit copy from the schedule payload (or none).

10. **Customer report V2 folders (`components/report/*V2`).** Six folders each ship a local r16 `card`, a 14/700 `eyebrow`, and copy-pasted `StatusPill`/`ScoreRing`/`TrendChip` (lawn and tree are near-identical files); the hero and re-entry card still render status pills after the 2026-09-04 no-pills ruling; lawn and tree dashboards nest inside the visit-summary card and drop to a ~160px text measure at 320px. **Redesign:** one `reportStyles.js`, one shared pill/ring module, edge-to-edge V2 sections, and the owner's per-pill ruling.

11. **Intelligence Bar chrome.** Four implementations (⌘K palette, `IntelligenceBarShell`, AgentEstimatePage's direct hook use, tech bar) with three markdown renderers and two recents stores; the primary palette is Tier 2 inline with its own palette and accent colours inside the monochrome admin, and no IB surface has a live region. **Redesign:** `IntelligenceBarShell` as the single admin chrome (owner ruling), one renderer, one recents key, `role=status` on loading and answers.

12. **Admin shell (`AdminLayoutV2.jsx`).** Structurally sound, but it owns no content cap (pages cap themselves eight ways), has no `<main>` landmark or skip link, its mobile drawer has no focus trap, the shared bell panel hardcodes 56px offsets, and unknown `/admin/*` URLs fall through to the customer portal. **Redesign (small):** shell-owned `max-w` with a `wide` opt-out, landmark + skip link, drawer on `useModalFocus`, wildcard routes.

---

## D. Functional bug report (verified)

Every entry survived the refute-by-default pass. "Fix" is the verifier's sketch where one exists. Verifier evidence is reproduced in full.

### F0053 · P1 · DATE columns (service/scheduled/payment dates) render one day early for ET viewers

- **Location:** `client/src/components/admin/Customer360ProfileV2.jsx:150` (reader customer-360:C360-03)
- **Reproduction:** Open any customer with a visit dated today in an ET browser; the Services tab shows yesterday's date.
- **Expected:** A visit stored as 2026-09-05 shows 'Sep 5, 2026' (canonical helper: formatETDateOnly in client/src/lib/timezone.js).
- **Actual:** pg serialises DATE columns as '2026-09-05T00:00:00.000Z' on the TZ=UTC Railway box; the regex branch misses the 'T', and new Date(...).toLocaleDateString() in America/New_York yields 'Sep 4, 2026'. Affects Upcoming Appointments, Recent Services, Service History, Scheduled Services, Recent Transactions, Payment History and the timeline (via timeAgo). The 360 has zero imports from lib/timezone.js (rule 15 canonical path).
- **Likely cause:** Local fmtDate written for 'YYYY-MM-DD' strings; the payload actually carries midnight-UTC ISO.
- **Fix:** In Customer360ProfileV2.jsx import `formatETDateOnly` from "../../lib/timezone" and use it (instead of fmtDate) at the DATE-typed call sites: :2185, :6522 (service_date), :6495, :6881 (scheduled_date), :5053, :6654, :7046 (payment_date), :7858 (application_date). Leave fmtDate for timestamp columns (created_at, signedAt, etc.).
- **Test:** Unit (TZ=America/New_York): formatETDateOnly('2026-09-05T00:00:00.000Z') === 'Sep 5, 2026'; RTL: render the Services tab with a service_record whose service_date is '2026-09-05T00:00:00.000Z' and assert 'Sep 5, 2026' is shown.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — Customer360ProfileV2.jsx:150-162 `fmtDate` only treats a bare `^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$` as date-only; anything else goes through `new Date(d).toLocaleDateString("en-US", {...})` with no timeZone. The GET /:id payload (server/routes/admin-customers.js:3078) returns raw `service_records.*` (:3140-3145), `payments.*` (:3147) and scheduled_services rows (customerScheduledHistoryQuery :152-159; upcoming :3150-3156) unmapped at :3341 (`interactions, preferences: prefs, services, estimates, payments, scheduled, upcomingScheduled`). No `setTypeParser` exists anywhere under server/, and pg-types registers OID 1082 → postgres-date (node_modules/pg-types/lib/textParsers.js:174), which builds `new Date(year, month, day)` in process-local time (node_modules/postgres-date/index.js:75); nothing in the server sets process.env.TZ, so on the UTC container a DATE serialises as `2026-09-05T00:00:00.000Z`, the regex misses the `T`, and an ET browser prints Sep 4. Affected DATE-column call sites: :2185 and :6522 (`s.service_date`), :6495 and :6881 (`s.scheduled_date`), :5053 / :6654 / :7046 (`payment_date`), :7858 (`application_date`). The file itself documents the exact shift for servicePausedAt at :6544-6546, client/src/pages/admin/PayerDetailSheet.jsx:31-41 already handles 'a midnight-UTC ISO' with timeZone:'UTC', and client/src/lib/timezone.js:87-92 `formatETDateOnly` is the canonical helper (the 360 has zero imports from lib/timezone).

### F0063 · P1 · Clicking any sub-modal backdrop bubbles to the overlay and closes the whole profile (mid-refund unmount)

- **Location:** `client/src/components/admin/Customer360ProfileV2.jsx:5907` (reader customer-360:C360-02)
- **Reproduction:** Open a customer → Edit → click the dim area outside the form. Profile closes and edits are lost.
- **Expected:** Clicking outside a sub-modal dismisses only that modal; while a refund/charge is in flight nothing closes.
- **Actual:** React synthetic events bubble through portals to the overlay's onClick={onClose}, so a backdrop click on Edit customer / Record prepay / Send prepay invoice / Cancel signup / Refund closes the entire 360. During a running refund the modal's own guard (!running) keeps it open, but the overlay still closes and unmounts the modal mid-request — the exact outcome the Escape-swallow at 5060-5069 was written to prevent.
- **Likely cause:** Sub-modals were added inside the overlay's React subtree without e.stopPropagation() on their backdrop handlers.
- **Fix:** Customer360ProfileV2.jsx:5907 — replace `onClick={onClose}` on the root overlay with `onClick={(e) => { if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose(); }}` so only a click on the overlay's own dim area closes the profile (covers all six sub-modals in one line).
- **Test:** RTL: open the profile, click Edit, fireEvent.click on the edit modal's backdrop — assert the profile onClose was not called and setEditOpen(false) ran; also click the profile overlay itself → onClose called.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — A @babel/parser walk of the file shows `<AnnualPrepayModal>` :8193, `<AnnualPrepayInvoiceModal>` :8204, `<CancelSignupModal>` :8214, `<CancelPlanDialog>` :8221, `<RefundPaymentModal>` :8228 and the inline edit modal `<div>` :8236 each have exactly ONE JSX ancestor: the root overlay div at :5902-5907 (`className="admin-shell-v2 fixed inset-0 bg-black/70 z-[1000] flex justify-end font-sans" onClick={onClose}`) — they are siblings of, not children of, the panel div :5910-5913 that does `onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}`. The sub-modal backdrop handlers never stop propagation: :4070 `onClick={() => !saving && onClose?.()}`, :4495 same, :4745 `onClick={() => !running && onClose()}`, :5008 same, :8238 `onClick={() => !savingEdit && setEditOpen(false)}`; ui/Dialog.jsx:88-91 backdrop is `onClick={onClose}` with no stopPropagation either, so CancelPlanDialog bubbles too. React synthetic events bubble through portals, so every backdrop click reaches the profile's onClose; during a running refund the `!running` guard keeps the modal's own state but the parent unmounts it — the exact outcome the :4938-4941 Escape swallow was written to prevent.

### F0074 · P1 · CancelPlanDialog (ui/Dialog z-120) is portaled beneath the Customer 360 overlay (z-1000)

- **Location:** `client/src/components/admin/Customer360ProfileV2.jsx:8221` (reader overlays:OV-01)
- **Reproduction:** Open /admin/customers -> a customer (Customer 360 panel) -> Actions -> Cancel plan. Observe whether the dialog is visible/clickable above the dark scrim.
- **Expected:** Cancel plan dialog paints above the Customer 360 panel and its bg-black/70 scrim, like the sibling Refund / Cancel-signup / Edit-customer modals that were raised to z-[1100]/z-[1120].
- **Actual:** Both roots are direct children of document.body with explicit z-index; z-[120] loses to z-[1000], so the dialog sits under the opaque scrim and panel. ui/Dialog has no layer prop (Sheet does) and CancelPlanDialog passes no className/style override. Static analysis only - not runtime verified.
- **Likely cause:** Dialog.jsx z ladder comment (lines 70-74) only accounts for shell chrome up to 110; Customer 360 predates it at z-[1000] and its child modals were hand-bumped instead of the primitive gaining a layer prop.
- **Fix:** client/src/components/admin/CancelPlanDialog.jsx:203 — `<Dialog open onClose={() => !running && onClose()} size="md" style={{ zIndex: 1100 }}>` with a one-line comment: hosted inside the Customer 360 overlay (z-[1000]); matches the sibling C360 modals at z-[1100].
- **Test:** client/src/components/admin/CancelPlanDialog.test.jsx: render the dialog (existing fetch mocks), then `expect(document.querySelector('[role="dialog"]').style.zIndex).toBe('1100')`; optionally Dialog.test.jsx asserting `<Dialog open style={{zIndex: 1100}}>` lands zIndex on the portal root.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — Proven by CSS stacking, no runtime needed. client/src/components/admin/Customer360ProfileV2.jsx:5901-5906 `return createPortal(<div … className="admin-shell-v2 fixed inset-0 bg-black/70 z-[1000] flex justify-end font-sans" onClick={onClose}>` … :8473 `, document.body);`. CancelPlanDialog is rendered inside that tree at :8220-8225 `{cancelPlanOpen && (<CancelPlanDialog customer={c} onClose={() => setCancelPlanOpen(false)} onDone={reloadCustomer} />)}` (opened from :7127 `onClick={() => setCancelPlanOpen(true)}`); client/src/components/admin/CancelPlanDialog.jsx:203 `<Dialog open onClose={() => !running && onClose()} size="md">` passes no style; client/src/components/ui/Dialog.jsx:67 `return createPortal(` … :75 `'fixed inset-0 z-[120] flex items-center justify-center p-4'` … :112 `document.body`. Two sibling body-level fixed roots with explicit z-index: 120 loses to 1000 regardless of portal order, so the dialog paints under the 70% scrim and (on phones, the 100%-width `.c360-panel`) fully under the panel, and clicks hit the C360 scrim's `onClick={onClose}`. C360 is the dialog's only host (grep: CancelPlanDialog.jsx, its test, Customer360ProfileV2.jsx). The file's own precedent hand-bumps sibling modals: :4069/:4494 `z-[1120]`, :4744/:5007/:8237 `z-[1100]`. z-[1000] dates from ceaf3f108 (2026-04-18); the dialog from d8900e370 (#3666, 2026-09-01), whose ui-verify ran in a standalone dev harness (preview-cancel-dialog.html, stubbed fetch — per the #3666 lane notes), never inside the 360, which is how it slipped. Correction to the proposed fix: no `layer` prop is needed — Dialog.jsx:84-90 `style={{ paddingTop: …, ...style }}` spreads `style` onto the z-[120] root (`className` at :104 goes to the inner panel, so it cannot help); precedent client/src/components/admin/EstimateModalsV2.jsx:64 `<Dialog open onClose={onClose} size="md" style={ROBOTO_STYLE}>`. One local line fixes it; the 'bring C360 down to the 120 ladder' follow-up is a separate design decision, not required. P1 stands: the action is unusable from its only host (the IB cancel_plan tool is an alternate path, not a substitute).

### F0195 · P1 · MobileServiceEditModal rewrites the visit's service_type on every save, appending '— Billed Monthly' to any type without a cadence keyword

- **Location:** `client/src/components/schedule/MobileServiceEditModal.jsx:101` (reader schedule-mobile-sheets:SMS-01)
- **Reproduction:** Dispatch mobile -> appointment -> Review & checkout -> tap the base service line -> change only Price -> Save -> reload: service_type now ends with '— Billed Monthly' (or has its cadence prefix moved after an em dash).
- **Expected:** Editing price, staff, duration or notes leaves service_type untouched unless the tech explicitly changed the cadence; a service with no cadence keyword ('Lawn Care - Track B', 'Rodent Bait Station') is never relabeled.
- **Actual:** detectTier defaults to 'monthly' and handleSave always rebuilds the string, so a price-only save on 'Lawn Care - Track B' persists 'Lawn Care - Track B — Billed Monthly' (em dash + cadence label); 'Quarterly Pest Control' becomes 'Pest Control — Quarterly'. Downstream readers (serviceColor, isLawn regex, labels, cadence inference) see a rewritten string.
- **Likely cause:** legacy POS-style 'choose one tier' list assumed every service type carries a cadence; fallback tier is treated as a selection.
- **Fix:** MobileServiceEditModal.jsx: `const initialTier = useRef(detectTier(service?.serviceType));` then in handleSave `const tierChanged = tier !== initialTier.current;` and build the body with `...(tierChanged && tierOpt ? { serviceType: `${baseName} — ${tierOpt.label}` } : {})` — omit `serviceType` entirely when the tech did not pick a different tier so the server keeps the stored value.
- **Test:** RTL: render with service.serviceType='Lawn Care - Track B', edit Price, Save → the PUT body has no `serviceType` key; pick 'Quarterly' then Save → body.serviceType === 'Lawn Care - Track B — Quarterly'.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — MobileServiceEditModal.jsx:41-47 `function detectTier(serviceType) { if (!serviceType) return 'monthly'; for (const opt of TIER_OPTIONS) { if (opt.pattern.test(serviceType)) return opt.key; } return 'monthly'; }` — the fallback is indistinguishable from a selection; 96-101 `const tierOpt = TIER_OPTIONS.find((o) => o.key === tier); const nextServiceType = tierOpt ? `${baseName} — ${tierOpt.label}` : service.serviceType;` and 107 `serviceType: nextServiceType,` are sent on EVERY save regardless of what changed; 49-56 `baseServiceName` strips the cadence word, so 'Quarterly Pest Control' → 'Pest Control — Quarterly' and 'Lawn Care - Track B' → 'Lawn Care - Track B — Billed Monthly'. Server writes it verbatim: server/routes/admin-schedule.js:7754 `if (serviceType !== undefined) updates.service_type = serviceType;` (the comment at 7755-7765 confirms a label-only post with no serviceId is treated as a plain service_type write). Caller DispatchPageV2.jsx:2235-2240 passes the raw row and refetches, so the rewritten label is what every downstream reader sees. No test file exists for the modal.

### F0288 · P1 · Visits tab prints invented per-visit treatment descriptions

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/PortalPage.jsx:3960` (reader customer-portal-app-a:CPA-001)
- **Reproduction:** Log in as any customer with ≥1 upcoming visit → Visits → Upcoming: read the 'Visit #N — …' line on the featured/compact card.
- **Expected:** Upcoming-visit cards only show what the schedule payload says will be done (or nothing), per the truth rule the same tab states at 4881-4883 ('never invent a next-treatment month or mosquito restart').
- **Actual:** Every upcoming card shows 'Visit #N — <hardcoded sentence>' chosen by visitNumber modulo a static list (3930-3958), e.g. a lawn visit #3 always reads 'Summer fertilizer + chinch bug preventive' regardless of season, plan, or the work order — a fabricated promise to the customer.
- **Likely cause:** Legacy placeholder copy from before the schedule payload carried real service data; never removed when the truth-copy pass hit the empty state.
- **Fix:** Drop SCHEDULE_TREATMENT_DESCRIPTIONS/getScheduleVisitDescription and render a server-supplied description field only when present (or the service type alone). Owner to confirm whether the visit counter '#N' should stay.
- **Test:** Render ScheduleTab with a getSchedule payload row (serviceType 'Lawn Care', no description) and assert `queryByText(/fertilizer\|chinch\|pre-emergent/)` is null. The truthful `Visit #N` counter is an owner decision (see the Fix) and is not asserted either way.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — client/src/pages/PortalPage.jsx:3930-3958 is a static table (`'Lawn': ['Spring green-up fertilizer + pre-emergent weed control', ..., 'Summer fertilizer + chinch bug preventive', ...]`) and 3960-3966 picks by index: `return descs[(num - 1) % descs.length];`. PortalPage.jsx:4562-4564 applies it unconditionally, overwriting any payload field: `const visitNum = s.visitNumber \|\| (idx + 1); const description = getScheduleVisitDescription(s.serviceType, visitNum); return { ...s, ..., visitNum, description };`. Rendered at 4686 and 4769 `Visit #{s.visitNum} — {s.description}`. The API cannot supply either value: server/routes/schedule.js GET '/' (line 94) maps `serviceType: normalizeServiceType(s.service_type)` (207) and the file has no `visitNumber` or `description` field in that payload, so '#N' is the list index and the sentence is invented. The same tab's truth rule at 4881-4883 says "the schedule API is the only source of truth; never invent a next-treatment month", and client/src/pages/PortalPage.truth-copy.test.jsx:59 only covers the empty state. What replaces the line (service type only, nothing, keep the counter) is a content decision.
- **Needs owner decision**

### F0348 · P1 · 'Charge card on file' from Invoices collects an off-session charge with one tap, no amount and no confirmation

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/AdminInvoicesPage.jsx:1871` (reader billing-admin:BA-03)
- **Reproduction:** Admin (role admin) → Invoices → expand an open invoice for a customer with a saved card → Charge card on file → tap Charge: the card is charged immediately.
- **Expected:** Money movement follows the surface's own pattern: quote the exact amount (incl. funding-aware surcharge / credit) → confirm → charge with `expectedTotal`, as CreateProjectModal.jsx:1702-1722 already does against the same two endpoints.
- **Actual:** The sheet lists saved cards with a bare 'Charge' pill; nothing on screen states the amount that will be collected, there is no confirm step, and the request omits `expectedTotal`, so the server's amount guard (admin-invoices.js:1570 `const { paymentMethodId, expectedTotal } = req.body`) is not exercised from this path. The Record-payment, Apply-credit and Send flows on the same page all go through a modal with the amount shown.
- **Likely cause:** MobileCardOnFileSheet was built for the tech checkout sheet and reused on desktop via `desktopVisible` without the quote/confirm layer the tech project modal later gained.
- **Fix:** In MobileCardOnFileSheet (or a wrapper used by the invoice page), call POST /admin/invoices/:id/charge-card-quote for the selected card, show base / card fee / total, require a confirm, and pass `expectedTotal: quote.total` to charge-card. Keep the existing orphan/ambiguous 'Do not retry' handling.
- **Test:** MobileCardOnFileSheet test: Charge stays disabled until the charge-card-quote resolves and confirm is accepted; the charge-card request body contains expectedTotal equal to quote.total.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — AdminInvoicesPage.jsx:1871-1878 `{isAdminUser && canCollect && cardOnFile && !inv.payer_id && (<button onClick={() => setCardOnFileInvoice(inv)} …>Charge card on file</button>)}` -> :2241-2250 `<MobileCardOnFileSheet desktopVisible invoiceId={…} customerId={…} customerName={…} onClose onChargeSuccess>`. MobileCardOnFileSheet.jsx:72-79 POSTs `/admin/invoices/${invoiceId}/charge-card` with `body: JSON.stringify({ paymentMethodId: card.id })` only; :173-190 is a bare 'Charge' button per card; nothing in :101-200 renders an amount and there is no confirm. Server: admin-invoices.js:1570 `const { paymentMethodId, expectedTotal } = req.body \|\| {}` -> stripe.js:2332 and :2379 apply the exact-amount guard only `if (expectedTotal != null …)`, so this path skips it. CreateProjectModal.jsx:1702-1722 already does quote (`/charge-card-quote`) -> `confirm(pricingDetail)` -> charge with `expectedTotal: quote.total` against the same endpoints. The fix is a money-movement flow change on a component shared with the tech checkout sheet (where the quote/confirm lives, and whether the tech path gets the same step) — an owner/billing ruling, not an autofix.
- **Needs owner decision**

### F0467 · P1 · Mobile estimates list has no Follow Up, Mark lost, Schedule or proposal actions that desktop rows carry

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/EstimatesPageV2.jsx:3365` (reader pipeline-estimator:PE-01)
- **Reproduction:** Open /admin/pipeline?tab=estimates at <768px width, tap ⋯ on a sent/viewed estimate: no Mark lost / Follow up / Schedule entries.
- **Expected:** The mobile list (the view Adam gets on his phone under 768px, per useIsMobile(768) at 3961) exposes the same status transitions as desktop: Follow Up (POST /follow-up, increments followUpCount), Mark lost (DeclineModalV2 with normalized disposition), Schedule (CreateAppointmentModal with defaultEstimateId), Commercial proposal, Preview customer view, 1x-option/Invoice toggles.
- **Actual:** EstimatesMobileListView never imports FollowUpModalV2, DeclineModalV2 or CreateAppointmentModal (grep of lines 3490-3958 finds no setFollowUpTarget/setDeclineTarget/scheduleEstimate). On a phone an estimate cannot be marked lost, followed up via the tracked endpoint, scheduled from the row, or previewed; the operator must find a desktop.
- **Likely cause:** Mobile list was built as a 'touch-optimized counterpart' (comment at 3494) and never caught up as desktop row actions grew.
- **Fix:** Add followUpTarget/declineTarget/scheduleEstimate state to EstimatesMobileListView, render FollowUpModalV2 / DeclineModalV2 / CreateAppointmentModal alongside the existing ExtendEstimateModalV2, and add the 'Follow up', 'Mark lost', 'Schedule', 'Commercial proposal', 'Preview customer view' items (same status guards as desktop) to the mobile RowActionsMenu. Better: derive both rows' item lists from one buildRowActions(estimate, handlers) helper so the two views cannot drift again.
- **Test:** RTL: render EstimatesMobileListView with a status:'viewed' estimate, open the ⋯ menu, assert 'Mark lost' and 'Follow up' items exist and open DeclineModalV2 / FollowUpModalV2.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — Desktop pipeline view owns the transitions: state at 1700-1707 (`followUpTarget`, `declineTarget`, `scheduleEstimate`), modals at 2016 `<FollowUpModalV2`, 2026 `<DeclineModalV2`, 2068 `<CreateAppointmentModal`, row actions 2503 `onClick={() => setFollowUpTarget(e)}` 'Follow Up', 2519/2554 `setScheduleEstimate(e)`, 2719 ``navigate(`/admin/estimates/${e.id}/proposal`)``, 2735 'Preview customer view', 2749-2751 'Mark lost' → `setDeclineTarget(e)`. The mobile branch is the ONLY estimates view under 768px (EstimatesPageV2.jsx:3961 `useIsMobile(768)`, 4132-4135 `isMobile ? <EstimatesMobileListView …> : <EstimatePipelineViewV2 …>`), and EstimatesMobileListView (3499-3959) mounts only `<ExtendEstimateModalV2` (3940) — no FollowUpModalV2/DeclineModalV2/CreateAppointmentModal reference exists after line 3086. The MobileEstimateRow menu keys (3369-3489) are edit, send, send-booking, extend, mark-won, annual-prepay, copy-link, lawn-outline, audit, engine-review, new-estimate, archive, unarchive, delete — no follow-up, mark-lost, schedule, proposal or preview. Tapping the row only opens the customer panel (3116-3118 `openPanel` → `onOpenCustomerPanel`), and CustomerEstimatesPanel.jsx has no Mark lost / Follow up either (grep empty). 'Message contact' (3353) is a partial substitute for follow-up, but 'Mark lost' has no phone path at all. P1 stands for the sole operator's phone surface. The fix shape (full parity via a shared action builder vs. a curated mobile subset) is a design ruling the code cannot settle.
- **Needs owner decision**

### F0532 · P1 · RecapCapture renders white text on a white/light card (D.white copied from the dark tech palette)

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/SchedulePage.jsx:10213` (reader tokens-style:TS-01)
- **Reproduction:** Admin → Schedule → open a recap-eligible pest visit closeout (recapEligible) → 'Recap clips' → tap '+ Capture clip' → the bottom sheet title is blank; after a clip is added the role label row is blank.
- **Expected:** Clip role label and the 'What were you doing?' sheet title are readable on the light D palette (D.heading / D.text).
- **Actual:** #FFFFFF text on #FFFFFF (sheet) and on #F1F5F9 (clip row) — invisible. Same pattern at L19949 (`<span style={{ color: D.white }}>` for the re-entry stepper value inside CompletionPanel) and the block also authors Montserrat + fractional sizes 12.5/11.5/13.5 inside the Roboto-forced admin shell.
- **Likely cause:** RecapCapture was ported from the dark tech D palette (pages/TechCapturePreview.jsx uses the identical strings with D.white on #0f1923); SchedulePage's D was later flipped to a light palette without re-checking `color: D.white` call sites.
- **Fix:** SchedulePage.jsx:10194 `color: D.white` → `color: D.heading`; :10213 `color: D.white` → `color: D.heading` (optionally drop the inert `fontFamily: "'Montserrat', sans-serif"`); :19949 `<span style={{ color: D.white }}>` → `<span style={{ color: D.heading, fontWeight: 500 }}>`. Leave the other D.white uses (:4778, :4916, :5030, :5556, :18669 sit on dark/active backgrounds; :8303-8748 use it as a background).
- **Test:** RTL: render RecapCapture with one clip item and a pending file, assert the 'What were you doing?' element and the role label do not have inline color #FFFFFF (or that computed color differs from the parent background); regression grep: no `color: D.white` in SchedulePage.jsx lines 10127-10290.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — client/src/pages/admin/SchedulePage.jsx:110-127 defines the light palette: `bg: "#F1F5F9", card: "#FFFFFF", … white: "#FFFFFF", heading: "#0F172A"`. RecapCapture (:10127): :10191 row `background: D.bg` → :10194 `<div style={{ fontSize: 12.5, fontWeight: 500, color: D.white, textTransform: "capitalize" }}>{m.role}</div>` (white on #F1F5F9); :10211 sheet `background: D.card` → :10213 `<div style={{ fontFamily: "'Montserrat', sans-serif", fontWeight: 500, fontSize: 16, color: D.white, textAlign: "center" }}>What were you doing?</div>` (white on #FFFFFF). CompletionPanel :19947-19951 `<span style={{ fontSize: 14, color: D.text, flex: 1 }}>{row.label}: <span style={{ color: D.white }}>{formatReentryStepperMinutes(row.value)}</span></span>` with only light ancestors (nearest backgrounds :19723 `D.card`, :19757 `#FFF7ED`). Reachable: :15931-15933 `{recapEligible && (<div …><RecapCapture serviceId={service.id} />` with recapEligible at :11465-11469 (pest closeouts with the recap flag). Invisible sheet title, role labels and re-entry values in a live closeout flow — P1 holds. Montserrat is inert under index.css:187-191 (secondary).

### F0544 · P1 · Tax A/R 'Send Reminder' posts to a non-existent endpoint — every click fails

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/TaxPage.jsx:3776` (reader billing-admin:BA-01)
- **Reproduction:** Admin → Taxes → Exports & A/R → A/R → any overdue row with a phone → Send Reminder → confirm → 'Failed to send: HTTP 404'.
- **Expected:** The A/R row's reminder action either reaches a mounted SMS route or defers to the invoice follow-up sequence (POST /admin/invoices/:id/followup/send-now) which already owns stopOnPayment, quiet hours and audit.
- **Actual:** server/index.js mounts only '/api/admin/sms-templates'; grep of server/ finds no '/sms/send' handler (admin-communications exposes POST /sms under its own mount; admin-billing-health mounted at /api/admin has no sms route). The request 404s and the operator sees `alert("Failed to send: ...")` on every attempt; the confirm dialog promises a text that never goes out.
- **Likely cause:** Button was written against an SMS route that was later consolidated into admin-communications; the Tax page was never re-pointed.
- **Fix:** Remove the dead button or replace it with a link to the invoice row (`/admin/invoices?invoice=<id>`) where the follow-up panel's 'Send Next Now' already sends the reminder through the sequence. Do not wire it to a raw SMS send — reminders belong to the follow-up lane (owner decision).
- **Test:** Owner ruling needed: remove the A/R 'Send Reminder' control, or replace it with a Link to `/admin/invoices?invoice=<id>` (follow-up panel owns quiet hours/stop-on-payment/audit). Never re-point it at a raw SMS send (rule 12). After the ruling: RTL asserts the A/R row renders no control that posts to /admin/sms/send; optionally a server contract test that every `adminFetch("/admin/…")` literal in client/src resolves to a mounted route.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — client/src/pages/admin/TaxPage.jsx:3776-3783 `await adminFetch("/admin/sms/send", { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify({ to: inv.phone, message: \`Hi ${inv.customerName}, this is Waves Pest Control. You have an outstanding balance …\` }) }); alert("Reminder sent!");` after a `window.confirm` at 3767-3771. adminFetch (43-45) resolves to `${API_BASE}${path}` with API_BASE `/api` (line 21) → `/api/admin/sms/send`. server/index.js mounts only `app.use('/api/admin', require('./routes/admin-billing-health'))` (line 808 — that router has no sms route) and `/api/admin/sms-templates` (793); the only `router.post('/sms'…)` handlers are admin-communications.js:253 (mounted at /api/admin/communications, index.js:611), admin-import-sheets.js:33, twilio-webhook.js:143 — none serve /api/admin/sms/send. The path is referenced nowhere else in client/src. Result: 404 → adminFetch throws `HTTP 404` (56-59) → `alert("Failed to send: HTTP 404")` every time; no text goes out (so no comms leak today, but the confirm dialog promises one). The follow-up lane the finding points at exists: server/routes/admin-invoices.js:2934 `router.post('/:id/followup/send-now', requireAdmin, …)` and AdminInvoicesPage.jsx:7278-7282 'Send Next Now'.
- **Needs owner decision**

### F0547 · P1 · A/R 'Total Outstanding' renders white text on a white card when total ≤ $500

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/TaxPage.jsx:3855` (reader billing-admin:BA-02)
- **Reproduction:** Admin → Taxes → A/R with total outstanding ≤ $500 → first stat card shows no number.
- **Expected:** The headline receivable is always legible; only the > $500 case is emphasized red.
- **Actual:** Below the threshold the value is painted #FFFFFF on a #FFFFFF card (StatCard background D.card), so the number is invisible. Leftover from the dark-palette era where D.white on a dark card made sense.
- **Likely cause:** V2 token fold changed the card background to white but left the D.white value color.
- **Fix:** TaxPage.jsx:3855 → `color={s.total > 500 ? D.red : D.heading}` (or `color={s.total > 500 ? D.red : undefined}` so the StatCard default applies).
- **Test:** RTL: render AccountsReceivableTab with /admin/tax/accounts-receivable mocked to summary.total = 100; find the 'Total Outstanding' value node and assert its inline color !== '#FFFFFF' / rgb(255, 255, 255); with total = 600 assert it is D.red.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — client/src/pages/admin/TaxPage.jsx:3853-3858 `<StatCard label="Total Outstanding" value={fmtM(s.total)} color={s.total > 500 ? D.red : D.white} sub={…} />`. Palette at 23-40: `card: "#FFFFFF"` (line 25) and `white: "#FFFFFF"` (line 35). StatCard (87-133) paints `background: D.card` (line 92) and the value at 118-127 `style={{ fontFamily: MONO, fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 700, color: color \|\| D.heading }}` — so for total ≤ $500 the number is #FFFFFF on #FFFFFF. The file comment at line 22 ('V2 token pass: teal/blue/purple/orange fold to zinc-900') confirms the dark→light fold that left this behind; it is the only `color={…D.white…}` on a StatCard in the file.

### F0008 · P2 · /admin index redirect pushes a history entry → Back is trapped after every login / PWA launch

- **Location:** `client/src/App.jsx:563` (reader admin-shell-nav:ASN-01)
- **Merged:** F0009 (navigation-map:NAV-01) reported the same line and defect; its verifier labelled it a duplicate, so it is folded into this entry and counted once.
- **Reproduction:** Log in at /admin/login (or open the installed PWA), land on /admin/dashboard, press browser Back — you are returned to the dashboard.
- **Expected:** The index redirect replaces the /admin entry (react-router v6 <Navigate> pushes unless replace is set), so Back from /admin/dashboard leaves the admin or returns to the previous page.
- **Actual:** AdminLoginPage navigates to '/admin' (replace) and admin-manifest.json start_url is '/admin/', so the first history entry is /admin; the index route then PUSHES /admin/dashboard. Pressing Back lands on /admin, which pushes /admin/dashboard again — a back-button loop. Technicians take a further /admin/schedule → /admin/dispatch hop on top.
- **Likely cause:** Missing `replace` on the index <Navigate>; every other admin redirect in the block passes replace.
- **Fix:** client/src/App.jsx:563 — replace `<Route index element={<Navigate to="dashboard" />} />` with `<Route index element={<Navigate to="dashboard" replace />} />`.
- **Test:** client/src/components/admin/AdminAliasRoutes.test.jsx already has a Probe with Back/Forward buttons and a MemoryRouter harness: render with initialEntries ['/x', '/admin'] and the index Navigate, expect location /admin/dashboard, click Back, expect /x (not /admin/dashboard).
- **Verifier:** confirmed — App.jsx:563 `<Route index element={<Navigate to="dashboard" />} />` has no replace while every sibling redirect does (:581 revenue, :584 ads, :663/:664, :668 all pass `replace`). BrowserRouter (App.jsx:501) is a non-data router, and react-router 6.30.4 (client/package.json:47; node_modules/react-router/package.json) implements Navigate as `React.useEffect(() => navigate(JSON.parse(jsonPath), { replace, ... }))` (node_modules/react-router/dist/index.js:1147-1151) with useNavigateUnstable doing `(!!options.replace ? navigator.replace : navigator.push)(path, ...)` (:241) — undefined replace pushes. Entry points land on /admin with replace: AdminLoginPage.jsx:85-88 `destination = ... '/admin'; navigate(destination, { replace: true })`, MorePage.jsx:44 `<Navigate to="/admin" replace />`, admin-manifest.json start_url '/admin/'. So history becomes [/admin, /admin/dashboard]; Back returns to /admin, whose index route pushes /admin/dashboard again — the loop is real. isPathAdminOnly('/admin') is false (adminNavigation.js:416) so the layout's role guard does not interfere; technicians then hop via replace (AdminLayoutV2.jsx:169, App.jsx:155).

### F0055 · P2 · 'Charge now' is offered to every lane; the server refuses non-monthly customers after the confirm dialog

- **Location:** `client/src/components/admin/Customer360ProfileV2.jsx:3372` (reader customer-360:C360-13)
- **Reproduction:** Open a per-visit customer → Billing → Charge now → confirm → error.
- **Expected:** Per the contract ('Disabled actions with a reason'), the button is hidden/disabled with the lane reason unless billingMode is monthly_membership — c.billingMode is already in the same payload and shown in the panel directly above.
- **Actual:** Per-visit / prepay / one-time customers get a 'Charge now ($X)' button and a money confirm, then a 400.
- **Likely cause:** canCharge = isAdmin only.
- **Fix:** Expose the server-resolved lane in the GET /:id payload (`billingLane: resolveBillingLane(customer)`, the same helper the charge route uses) and gate the action on `data.billingLane?.mode === 'monthly_membership'` — not on raw `c.billingMode`, which is null for customers whose tier + monthly rate still resolve to monthly_membership; otherwise disable the button with the lane reason as its title.
- **Test:** Server: add `billingLane: resolveBillingLane(c)` (mode + source) to the GET /:id payload; client: `canCharge={isAdmin && data.billingLane?.mode === 'monthly_membership'}` (or disabled + title with the lane reason). RTL: billing_mode null + tier Silver + rate 89 → button present; billing_mode 'per_visit' → absent.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — Customer360ProfileV2.jsx:6957-6962 mounts `<AdminAutopayPanelV2 … canCharge={isAdmin} />`; :3372-3378 renders 'Charge now ($X)' whenever canCharge; chargeNow :3301-3316 runs `window.confirm(...)` then POSTs `{}` (never an amount). server/routes/admin-billing-health.js:104-112: `if (amount == null) { const lane = resolveBillingLane(customer); if (lane.mode !== 'monthly_membership') return res.status(400)...` — so every non-monthly customer gets the money confirm then a 400. The READER'S FIX IS WRONG though: server/services/billing-lane.js:74-79 infers `monthly_membership` for a NULL billing_mode + real tier + monthly_rate > 0 (`source: 'inferred'`), and the 360 payload exposes only the raw column (`billingMode: c.billing_mode \|\| null`, admin-customers.js:3299) with no resolved-lane field, so gating on `c.billingMode === 'monthly_membership'` would hide a working action for every inferred member and duplicate the canonical resolver client-side (rule 15).

[{Payment:80},{Engagement:60},{Service:70},{Satisfaction:75},{Tenure:90},{Revenue:65}] : [score+10, score, score+5, score-5, tenure/3.65, lifetimeRevenue/50]`, with `hs = data.healthScore \|\| {}` (:5598) and `score = hs.overall_score ?? hs.health_score ?? hs.score` (:5599); rendered under `<SectionTitle>Health Radar</SectionTitle><RadarChart data={radarData} />` at :6768-6769. The row comes from customer_health_scores (admin-customers.js:1012-1030) whose real axes are payment_score/service_score/engagement_score/satisfaction_score/loyalty_score/growth_score (server/models/migrations/20260401000093_customer_health_scoring.js:8-13); `risk_factors` is the legacy 037-shape column (20260401000037:22, copied into churn_signals by 20260611000001:116-120), so the widget never plots a stored sub-score. Downgraded from P1: nothing operational keys off the chart, the header (:5-6, :21) says it was ported 1:1 from V1 and only recolored, and the real per-axis radar already exists at client/src/pages/admin/CustomerHealthTabs.jsx:208-219. The fix needs a ruling — remap six axes (Tenure/Revenue ≠ loyalty/growth) or drop the widget.
- **Needs owner decision**

### F0070 · P2 · Comms thread opens scrolled to the oldest message inside a 400px box

- **Location:** `client/src/components/admin/Customer360ProfileV2.jsx:7153` (reader customer-360:C360-20)

Note: becomes moot if F0071's panel adoption is chosen — the inline thread would be deleted.
