The shared panel does anchor: client/src/components/admin/customer360/CustomerSmsPanel.jsx:180 `if (el) el.scrollTop = el.scrollHeight;` and :230-232 `if (!hadAny \|\| wasNearBottom) { requestAnimationFrame(scrollToBottom); }`. The claim holds exactly as written; P2 is right for a daily-driver read surface.

### F0076 · P2 · Decline / Follow-up / Extend dialogs swallow the server's error message ('Failed: HTTP 409')

- **Location:** `client/src/components/admin/EstimateModalsV2.jsx:28` (reader pipeline-estimator:PE-06)
- **Reproduction:** Mark as Lost on an estimate that is mid-clarify hold → alert shows 'Failed: HTTP 409'.
- **Expected:** The operator sees the server's reason (the shared client/src/utils/admin-fetch.js:86-105 already extracts body.error, code and details).
- **Actual:** Every non-2xx from Mark as Lost, Follow Up and Extend surfaces as 'Failed: HTTP 400/409' with no explanation, even though the file's own comment (line 111) acknowledges the server 400s a blank Other note.
- **Likely cause:** Copy-pasted minimal fetch wrapper predating utils/admin-fetch.
- **Fix:** In client/src/components/admin/EstimateModalsV2.jsx delete lines 26-38 (`const API_BASE …` and the local `function adminFetch`) and add `import { adminFetch } from "../../utils/admin-fetch";` after the existing imports. No call-site change: all three calls already pass `(path, { method, body })`. (Side effect, consistent with the canonical path: 401 now redirects to /admin/login and 429 retries once.)
- **Test:** EstimateModalsV2.test.jsx: mock global.fetch to resolve `new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'held for a re-price' }), { status: 409 })`, spy window.alert, render DeclineModalV2 with a reason selected, click Save, assert alert was called with a string containing 'held for a re-price' (today it receives 'Failed: HTTP 409').
- **Verifier:** confirmed — client/src/components/admin/EstimateModalsV2.jsx:28-38 defines a local `adminFetch` whose only error path is `if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${r.status}`)` (line 36); consumers alert `err.message` at :58 ('Follow-up failed: '), :124 ('Failed: '), :272 ('Extend failed: '). The server bodies it discards carry the reason: server/routes/admin-estimates.js:4959 `res.status(409).json({ error: 'This estimate is held for a re-price (a customer clarify reply). Revise it with the answered unit, or archive it, instead of declining.' })` on PATCH /:id (:4879); :4537 `{ error: 'No phone on file' }`, :4538 `{ error: 'Already accepted' }`, :4545-4546 409 on /:id/follow-up; /:id/extend (:4751) returns 404/422/`err.statusCode` bodies with `error`. The canonical helper client/src/utils/admin-fetch.js:86-105 reads `body?.error`, `body?.code`, `body` into `err.message/.code/.details`, same `adminFetch(path, options)` signature, and is already imported by 35 client files. Only consumer of the modals is EstimatesPageV2.jsx:44.

### F0085 · P2 · Confirm-write card renders in the TRUE dark navy D palette inside the white desktop palette (variant='dark' mismatch)

- **Location:** `client/src/components/admin/GlobalCommandPalette.jsx:998` (reader agents-ib:AIB-03)
- **Reproduction:** ⌘K on desktop, ask for any write tool (e.g. 'reschedule …'); the proposal card is dark navy.
- **Expected:** The operator confirmation card (the write trust boundary) looks the same everywhere: zinc 'light' variant as on the mobile sheet (1436), IntelligenceBarShell (481) and AgentEstimatePage (1087).
- **Actual:** On desktop ⌘K a #1e293b navy card with #e2e8f0 text, amber border and a #10b981 green Confirm button appears inside a white modal — the only place in the admin that shows the dark-mode D palette.
- **Likely cause:** The palette's local D was recoloured to light without updating the PendingActionsCard variant it passes.
- **Fix:** GlobalCommandPalette.jsx:998 → `<PendingActionsCard actions={pendingActions} variant="light" onResolved={onActionResolved} />`. (Follow-up per rule 19, separate: `variant="dark"` then has zero callers.)
- **Test:** GlobalCommandPalette.test.jsx (desktop, useIsMobile → false): mock /admin/intelligence-bar/query to return `{ response: 'ok', pendingActions: [{ id: 'p1', tool: 'x', summary: 's', expiresAt: … }] }`, submit, assert the card container has class containing 'bg-white' and no inline `background: #1e293b`.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — GlobalCommandPalette.jsx:49 `card: "#FFFFFF"` and :752 `background: D.card` make the desktop modal white, yet :998 renders `<PendingActionsCard actions={pendingActions} variant="dark" …>`. client/src/components/admin/PendingActionsCard.jsx:18-22 `const D = { bg: "#0f1923", card: "#1e293b", border: "#334155", … green: "#10b981", … text: "#e2e8f0" }`, :238-241 dark card `background: D.card, border: 1px solid … D.amber, borderRadius: 10`, :313 Confirm button `background: D.green`. Repo-wide usage grep: IntelligenceBarShell.jsx:481 `variant="light"`, GlobalCommandPalette.jsx:1436 (mobile) `variant="light"`, AgentEstimatePage.jsx:1087 `variant="light"` — line 998 is the only dark caller. Even the palette's sibling ToolActivityList 'dark' variant paints light slate (ToolActivityList.jsx:32-34 `#64748B` / `#334155` / `#E2E8F0`), so the navy card is the sole dark-mode surface in the admin. The palette's D was recoloured to light in commit 7d667f08a without touching the card variant.

### F0095 · P2 · Mobile New Customer sheet: all 9 fields placeholder-only with no accessible name, plus fake dropdown carets on plain inputs

- **Location:** `client/src/components/admin/MobileNewCustomerSheet.jsx:239` (reader forms:FORMS-02)
- **Reproduction:** Open /admin/customers on an iPhone → New customer → fill First name; the field's only label is gone.
- **Expected:** Admin consistency contract + the V2 pattern elsewhere (CustomersPageV2.jsx:692 `<label htmlFor={`${formId}-phone`} className={LABEL_CLS}>Phone *</label>`): visible label or at minimum aria-label per field; a caret only on a real <select>.
- **Actual:** Nine inputs (239-286, 343-408) rely solely on placeholder text, which disappears on entry; the '+1 ▾' (255-276), 'Country ▾' (299-322) and 'State ▾' (381-399) wrappers look like dropdowns but are decorative — the country cannot be changed and State is a free-text 2-char input. Required (first name + phone, line 84-87) is never marked.
- **Likely cause:** Built to match a screenshot (header comment IMG_3735/3736) rather than the form contract.
- **Fix:** Render a visible `<label htmlFor>` per field — the CustomersPageV2.jsx:692 pattern — with matching `id`s on the inputs at :239, :247, :266, :278, :323 (through AddressAutocomplete's `...inputProps`), :343, :362, :371, :381, :400 and on the <select> at :351, so the field's name stays on screen after entry (an `aria-label` alone is invisible and leaves the reproduction unchanged); add `required` + `aria-required="true"` to First name (:239) and Phone (:266). Leave the caret/select question to F0094's ruling.
- **Test:** RTL: render MobileNewCustomerSheet open; `getByLabelText('First name')`, `getByLabelText('Phone number')`, `getByLabelText('ZIP')` resolve to inputs whose associated <label> is in the document and visible (not aria-label only); `getByLabelText('First name')` has `required`.
- **Verifier:** downgraded — Facts verified: MobileNewCustomerSheet.jsx inputs :239-246 (`placeholder="First name"`), :247-254, :266-276 (`placeholder="Phone number"`), :278-286, :323-341 (AddressAutocomplete `placeholder="Address line 1"`), :343-350, :362-369, :371-378, :381-393 (State), :400-408 (ZIP) rely on placeholder only, and the <select> at :351 is unlabeled too; `canSave` :83-86 requires `form.firstName.trim() && form.phone.trim()` but no input carries `required`/`aria-required`; the Tier-1 pattern exists (CustomersPageV2.jsx `<label htmlFor=…>`); the caret claims match F0094 (:263, :318-320, :396-398). Over-rated at P1: HTML-AAM computes the placeholder as a fallback accessible name, so screen readers do announce these fields — the defect is the discouraged fallback and the visible label vanishing on entry, not 'no name'; and the required state already has a visible affordance (the Save pill dims/disables when invalid, :189-200 `disabled={!canSave \|\| submitting}`). Also overlaps F0094 (carets decided there). P2; the label/required half is a local, unambiguous attribute fix.

### F0127 · P2 · RevenuePanelV2 and CSRPanelV2 never check res.ok — auth/server failures render as the empty state; sibling panels handle errors three different ways

- **Location:** `client/src/components/dispatch/RevenuePanelV2.jsx:27` (reader dispatch-board:DB-18)
- **Reproduction:** Expire the admin token and open ?tab=revenue — 'No jobs found for <date>' with no error.
- **Expected:** All four reference panels share one loading / error-with-retry / empty pattern; a 401/500 is shown as a failure, never as 'No jobs found'.
- **Actual:** Job Scores and CSR Booking swallow non-OK responses (a 401 body `{error}` becomes `[]`), so an expired session or server error reads as an empty day; Insights has a full error card, Tech Match an inline alert, Job Scores/CSR none.
- **Likely cause:** 1:1 port of V1 panels that also swallowed errors.
- **Fix:** RevenuePanelV2.jsx:27-31 and CSRPanelV2.jsx:33-43: add `if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`)` after fetch, an `error` state set in catch, and render the InsightsPanelV2.jsx:79-82 block (role=alert message + secondary Retry calling load/loadSlots) instead of the empty-state text when error is set.
- **Test:** RTL: mock fetch -> { ok: false, status: 500, json: async () => ({ error: 'boom' }) } for RevenuePanelV2; expect getByRole('alert') and getByRole('button', { name: /retry/i }); expect queryByText(/No jobs found/) toBeNull.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — RevenuePanelV2.jsx:27-31 `const res = await fetch('/api/dispatch/jobs?...', { headers: authHeader() }); const data = await res.json(); const sorted = (Array.isArray(data) ? data : []).sort(...)` then `} catch { setJobs([]); }` — no res.ok; :140-142 `{!jobs.length && (<div ...>No jobs found for {date}</div>)}`. CSRPanelV2.jsx:33-43 same shape, `setSlots(data.slots \|\| [])`. The server returns non-array bodies on failure: server/routes/dispatch.js:431-439 `res.json(jobs)` / `res.status(500).json({ error: err.message })`, and server/index.js:693 mounts `/api/dispatch` behind `adminAuthenticate` + `requireTechOrAdmin`, so an expired token yields a 401 `{error}` body that becomes `[]` -> 'No jobs found'. The shell's 401 handling (AdminLayoutV2.jsx:148) fires only on the /admin/auth/me probe at mount, not per fetch. Siblings differ as described: InsightsPanelV2.jsx:77-83 role=status loading + Card error + Retry; TechMatchPanelV2.jsx:115/:121 inline role=alert + 'Retry match'. The panels' 'strict 1:1' header comments describe the V2 port, not a bar on adding error states.

### F0166 · P2 · Schedule-appointment submit sets `saving` only after an awaited re-quote fetch, leaving a double-submit window for auto-priced mosquito lines

- **Location:** `client/src/components/schedule/CreateAppointmentModal.jsx:1773` (reader states:STATES-09)
- **Reproduction:** Add a one-time mosquito line without a price, tap Schedule twice quickly on a slow network.
- **Expected:** Re-entrancy guard at the top of handleSubmit (`if (saving) return; setSaving(true)`), like submitQuickAdd (:1331).
- **Actual:** While the re-quote request is in flight `saving` is still false, so the Schedule button stays enabled and a second tap runs a second handleSubmit; both then POST the appointment groups.
- **Likely cause:** The re-quote step was inserted before the existing setSaving(true) call.
- **Fix:** Add `const submittingRef = useRef(false);` next to :914; at the top of handleSubmit (after the :1751 validity return) `if (submittingRef.current) return; submittingRef.current = true; setSaving(true);` and wrap the body so every early `return` and the end path run `submittingRef.current = false; setSaving(false);` (a try/finally around the whole body is simplest — keep the existing :1999 setSaving(false) or fold it into the finally). Delete the now-duplicate :1789 `setSaving(true);`.
- **Test:** CreateAppointmentModal.test: one-time mosquito line with no price, mock the mosquito-onetime-quote fetch to a pending promise, click Schedule twice, resolve, assert exactly one POST /admin/schedule.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — CreateAppointmentModal.jsx:1750-1751 `const handleSubmit = async () => { if (!selectedCustomer \|\| services.length === 0 \|\| bookingPropertyState === 'loading') return;` — no `saving` check. :1772-1773 `if (services.some((s) => isOneTimeMosquitoLine(s) && !lineHasEnteredPrice(s))) { try { const fresh = await adminFetch(`/admin/schedule/mosquito-onetime-quote?…`);` runs BEFORE :1789 `setSaving(true);`. :2165 `const canSubmit = !!selectedCustomer && !!selectedService && !saving && …` gates the three buttons (:2268, :3436, :3451), so while the re-quote is in flight a second tap enters handleSubmit again; both calls then reach the POST loop, and :1951-1952 `const r = await adminFetch('/admin/schedule', { method: 'POST' … }); createdGroupKeysRef.current.add(key);` only dedupes AFTER the first POST resolves. Server side the race-safe duplicate guard is recurring-only: server/routes/admin-schedule.js:5486 `if (isRecurring && req.body.allowDuplicateSeries !== true) {` … :5496 `duplicate_recurring_series` — the one-time mosquito booking that triggers the awaited branch has no such backstop, so two one-time appointments (and the first-group customer confirmation, twice) result. submitQuickAdd :1331 `if (quickAddSubmitting) return;` is the in-file pattern the submit path lacks.

### F0171 · P2 · Mobile dispatch tech assignment fails silently — PUT error goes to console only [F0172 folded in: same line, same defect]

- **Location:** `client/src/components/schedule/InlineTechPicker.jsx:18` (reader states:STATES-02)
- **Merged:** F0172 (schedule-mobile-sheets:SMS-06) reported the same line and defect; its verifier labelled it a duplicate, so it is folded into this entry and counted once.
- **Reproduction:** Dispatch day view on mobile width, tap the tech initial on a stop, pick a tech while the API returns 500.
- **Expected:** A failed assignment tells the operator (inline alert text or toast) and leaves the picker open with the previous value.
- **Actual:** The picker just un-busies; no message, no state change. The picker is live in DispatchPageV2 via MobileDispatchList (:271, mounted at DispatchPageV2 :1385 and :1748), so a scheduling write can fail with zero feedback. grep for error\|alert in the file finds no UI path.
- **Likely cause:** Component predates the seriesMove apiErrorMessage helper and never had an error state.
- **Fix:** Same edit as F0172 — apply once.
- **Test:** See F0172.
- **Verifier:** downgraded — Code is as quoted: InlineTechPicker.jsx:27-33 `if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`); onAssigned?.(techId); } catch (e) { console.error('Assign failed:', e); } setBusy(false); onClose();` — no error state, no UI path (file grep: no `error`/`alert` rendering). Sole consumer MobileDispatchList.jsx:271 `<InlineTechPicker … onAssigned={() => { onRefresh?.(); }} onClose={() => setShowTechPicker(false)} …`, mounted at DispatchPageV2.jsx:1385 and :1748. Same defect as F0172 (fix once). P1 is over-rated: on failure onAssigned/onRefresh never fire so the row keeps showing the OLD initial (the failure is visible as a no-op, not as a false success), it is a single dispatcher's phone list, and no data is corrupted. P2 states defect.

### F0186 · P2 · Checkout line items round money to whole dollars (toFixed(0)) while Total and extras show cents — a $112.50 service reads '$113' above a '$112.50' total

- **Location:** `client/src/components/schedule/MobileCheckoutSheet.jsx:409` (reader schedule-mobile-sheets:SMS-04)
- **Expected:** Every money figure on the checkout review shows cents (owner directive in lib/money.js: 'every price shows the full amount with cents — never a bare $149'); line items sum visibly to the Total.
- **Actual:** Base service and add-on rows use toFixed(0), so interpolated-bracket prices with cents are rounded (112.5 -> '$113') and the visible rows do not add up to the Total shown two rows below. Same toFixed(0) in MobileServicePickerSheet:36 and MobileServiceLibrary:415/802.
- **Fix:** MobileCheckoutSheet.jsx:409 `${baseServicePrice.toFixed(0)}` → `${baseServicePrice.toFixed(2)}`; :430 `${Number(addon.estimatedPrice \|\| 0).toFixed(0)}` → `${Number(addon.estimatedPrice \|\| 0).toFixed(2)}`.
- **Test:** MobileCheckoutSheet.test: service.estimatedPrice = 112.5 → expect getByText('$112.50') on the base row and the Charge button name 'Charge $112.50'; with a 12.5 add-on expect '$12.50'.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — MobileCheckoutSheet.jsx:409 `${baseServicePrice.toFixed(0)}` and :430 `${Number(addon.estimatedPrice \|\| 0).toFixed(0)}` versus :455 `{isDiscount ? '−' : ''}${Math.abs(Number(e.amount)).toFixed(2)}` and :484 `${total.toFixed(2)}`. The math keeps cents — :96-98 `const baseServicePrice = splitAppointmentAddons ? Math.max(0, Math.round((price - appointmentAddonTotal) * 100) / 100) : price;` and :147 `const total = Math.max(0, totalBeforePrepaid - prepaidCredit);` — so a 112.5 line renders '$113' above a '$112.50' Total; rows visibly fail to sum. lib/money.js:1-5 records the cents directive (customer surfaces), and the admin contract (line 31) wants tabular money; the mismatch stands on its own regardless. The existing suite (MobileCheckoutSheet.test.jsx) asserts only $X.00 strings on the invoice-preview path, so the fix breaks no test. Scope note: MobileServicePickerSheet.jsx:36 and MobileServiceLibrary.jsx:415/:802 are catalog base_price summaries that do not sum to a displayed total — leave those to the owner; the autofix is the two checkout lines.

"text-alert-fg" : "text-ink-primary"`. ### F0227 · P2 · Badge silently renders unstyled for unknown tones; 14 live call sites use non-existent 'muted'/'danger' tones

- **Location:** `client/src/components/ui/Badge.jsx:8` (reader ui-primitives:UIP-03)
- **Reproduction:** Open /admin/newsletter — the 'active subscribers' / 'sent campaigns' badges have no chip background; open Email Templates health issues — the event_type badge is not red.
- **Expected:** Only neutral/strong/alert exist. An unknown tone should either resolve to a documented fallback or fail loudly in dev; a 'danger' badge on the email-template issues table should read as an alert.
- **Actual:** TONES['muted'] / TONES['danger'] are undefined, cn() drops them, so the badge renders with no background and inherited colour — an invisible box with uppercase 11px text. Static sites: NewsletterTabs.jsx:2425, 2774, 3107, 3115; NewsletterPage.jsx:166, 172, 493; EmailAutomationsPanelV2.jsx:944; CustomerEstimatesPanel.jsx:282; EmailTemplatesPanelV2.jsx:383 (danger). Dynamic: EmailAutomationsPanelV2.jsx:711, NewsletterPage.jsx:176. The 'issue' badge on the templates health table therefore never reads red. Button has the same silent-undefined pattern for variant/size (no live misuse found).
- **Likely cause:** No prop validation; authors assumed a `muted` tone existed.
- **Fix:** Badge.jsx: `const toneClass = TONES[tone] \|\| TONES.neutral; if (import.meta.env.DEV && !TONES[tone]) console.warn(`Badge: unknown tone "${tone}"`);` and use `toneClass` / `DOT_TONES[tone] \|\| DOT_TONES.neutral`; sweep the 13 call sites from `muted` → `neutral` and `danger` → `alert` in the same PR, so no alias is needed and the invalid vocabulary leaves the repo. Same `\|\| SIZES.md` / `\|\| VARIANTS.primary` fallback + DEV warn in Button.jsx.
- **Test:** RTL: render <Badge tone="alert"> and assert `bg-alert-bg text-alert-fg`; render <Badge tone="nope"> and assert the neutral background class is present (fallback) and, in DEV, that console.warn was called once. Do not test `muted` / `danger` — after the sweep they are not part of the API.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — client/src/components/ui/Badge.jsx:8-12 `TONES = { neutral, strong, alert }` only, :21 `className={cn(BASE, TONES[tone], className)}` — an unknown tone is `undefined`, cn drops it, so the chip renders with no background and inherited colour (BASE :4-6 keeps the 11px uppercase text). Live misuse is 13 sites, not 14 (title) / 12 (body): static `tone="muted"` at pages/admin/NewsletterTabs.jsx:2425, 2774, 3107, 3115; NewsletterPage.jsx:166, 172, 493; EmailAutomationsPanelV2.jsx:944; CustomerEstimatesPanel.jsx:282; `tone="danger"` at EmailTemplatesPanelV2.jsx:383; dynamic muted at EmailAutomationsPanelV2.jsx:707 and :711 and NewsletterPage.jsx:176. Every one imports Badge from `../../components/ui` (NewsletterTabs:29, NewsletterPage:24, EmailAutomationsPanelV2:34, CustomerEstimatesPanel:23, EmailTemplatesPanelV2:20-31). The health-issues `danger` badge therefore never reads alert red; it is a genuine alert (failure rows), so the alias respects the alert-fg rule. Button.jsx:9-22 has the same silent `SIZES[size]`/`VARIANTS[variant]` pattern with no live misuse.

### F0314 · P2 · A failed property switch gives the customer no feedback

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/PortalPage.jsx:15556` (reader customer-portal-app-b:F02)
- **Reproduction:** Multi-property account, throttle network to offline, open account menu, tap another property.
- **Expected:** A rejected /auth/select-property (network drop, 5xx, 401) should tell the customer the switch did not happen and leave a retry.
- **Actual:** switchProperty stores the raw err.message in AuthContext.error, but PortalPage never reads `error` (15320 destructures only customer/logout/properties/propertiesError/refreshProperties/switchProperty/refreshCustomer). The 'Switching' label (15828) simply disappears and the menu stays on the old property with no message.
- **Fix:** PortalPage.jsx after the `if (switched) { ... }` block at 15558-15570 add `else { showCustomerAlert('We could not switch to that property just now. Please try again.'); }` (showCustomerAlert is already imported at line 13).
- **Test:** Mock api.selectAuthProperty to reject; open the account menu, click another property, assert showCustomerAlert was called with the copy and no 'Switching' text remains.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — client/src/pages/PortalPage.jsx:15556-15558 `const switched = await switchProperty(propertyId); setSwitchingPropertyId(null); if (switched) { ... }` has no else branch, while the sibling failure two lines up (15551-15554) does surface: `showCustomerAlert('Your latest property edits could not be saved, ...')`. client/src/hooks/useAuth.jsx:371-374 `} catch (err) { setError(err.message); return false; }`. PortalPage.jsx:15320 destructures `{ customer, logout, properties, propertiesError, refreshProperties, switchProperty, refreshCustomer }` — never `error`. The only other consumer, client/src/App.jsx:437 + 480-482, renders `{error && (<p ...>{error}</p>)}` inside the 'Loading your portal' splash, which shows only while `loading` is true — and useAuth.jsx:363 `setLoading(true)` runs only after `api.selectAuthProperty` succeeds, so a rejected request leaves loading false and the message unrendered. The 'Switching' label at 15828 just disappears.

### F0341 · P2 · Invoice list refetches on every keystroke with no debounce and no latest-request guard; failed loads read as 'No invoices match'

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/AdminInvoicesPage.jsx:975` (reader billing-admin:BA-05)
- **Reproduction:** Throttle network, type quickly in the invoice search, watch rows flicker to an earlier query's results; or block /admin/invoices and observe 'No invoices match'.
- **Expected:** Search is debounced (the same file debounces customer search at 5019 by 300 ms), only the newest response paints (BankingPage reqIdRef pattern, lines 191-215), there is a loading state, and a failed fetch shows an error with retry rather than an empty state.
- **Actual:** Typing 'smith' fires five list requests; a slower earlier response can overwrite the newer rows and `total`. No `loading` flag exists for the list, so the first paint and any network/401 failure both render 'No invoices match'.
- **Likely cause:** List effect keyed directly on `query`; error path was written as a silent reset.
- **Fix:** Add a 300 ms debounced copy of `query` (useState + useEffect/setTimeout) and put it in `load`'s deps; `const reqIdRef = useRef(0)` with `const reqId = ++reqIdRef.current` at the top of load and `if (reqId !== reqIdRef.current) return;` after the await; add `listLoading` / `listError` state; render 'Loading…' and "Couldn't load invoices — Retry" (onClick={() => load()}) branches ahead of the empty state at :1573.
- **Test:** Mock adminFetch to resolve two list calls out of order and assert the later query's rows/total win; reject the fetch and assert the error copy renders instead of 'No invoices match'.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — AdminInvoicesPage.jsx:976-1011 `load` has deps `[PAGE_SIZE, customerFilterId, datePeriod, filter, query, sort]` and :1013-1015 `useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load, refreshKey]);`, so every keystroke through :1432 `onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}` rebuilds `load` and refires the list request; no reqId/AbortController guard (0 hits in the file) and no list loading flag (only :952 `loadingMore`). Failure path :986-996 `adminFetch(...).catch(() => null)` -> `setInvoices([]); setTotal(0)` -> :1573-1582 `rows.length === 0` renders 'No invoices match'. The same file debounces the builder's customer search 300 ms (:5019-5027) and BankingPage.jsx:191-215 has the `reqIdRef` + `loadError` pattern.

### F0342 · P2 · Reverse prepaid invoice has no pending state — double-click fires two money-moving POSTs

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/AdminInvoicesPage.jsx:1161` (reader states:STATES-01)
- **Reproduction:** Invoices page, a prepaid invoice row, click Reverse prepaid, confirm, click again before the toast lands.
- **Expected:** Same guard as the sibling actions: setRowActionBusy(id) before the POST, cleared in finally, and the button disabled/dimmed while rowActionBusy === inv.id (handleVoid :1126-1139, button :1965-1972).
- **Actual:** No busy flag is set and the button is never disabled; a second click while the first POST is in flight sends a second reverse-prepaid request for the same invoice.
- **Likely cause:** Handler added after the rowActionBusy pattern and never wired to it.
- **Fix:** Wrap :1167-1179 in `setRowActionBusy(id); try { … } catch … finally { setRowActionBusy(null); }`; at :1975 add `disabled={rowActionBusy === inv.id}` and `style={{ ...sBtn("transparent", D.red, isMobile), opacity: rowActionBusy === inv.id ? 0.5 : 1 }}` to match Void/Unvoid.
- **Test:** Render a prepaid row with a never-resolving adminFetch mock, click Reverse prepaid twice (confirm mocked true), assert exactly one POST and the button disabled.
- **Verifier:** downgraded — Client side is as claimed: AdminInvoicesPage.jsx:1161-1180 `handleReversePrepaid` never calls setRowActionBusy, and the button at :1975-1981 has no `disabled`/opacity, unlike handleVoid :1126-1139 and its button :1965-1972 — a double-click does send two POSTs. But the money side is guarded on the server: admin-invoices.js:2354-2364 locks the row `trx('invoices').where({ id }).forUpdate().first()` inside a transaction, :2395-2397 throws 400 'No applied credit to restore' when `restore <= 0`, and :2413-2421 / :2440-2443 write `credit_applied: 0` (and the restored status) in that same transaction — the second request finds nothing to restore and is refused, so credit cannot be returned twice. The real defect is the missing busy state plus a spurious 'Reverse failed: …' toast (:1178) after the success toast — P2, not a P1 double money movement.

### F0343 · P2 · Client-side overdue check uses browser-local time and breaks on the ISO date shape the same file already normalises

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/AdminInvoicesPage.jsx:1365` (reader billing-admin:BA-19)
- **Reproduction:** Open an unpaid invoice due yesterday before the overdue cron runs: list shows 'Sent'.
- **Expected:** One overdue rule (ET calendar day, both 'YYYY-MM-DD' and midnight-UTC ISO inputs) shared with the pay page, which already imports `isInvoiceDueDateOverdue` (PayPageV2.jsx:2608).
- **Actual:** `invoices.due_date` is a pg DATE column (migration 20260401000082_invoices.js:17 `t.date('due_date')`); no `setTypeParser`/`dateStrings` override exists in server/, so the list serialises it as an ISO instant, and `"2026-09-07T00:00:00.000Z" + "T23:59:59"` is an Invalid Date — the client branch never fires and the row only reads Overdue once the cron flips status. When the value is date-only the comparison is in the operator's browser zone rather than ET.
- **Likely cause:** Local helper predates lib/invoiceDates.
- **Fix:** AdminInvoicesPage.jsx:80: `import { invoiceDateOnly, formatInvoiceDate, isInvoiceDueDateOverdue } from "../../lib/invoiceDates";` and replace :1365-1369 with `if (isInvoiceDueDateOverdue(inv.due_date)) return { key: "overdue", label: "Overdue", color: D.red };`.
- **Test:** Unit test getDisplayStatus: { status: 'sent', due_date: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z' } -> key 'overdue'; due_date = today's ET date -> not overdue.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — AdminInvoicesPage.jsx:1365-1369 `const due = new Date(inv.due_date + "T23:59:59"); if (Date.now() > due.getTime()) return { key: "overdue", … }`. Migration 20260401000082_invoices.js:17 `t.date('due_date')`; InvoiceService.list selects `"invoices.due_date"` raw (invoice.js:3686) and the route returns rows untouched (admin-invoices.js:559-570 `res.json({ invoices, total, page: pageNum })`); no `setTypeParser`/`dateStrings` exists anywhere under server/ or knexfile, so pg hands back a Date and JSON serialises it as 'YYYY-MM-DDT00:00:00.000Z'; the concatenation is an Invalid Date, `Date.now() > NaN` is false and the branch never fires. Only :1370 `inv.status === "overdue"` can label a row, and status 'overdue' is written only by the dispute-reopen paths (stripe-webhook.js:6479, :6718) — there is no overdue cron, so the impact is larger than the finding states: ordinary past-due invoices never read 'Overdue' in this list. lib/invoiceDates.js:21-25 `isInvoiceDueDateOverdue` already normalises both shapes (`invoiceDateOnly` regex at :7) and compares ET calendar days (`etDateString`); PayPageV2.jsx:2608 uses it and this page already imports from the module (:80).

### F0390 · P2 · Opening a conversation lands on the OLDEST message; no scroll-to-latest

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/CommunicationsPageV2.jsx:591` (reader communications:COMM-03)
- **Reproduction:** Desktop: open any thread with >10 messages in the SMS tab.
- **Expected:** A chat view opens scrolled to the newest message (CustomerSmsPanel does `requestAnimationFrame(scrollToBottom)` after load, lines 227-229).
- **Actual:** activeThread.messages is sorted newest-first (2045) and reversed to oldest-first for render; the 500px desktop scroll pane starts at scrollTop 0, so a long thread (the log loads 500 messages/page) opens on the oldest bubble and the operator scrolls down every time. On mobile there is no max-height, so the newest bubble is at the bottom of the page under the composer.
- **Likely cause:** V1 parity; no ref/effect on the scroll container.
- **Fix:** In ConversationViewV2 (:528): `const scrollRef = useRef(null); useEffect(() => { const el = scrollRef.current; if (el) el.scrollTop = el.scrollHeight; }, [thread.contactPhone, thread.ourNumber]);` and `ref={scrollRef}` on the :591 div. Key on thread identity, NOT messages.length — 'Load older SMS history' (:2878) prepends and would otherwise jump to the bottom; if live-appended messages should follow, add CustomerSmsPanel's near-bottom check (:186-229). On mobile the pane is not scrollable so the effect is a harmless no-op.
- **Test:** RTL: render ConversationViewV2 with 30 messages, Object.defineProperty(scrollHeight) on the pane, assert scrollTop === scrollHeight after mount and unchanged after a prepend re-render.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — CommunicationsPageV2.jsx:591 `<div className="flex-1 md:max-h-[500px] md:overflow-y-auto flex flex-col gap-2">` carries no ref; grep scrollTop\|scrollHeight\|flex-col-reverse in the file returns nothing (the only scroll calls are :2161 scrollIntoView on the compose card and :3084 `.admin-main` scrollTo). :2045 `t.messages.sort((a, b) => new Date(b.createdAt) - new Date(a.createdAt));` (newest-first) and :2870 `messages={activeThread.messages.slice().reverse()}` (oldest-first render), :257 `const SMS_LOG_PAGE_SIZE = 500;`. The 500px desktop pane therefore opens at scrollTop 0 = oldest bubble; on mobile the pane has no max-height and the compose Card (:2281) sits above the thread Card (:2866), so the newest bubble is at the bottom of the page. CustomerSmsPanel.jsx:180-181 `el.scrollTop = el.scrollHeight` + :227-229 `requestAnimationFrame(scrollToBottom)` is the sibling behaviour.

### F0393 · P2 · Reading a thread in the inbox does not refresh the global Messages badge (C360 panel does)

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/CommunicationsPageV2.jsx:941` (reader communications:COMM-05)
- **Reproduction:** With an unread thread, open it in the SMS tab; watch the sidebar Messages badge stay for up to 30s.
- **Expected:** Unread indicators agree across surfaces: after the read POST succeeds the shell badge updates immediately, as CustomerSmsPanel does (`notifyUnreadChanged()` at line 195 after its own /messages/read call).
- **Actual:** markMessagesRead never calls notifyUnreadChanged(); the AdminLayoutV2 badge keeps the stale count until the next 30s poll (useUnreadConversations POLL_MS = 30000) even though the thread row's dot cleared instantly.
- **Likely cause:** The event hook was added with the C360 panel and not wired into the older inbox path.
- **Fix:** CommunicationsPageV2.jsx: add `import { notifyUnreadChanged } from "../../hooks/useUnreadConversations";` and inside markMessagesRead's try, directly after the awaited POST (:949), add `notifyUnreadChanged();`.
- **Test:** Extend an inbox RTL test: mock adminFetch, click a thread with an unread inbound, await the read POST, assert a window 'waves:sms-unread-changed' event was dispatched (pattern already in CustomerSmsPanel.test.jsx).
- **Verifier:** confirmed — CommunicationsPageV2.jsx:941-953: `await adminFetch("/admin/communications/messages/read", { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify({ messageIds: unreadIds, conversationIds, readBefore }) });` is followed only by `} catch { loadData(smsSearch.trim()); }` — nothing runs on success. grep notifyUnreadChanged\|useUnreadConversations\|UNREAD_CHANGED\|dispatchEvent in the file: no matches. Repo-wide the only emitter is CustomerSmsPanel.jsx:195 `notifyUnreadChanged();` after its own /messages/read POST. hooks/useUnreadConversations.js:12 `const POLL_MS = 30000;`, :36 `window.addEventListener(UNREAD_CHANGED_EVENT, load);`, consumed by AdminLayoutV2.jsx:106. The row dot clears at once via :927-938 (`isRead: true` → :2047 `t.unread`), so the shell badge lags the row by up to 30 s.

### F0394 · P2 · Upload / AI-draft / call failures use window.alert while the same composer reports other errors inline

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/CommunicationsPageV2.jsx:1384` (reader communications:COMM-26)
- **Reproduction:** Attach 11 images, or click AI Draft with an empty To.
- **Expected:** One feedback channel per composer: the existing sendResult line (with aria-live) for every outcome.
- **Actual:** Send, link-insert and rewrite errors go to the inline sendResult line, but attachment errors, the attachment cap, the AI-draft precondition and AI-draft failure open blocking native alerts; CallBridgeLink (26/29), CallRoutingSettingsV2 (90/105), useEmailInbox (103/250/300) and useEmailEditor (65) do the same. Native alerts block the whole tab and are unstyled on iOS PWA.
- **Likely cause:** Later features grafted with quick alert() calls.
- **Fix:** Replace the five composer alerts with the inline line: :1331 → `setSendResult({ ok: false, text: "Max 10 attachments per message" }); return;`; :1353-1361 → `setSendResult({ ok: false, text: <same budget string> }); return;`; :1384 → `setSendResult({ ok: false, text: \`Upload failed: ${e.message}\` });`; :1402 → `setSendResult({ ok: false, text: "Enter a To number first" }); return;`; :1422 → `setSendResult({ ok: false, text: "AI draft failed: " + e.message });`. Add `role="status"` to the :2803 div. Leave CallBridgeLink / CallRoutingSettingsV2 / email hooks to a separate app-wide pattern ruling.
- **Test:** RTL: vi.spyOn(window, 'alert'); trigger AI Draft with an empty To and an 11th attachment; assert alert was not called and the sendResult status line shows the copy.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — CommunicationsPageV2.jsx alert() sites: :1331 `alert("Max 10 attachments per message");`, :1353-1361 the attachment-budget alert, :1384 `alert(\`Upload failed: ${e.message}\`);`, :1402 `alert("Enter a To number first");`, :1422 `alert("AI draft failed: " + e.message);` — versus :1317 `setSendResult({ ok: false, text: \`Failed: ${e.message}\` });` for send failures, rendered at :2802-2810. One correction: that line is a plain `<div className={cn("mt-2.5 text-12", ...)}>{sendResult.text}</div>` with no role/aria-live today (the reader's 'with aria-live' is aspirational; the proposed role= is what would add it). Related surfaces do the same: CallBridgeLink.jsx:26/29, CallRoutingSettingsV2.jsx:90/105, useEmailInbox.js:103/250/300, useEmailEditor.js:65 — and 40 admin files use alert( repo-wide, so the app-wide replacement is a pattern ruling; the composer's own inline channel already exists, so the five composer sites are a local fix.

### F0406 · P2 · Compliance dashboard shows 'Loading dashboard…' forever when the API fails

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/CompliancePage.jsx:132` (reader admin-ops-pages:OPS-005)
- **Reproduction:** Block /api/admin/compliance-v2/dashboard in DevTools and open /admin/compliance — the page never leaves 'Loading dashboard…'.
- **Expected:** Contract required states: error with retry when retry is safe; never a perpetual loading indicator.
- **Actual:** useFetch (85-100) does `.then((r) => r.json()).then(setData).catch(console.error)` — a 403/500 body still parses to an object without `warningCount`, and a network error leaves data null with loading false; both branches render the loading line indefinitely. Same pattern in LicensesTab (`if (loading)` then reads data?.technicians) and the other tabs that use useFetch.
- **Likely cause:** useFetch never modelled failure.
- **Fix:** useFetch (:84-98): add `const [error, setError] = useState(null);` and in reload `setError(null); fetch(url, { headers: headers(token) }).then(async (r) => { if (!r.ok) throw new Error(\`HTTP ${r.status}\`); return r.json(); }).then(setData).catch((e) => setError(e.message \|\| "Request failed")).finally(() => setLoading(false));` returning `{ data, loading, error, reload }`. DashboardTab (:134-140) and LicensesTab (:747): render `if (error) return <div style={{ color: D.red, padding: 20 }}>Couldn't load — {error} <button type="button" onClick={reload}>Retry</button></div>;` before the loading branch, and keep the loading line only while `loading`.
- **Test:** CompliancePage.test.jsx: mock fetch to reject (and to resolve { ok:false, status:500 }) → expect the error copy and a Retry control; clicking Retry issues a second request.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — CompliancePage.jsx:84-98 useFetch: `fetch(url, { headers: headers(token) }).then((r) => r.json()).then(setData).catch(console.error).finally(() => setLoading(false));` — never checks `r.ok`, keeps no error state, returns `{ data, loading, reload }`. :134-140 `if (loading \|\| !data) return (<div style={{ color: D.muted, padding: 20 }}>Loading dashboard…</div>);`. A network error or non-JSON body rejects → data stays null → the loading line is permanent (confirmed). Correction to the finding: a JSON 403/500 body (`{ error }`) resolves, so `data` is truthy and :151-175 renders the StatCards with `data.ytdApplications` / `data.warningCount` undefined → '—' everywhere — a silent blank dashboard, not perpetual loading; 'both branches render the loading line' is half wrong. LicensesTab :702/:747-748 uses the same hook (`if (loading) return ...Loading…`, then reads data?.). CompliancePage.test.jsx covers token/tab/deep-link cases only (:71-113), no failure path.

### F0407 · P2 · ContentCalendar legend colors Social the same as Blog while items use a different color; TYPE_ICONS are empty strings

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/ContentCalendar.jsx:273` (reader admin-marketing-pages:AM-19)
- **Reproduction:** Open the calendar with both blog and social items scheduled; the legend shows two identical dark dots.
- **Expected:** Legend swatches match the item colors; no empty decorative spans.
- **Actual:** Blog and Social legend dots are both #18181B while social items are #71717A, so the legend misidentifies social posts; every item row and the day-detail list render an empty 16px span where an icon used to be.
- **Likely cause:** Token fold collapsed teal and purple to the same hex; TYPE_COLORS was patched but the legend was not; emoji stripped.
- **Fix:** Legend :275-281: use `TYPE_COLORS.blog`, `TYPE_COLORS.social`, `TYPE_COLORS.rss` for the three dots (or map over TYPE_COLORS entries); delete :42 TYPE_ICONS; :399 → literal `•`; delete the :500-502 empty span (or render `•` in `TYPE_COLORS[item.type]` there). Behaviour-preserving: the grid already always shows the bullet.
- **Test:** RTL: legend swatch inline colors equal TYPE_COLORS.blog/social/rss; the day-detail list renders no empty 16px span.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — ContentCalendar.jsx:14 `teal: "#18181B"`, :18 `purple: "#18181B"`; legend :275-281 `<span style={{ color: D.teal }}>●</span>Blog` / `<span style={{ color: D.purple }}>●</span>Social` / `<span style={{ color: D.green }}>●</span>RSS Auto` — Blog and Social dots are both zinc-900. Items are driven by :41 `const TYPE_COLORS = { blog: "#18181B", social: "#71717A", rss: "#15803D" };` (:392-393 grid, :517-518 day detail), and the :8-9 comment says TYPE_COLORS exists 'so blog/social/rss stay visually distinct after the fold' — the legend was never moved onto it. :42 `const TYPE_ICONS = { blog: "", social: "", rss: "" };`; grid :399 `{TYPE_ICONS[item.type] \|\| "•"}` always falls through to the bullet; day detail :500-502 `<span style={{ fontSize: 16 }}>{TYPE_ICONS[item.type]}</span>` is always empty. `git log -S` shows the empty icons and purple=#18181B both arrived in 7d667f08a (the token-fold commit).

- **Reproduction:** Estimates → click a customer with a draft or expired estimate.

- **Actual:** No background/text classes are applied, so those badges appear as bare uppercase 11px text with padding.

- **Fix:** CustomerEstimatesPanel.jsx:28 `draft: "neutral"`, :34 `expired: "neutral"`, :282 `tone="neutral"`, :402 `\|\| "neutral"`.
- **Test:** RTL: render the panel with a draft estimate and a commercial customer; the Draft and Commercial badges carry classes bg-zinc-100 and text-zinc-700.

The same phantom tone is used in NewsletterTabs.jsx:2425/2774/3107/3115, NewsletterPage.jsx:166/172/176/493 and EmailAutomationsPanelV2.jsx:154/707/711/944, and DECISIONS.md:229 records a PR describing `tone="strong"/"muted"` — the intent for a lighter variant exists but Badge never got it. Mapping to neutral fixes this file with the existing palette; adding a real `muted` tone to the primitive is a separate design call.

### F0431 · P2 · Desktop stats row renders '12services 3done 9left $617revenue' — spaces lost in a formatter pass

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/DispatchPageV2.jsx:1219` (reader dispatch-board:DB-03)
- **Reproduction:** Open /admin/dispatch?tab=schedule on desktop in Day view — the centered stats pill reads e.g. '4services 1done 3left'.
- **Expected:** '12 services · 3 done · 9 left · $617 revenue' (the pre-refactor markup was `{totalCount}</span> services`).
- **Actual:** JSX trims the newline between `</span>` and the word, so the number and its label render glued together for services/done/left (1219-1241) and revenue (1255-1261); commit 7d667f08a (2026-05-14) reformatted the single-line spans and dropped the literal space. The revenue span also picks up a stray leading `{" "}` after its left border (1256).
- **Likely cause:** Prettier-style reflow of `<span>…</span> services` into multi-line JSX without preserving the whitespace as `{' '}`.
- **Fix:** DispatchPageV2.jsx: insert `{" "}` between `</span>` and the label at :1222-1223 (services), :1228-1229 (done), :1239-1240 (left), :1259-1260 (revenue); delete the stray `{" "}` at :1256. Alternatively give each stat span `className="inline-flex gap-1"`.
- **Test:** RTL: render DispatchPageV2 (board tab, desktop matchMedia) with a 2-service day payload and assert `screen.getByText((_, el) => el.textContent === '2 services')` resolves; also assert '$… revenue' has a space.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — client/src/pages/admin/DispatchPageV2.jsx:1219-1224 `<span>\n<span className="u-nums font-medium text-zinc-900">\n{totalCount}\n</span>\nservices\n</span>` — the label sits on its own line after `</span>`; JSX removes whitespace runs that contain a newline between an element and text, so no space is emitted (same shape for done :1225-1230, left :1231-1241, revenue :1255-1261 which also has a stray `{" "}` at :1256 right after the border-left span opens). The parent at :1217 is `flex gap-3` but each number/label pair is inline inside one `<span>`, so no gap applies between them. `git show 7d667f08a` (2026-05-14 "Improve admin workspace navigation consistency") shows the removed line `<span><span className="u-nums font-medium text-zinc-900">{totalCount}</span> services</span>` with a literal space. Rendered when `activeTab === "board"` (:1214; :232 `{ id: "board", label: "Schedule" }`) on desktop only (`hidden md:flex` :1215).

### F0432 · P2 · List mode keeps the Prev/Next/Today date nav and month header live even though ScheduleListView ignores the page date

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/DispatchPageV2.jsx:1476` (reader dispatch-board:DB-16)
- **Reproduction:** Desktop Schedule tab → List → click Next twice: header advances two months, table unchanged.
- **Expected:** In List mode the date nav is hidden (or drives the list's From/To range), and the header reflects the list's filter range.
- **Actual:** Prev/Next shift `date` by a month, the header shows 'September 2026', a 'Today' button appears — none of which changes the table, which keeps its own From/To filters. Each click also triggers a full page 'Loading schedule…' (DB-05).
- **Likely cause:** List mode was added to ViewModeSelectorV2 after the shared nav row was built for calendar modes.
- **Fix:** Option A (smallest): wrap :1267-1297 in `{viewMode !== "list" && (…)}`. Option B: add a `date` prop to ScheduleListView that seeds filterFrom and make shiftDate page the range by 30 days in list mode. Owner picks.
- **Test:** RTL: select List mode; assert either the Prev/Next buttons are absent (A) or clicking Next updates the From date input (B).
- **Verifier:** confirmed — client/src/pages/admin/DispatchPageV2.jsx:1476-1486 `{viewMode === "list" && (<ScheduleListView technicians=… onEdit=… onRefresh={() => fetchSchedule(date)} refreshKey=… lastSave=… />)}` passes no date; client/src/components/schedule/ScheduleListView.jsx:39 `export default function ScheduleListView({ technicians = [], onEdit, onRefresh, refreshKey = 0, lastSave = null })`, :81-82 `const [filterFrom, setFilterFrom] = useState(today); const [filterTo, setFilterTo] = useState(thirtyDaysOut);`, :112-113 build the query from those. The nav is unconditional: :1266 comment `{/* Centered date nav — every tab. */}`, :1267-1297 Prev/`{dateHeader}`/Next/Today; :989-993 `shiftDate` → `else setDate(addMonthsISO(date, dir))` for list; :1143-1154 `dateHeader` falls to `formatETDate(dateAtNoonUTC(date), { month: "long", year: "numeric" })` for list. The only `viewMode === "list"` guard in the file is :1476. So in List mode the nav mutates `date`, refetches the day, and changes nothing the operator sees in the table. Whether to hide the nav or make it drive the list's From/To is a design ruling.
- **Needs owner decision**

### F0441 · P2 · Equipment maintenance record and mileage log saves fail silently (console.error only, no error UI in the file)

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/EquipmentMaintenancePage.jsx:1477` (reader states:STATES-03)
- **Reproduction:** Equipment > Maintenance > add record with the API failing; nothing happens on Save.
- **Expected:** A failed save leaves the modal open with a visible error and the operator's data intact.
- **Actual:** The modal stays open with no message; grep -E 'setError\|error &&' over the file returns nothing, so there is no error surface anywhere on the page beyond the success toast (showToast :303).
- **Likely cause:** Page was built before an error-state convention; only the happy path was wired.
- **Fix:** In both modal components add `const [error, setError] = useState("")`; in each `submit` set `setError("")` before the try and `setError(\`Save failed: ${e.message}\`)` in the catch (keep console.error); render `{error && <div style={{ color: D.danger ?? "#B91C1C", fontSize: 13, marginBottom: 8 }}>{error}</div>}` directly above the Save button rows (~:1758 and ~:1963).
- **Test:** RTL: mock fetch to resolve `{ ok: false, status: 500 }`, fill taskName, click Save, assert /Save failed/ is in the document and onDone was not called; then resolve ok and assert onDone fires.
- **Verifier:** downgraded — client/src/pages/admin/EquipmentMaintenancePage.jsx:29-40 `function af(...)` → `.then((r) => { if (!r.ok) throw new Error(\`HTTP ${r.status}\`); return r.json(); })`; :1440-1479 record modal `submit` → `try { await af(…, { method: "POST", … }); onDone(); } catch (e) { console.error(e); } setSaving(false);`; :1799-1822 mileage `submit` identical. Neither modal receives `showToast`, and the file's only error surfaces are the page-level toast (:198 `const showToast`, :303 `showToast("Error resolving alert")`) and the `onDone` success toasts (:929 "Maintenance recorded", :944 "Mileage logged"), so a failed POST re-enables Save with no message. Real. Over-rated: the claim "no error surface anywhere on the page" is wrong (:303), the modal stays open with the operator's form intact (no data loss), and it is a single-operator ops page → P2.

In editMode the same four buttons correctly get `grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4`, which shows the non-edit branch was not updated when Preview was added.

Children: Generate/Regenerate (7055, unconditional), Save draft (7068-7079, `{estimate && …}`), Preview (7080-7092, `{estimate && …}`), Review and send (7093-7102, unconditional, outside any guard). ### F0474 · P2 · Inventory Products tab: load() has no try/catch — a failed fetch leaves 'Loading products...' forever

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/InventoryPage.jsx:1993` (reader admin-ops-pages:OPS-006)
- **Reproduction:** Return 500 from /api/admin/inventory and open /admin/inventory.
- **Expected:** Error state with the message and a retry; loading cleared in finally.
- **Actual:** adminFetch (line 37-48) throws on any non-2xx; the products request has no catch, so setLoading(false) is skipped and the default tab of a 5.8k-line page shows only 'Loading products...' with no toast. The vendors call was hardened for the tech-role 403 (comment at 2004-2006) but the primary request was not.
- **Fix:** InventoryPage.jsx:2001-2012 — wrap the body: `let pData, vData; try { [pData, vData] = await Promise.all([...same two calls...]); setProducts(pData.products \|\| []); setCategories(pData.categories \|\| []); setTotalProducts(pData.total \|\| 0); setVendors(vData.vendors \|\| []); setLoadError(null); } catch (e) { setLoadError(e.message); showToast(`Failed to load products: ${e.message}`); } finally { setLoading(false); }`; add `const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState(null);` next to `loading`, and before the main return render `if (loadError) return (<div style={{ color: D.red, padding: 40, textAlign: "center" }}>Failed to load products: {loadError} <button type="button" style={sBtn(D.teal, D.white)} onClick={() => { setLoading(true); load(); }}>Retry</button></div>);`.
- **Test:** RTL: mock fetch to return 500 for `/admin/inventory?…`; assert 'Loading products...' disappears, the error text and a Retry button render, and clicking Retry re-issues the request.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — InventoryPage.jsx:37-48 `adminFetch` does `if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${r.status}`)`. ProductsTab's `load` (1993-2013) awaits `Promise.all([adminFetch(`/admin/inventory?…`), adminFetch("/admin/inventory/vendors").catch(() => ({ vendors: [] }))])` with no try/catch; `setLoading(false)` at 2012 runs only on success, and `useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);` (2015-2017) swallows nothing, so a non-2xx on the products request leaves `if (loading) return (<div …>Loading products...</div>)` (2071-2076) up forever with an unhandled rejection. Only the vendors leg was hardened (comment 2004-2006). `showToast` is already passed into ProductsTab at 453.

### F0479 · P2 · Native alert()/confirm() dialogs for outcomes — including success — across ops pages

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/LawnAssessmentPanel.jsx:269` (reader admin-ops-pages:OPS-017)
- **Expected:** Overlays use Dialog/Sheet; outcomes surface inline or via the page toast (ProjectsPage already replaced confirm()/prompt() with a Dialog-backed ask(), lines 42-109).
- **Actual:** Blocking browser alerts remain in LawnAssessmentPanel (235, 269, 271, 338, 365, 381 — two of them on SUCCESS, so every field confirm interrupts the tech), EquipmentCalibrationPanel:220/250, CredentialsPage:380/397 (+ window.confirm 388), PushSettings/PushSettingsV2:167/182, PricingLogicPanel:1161/1185/1333, SettingsPage (5), ServiceLibraryPage (4), TimeTrackingPage (4), DocumentRequestsPage:152, DocumentTemplatesPage (1). On the iOS PWA these render as unstyled system sheets.
- **Fix:** Success alerts → the page's existing showToast/inline status; failures → inline error text (Tier 2) or the ui Dialog / an `Alert` (Tier 1); destructive confirms → ui Dialog (Tier 1) or ProjectsPage's ask() pattern (inline copy for Tier 2).
- **Test:** Per page: add a `notice` state rendered inline under the active step; RTL asserts that after a successful confirm no `window.alert` is called (spy) and the inline 'Assessment confirmed' text renders.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — LawnAssessmentPanel.jsx has six native alerts: :235 `alert("Analysis failed: " + e.message);`, :269 `alert("Assessment confirmed.");`, :271 `alert("Confirm failed: " + e.message);`, :338 `alert("Failed to load turf profile: " + e.message);`, :365 `alert("Turf profile saved");`, :381 `alert("Save failed: " + e.message);` — two on success, and the file has no toast or status mechanism at all (grep for showToast/toast returns nothing; imports are only React hooks, lucide, AdminCommandHeader). Spec §3.9 makes confirmations neutral toasts and the contract lists overlays as `Dialog` or `Sheet`; ProjectsPage.jsx:42-60 already has the Dialog-backed `useConfirmDialog`. Sweep counts verified (SettingsPage 5, TimeTrackingPage 4, ServiceLibraryPage 4, PricingLogicPanel 3, CredentialsPage 3, EquipmentCalibrationPanel 2, PushSettingsV2 2, DocumentRequestsPage 1, DocumentTemplatesPage 1). Not autofixable: replacing blocking alerts with inline status/toast is a deliberate behaviour change that needs a per-step UI placement in a file with no status surface, and the contract's one-page-at-a-time migration rule rules out the proposed multi-file sweep. No owner ruling needed — the spec already settles the pattern.

### F0482 · P2 · Lead → Create Estimate drops city/zip: prefill passes raw lead.address instead of formatLeadAddress(lead)

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/LeadsTabs.jsx:276` (reader pipeline-estimator:PE-12)
- **Reproduction:** Create a lead via the call-extraction path with separate city/zip columns → Create Estimate → address field lacks city.
- **Expected:** The estimator's Service address (EstimateToolViewV2 4551-4559, fed into Property Lookup) receives the composed 'street, City, FL zip' string the lead card itself displays.
- **Actual:** Leads written by intake paths that store a street-only address arrive in the estimator as e.g. '123 Palmetto Rd' with city/zip stripped, so Property Lookup geocodes without locality context and the operator re-types information the lead already holds. lead.customer_id is likewise never forwarded (compare createEstimateUrl in the dead OpportunityActions.jsx:19-30, which did pass customerId).
- **Likely cause:** leadEstimateParams predates formatLeadAddress.
- **Fix:** LeadsTabs.jsx:276 — replace `if (lead.address) params.set("address", lead.address);` with `const address = formatLeadAddress(lead); if (address) params.set("address", address);` (formatLeadAddress is defined at :21 in the same file and already guards against double-appending an included city/zip).
- **Test:** Export `leadEstimateParams` for test (or test through the row action) and assert `leadEstimateParams({ id: 'l1', address: '123 Palmetto Rd', city: 'Palmetto', zip: '34221' }).get('address') === '123 Palmetto Rd, Palmetto, 34221'` and that an already-composed address is passed through unchanged.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — LeadsTabs.jsx:276 `if (lead.address) params.set("address", lead.address);` forwards the raw column, while the same file's comment at 15-18 says 'leads.address may hold either a street-only line or a fully composed "street, City, FL zip" string depending on which intake path wrote the row' and the lead card itself displays `formatLeadAddress(lead)` at :1529. Downstream: EstimatesPageV2.jsx:3972 `address: params.get("address") \|\| ""` → :4163 `initialAddress={prefill.address}` → EstimateToolViewV2.jsx:1507/1519 seeds `form.address` verbatim into the 'Service address' input (4551-4557); the Places autocomplete (1268-1270) only sets `componentRestrictions: { country: "us" }`, adding no locality. So a street-only lead reaches the estimator without city/zip. `lead.customer_id` is likewise not forwarded although `readLeadPrefill` reads `customerId` (3971) — that part is a behaviour extension, kept out of the autofix.

### F0488 · P2 · 'Convert to Customer' asks the operator to paste a raw customer UUID

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/LeadsTabs.jsx:4331` (reader pipeline-estimator:PE-02)
- **Reproduction:** Pipeline → Leads → expand a lead → More actions → Convert to Customer.
- **Expected:** Converting a lead links it to a customer by picking one (search by name/phone/email, defaulting to the contact-match the New Lead flow already computes via /admin/leads/contact-matches) or by creating the customer from the lead's own fields — the way POST /admin/leads/:id/schedule-appointment already auto-creates one (admin-leads.js:1334).
- **Actual:** The modal (opened from the lead card at 2149-2158 'Convert to Customer') requires an existing customer UUID typed by hand; the server (admin-leads.js:1183-1186) 400s without it. There is no picker and no create path, so the button is effectively unusable without opening another tab, finding the customer and copying its id from the URL.
- **Likely cause:** Legacy convert modal written before customer search/contact-match endpoints existed; never revisited.
- **Fix:** Replace the UUID field with a customer picker (reuse the estimator's customer search endpoint used at EstimateToolViewV2 4497-4531, seeded from /admin/leads/contact-matches for this lead's phone/email) and offer 'Create customer from this lead' when no match; or hide 'Convert to Customer' in favor of the estimate-accept and schedule-appointment paths that already convert. Owner decision on which path is canonical.
- **Test:** After the ruling: RTL on the convert modal asserting a customer search input (role combobox) replaces the UUID field and that submit posts the selected id; or that the 'Convert to Customer' button is absent.
- **Verifier:** downgraded — The defect is real: LeadsTabs.jsx:4331-4337 `<Input label="Customer ID (required)" value={formData.customer_id} … placeholder="Existing customer UUID" />`, submit at :1157-1162 `if (!formData.customer_id) { alert("Customer ID is required to convert a lead."); …}`, opened from the card's 'Convert to Customer' `<Btn>` at :2149-2158; server admin-leads.js:1182-1186 returns 400 `customer_id is required to convert a lead` and :1190-1191 404 on an unknown id. But it is not P1: two canonical conversion paths already work without a UUID — admin-leads.js:1332-1335 `POST /:id/schedule-appointment` 'Auto-creates (or reuses) a customer from the lead, creates the scheduled service, and marks the lead converted', and the quote flow links `customer_id` (LeadsTabs.jsx:2553 comment) — so the manual modal is a redundant legacy link tool, not a blocker for the daily lead workflow. Which path becomes canonical (customer picker seeded from /admin/leads/contact-matches at :823 / admin-leads.js:843, create-from-lead, or hiding the button) is an owner ruling.
- **Needs owner decision**

### F0502 · P2 · Payers, statements and AR dialogs collapse fetch failures into their empty states

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/PayersPage.jsx:80` (reader billing-admin:BA-11)
- **Reproduction:** Block /admin/payers → 'No payers yet'.
- **Expected:** Contract 'Required states': error with retry, and never present missing metrics as zero/empty.
- **Actual:** A rate-limited or failed request tells the operator there are no payers, no statements, or no outstanding balance — the reassuring answer — with no retry.
- **Likely cause:** Tier-1 migration copied the fetch shape without an error branch.
- **Fix:** PayersPage.jsx load(): add `const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState("")`; before `setPayers` do `if (!r.ok) throw new Error(data?.error \|\| \`HTTP ${r.status}\`)`; in catch `setPayers([]); setLoadError(e.message \|\| "Could not load payers.")`; reset it at the top of load(); in the table (:214) render `loadError ? <TR><TD colSpan={6} className="text-center py-6 text-alert-fg">{loadError} <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={load}>Retry</Button></TD></TR> : payers.length === 0 ? …`. Same shape for loadStatements/loadAr in PayerDetailSheet.jsx:88-108 (render the error line above the Statements/AR panels with a Retry calling refresh) and for PayerArAgingDialog.jsx:38-49 (error line + Retry in DialogBody instead of the empty copy). Genuine load failure is a sanctioned alert-fg use.
- **Test:** RTL for each component: mock adminFetch to resolve `{ ok:false, status:500, json: async () => ({ error: 'boom' }) }` and separately to reject; assert the error text (not 'No payers yet' / 'No statements yet' / 'No outstanding balance') renders and that clicking Retry re-invokes adminFetch.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — client/src/lib/adminFetch.js:13-27 returns the raw fetch Response (no ok check, no throw). PayersPage.jsx:80-93: `const r = await adminFetch(…); const data = await r.json(); setPayers(Array.isArray(data?.payers) ? data.payers : []); } catch { setPayers([]); }` — a 403/500 `{error}` body has no `payers` so it becomes `[]`, and :214-218 renders 'No payers yet. Create one to bill a third party.' (the only `error` state, :76/:390, belongs to the save form). PayerDetailSheet.jsx:88-99 `catch { setStatements([]); }` → :156 'No statements yet…'; :101-108 `setAr(await r.json())` / `catch { setAr(null); }` → ArSummary :451-453 'No outstanding balance.'. PayerArAgingDialog.jsx:38-49 `catch { if (alive) setAr(null); }` → :72-76 'No outstanding payer statements.'. No retry affordance in any of the three loaders. A failed request on a billing surface reads as 'nothing owed' — P2 holds.

### F0508 · P2 · Pricing fetch helpers return error bodies as data (no r.ok check) — margins/logic tables go blank silently

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/PricingLogicPage.jsx:64` (reader admin-ops-pages:OPS-018)
- **Expected:** Non-2xx responses reject so callers show the server's error (every other page's adminFetch does this).
- **Actual:** MarginCalculator (103-121) does `setMargins(data)` with a 403/500 body like `{error:"…"}`; `margins.services` is undefined so the table just disappears with 'Calculate' re-enabled and no message. PricingLogicPanel.jsx:30-38 has the identical helper feeding its save/load paths (a technician's 403 on save reads as success unless the panel checks `d.error`).
- **Fix:** In PricingLogicPanel.jsx:30-38 and PricingLogicPage.jsx:64-72 change the helper to `.then(async (r) => { const body = await r.json().catch(() => ({})); if (!r.ok) throw new Error(body.error \|\| \`HTTP ${r.status}\`); return body; })` (EquipmentCalibrationPanel.jsx:22-33 idiom). Wrap the PricingLogicPanel saves (:1313, :1325, :1768, :1932) in try/catch that surfaces `e.message` via the existing toast (:620 pattern `setToast({ kind: "error", msg })`), skips `onUpdate`, and reverts the optimistic `setTracks` at :1766; in MarginCalculator :117-119 set `setMargins({ error: e.message })` instead of null so the existing :490 branch shows it.
- **Test:** RTL: mock PUT /admin/pricing-config/:key → 400 `{ error: 'bad value' }`; edit a value in PricingLogicPanel and assert the error toast renders and onUpdate is not called; mock POST margin-check → 500 `{ error }` and assert the message renders in MarginCalculator.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — Helper claim holds: client/src/pages/admin/PricingLogicPage.jsx:64-72 `const af = (p, o = {}) => fetch(…).then((r) => r.json());` and client/src/components/admin/PricingLogicPanel.jsx:30-38 identical — neither checks r.ok. BUT the cited MarginCalculator symptom is wrong: PricingLogicPage.jsx:490-494 `{margins?.error && (<div style={{ color: D.red, fontSize: 12, padding: 10 }}>{margins.error}</div>)}` renders the error body, and every auth/role failure is `{ error: '…' }` (server/middleware/admin-auth.js:29-68; router-level `router.use(adminAuthenticate, requireAdmin)` at server/routes/admin-pricing-config.js:15), so a 403/500 shows a message rather than a blank card (only a non-JSON body reaches `catch { setMargins(null) }` at :117-119). The real, un-cited consequence is in PricingLogicPanel: :1313-1318 `await af(PUT /admin/pricing-config/${key})` then `onUpdate(config.config_key, updated)` unconditionally; :1325-1331 handleRawSave same; :1766-1774 optimistic `setTracks(...)` then `await af(PUT lawn-brackets)` with no check; :1932 discount-rules same — while the routes reject with `{error}` (admin-pricing-config.js:745-775, :841-862, :1312 `if (validationError) return res.status(400).json({ error: validationError })`). A validation-rejected pricing edit therefore reads as saved in the UI (technician 403s are already fenced client-side by canEditPricing, PricingLogicPanel.jsx:10/1255). Scope corrected; P2 stands for silent failed saves on DB-authoritative pricing.

### F0543 · P2 · Tax 'Add Expense → Save' has no in-flight guard — a double click records the expense twice

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/TaxPage.jsx:1006` (reader billing-admin:BA-15)
- **Reproduction:** Taxes → Expenses → + Add Expense → fill → double-click Save → two identical rows.
- **Expected:** Money-recording writes are single-flight (AdminInvoicesPage uses `rowActionBusy`/`saving`; PayerDetailSheet uses `busy`).
- **Actual:** Two rapid taps (common on iOS) issue two POSTs before the form closes, creating duplicate expense rows that then feed the P&L and Schedule C export.
- **Likely cause:** Missing saving state.
- **Fix:** TaxPage.jsx ExpensesTab: `const [savingExpense, setSavingExpense] = useState(false);` at the top; in handleAdd add `if (savingExpense) return; setSavingExpense(true);` before the fetch and `finally { setSavingExpense(false); }`; on the Save button (1199) add `disabled={savingExpense}` and `opacity: savingExpense ? 0.6 : 1`, label `{savingExpense ? "Saving…" : "Save"}`.
- **Test:** RTL: mock fetch with a deferred promise; fill description/amount/date; click Save twice before resolving; assert fetch called once with POST /admin/tax/expenses and the button is disabled while pending.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — client/src/pages/admin/TaxPage.jsx:1006-1024 `const handleAdd = async () => { if (!form.description \|\| !form.amount \|\| !form.expenseDate) return; try { await adminFetch("/admin/tax/expenses", { method: "POST", … }); setShowAdd(false); … load(); } catch (e) { alert("Failed: " + e.message); } };` — no in-flight flag; the Save button at 1199-1213 `<button onClick={handleAdd} style={{…}}>Save</button>` has no `disabled`. Server side, server/routes/admin-tax.js:472-531 `router.post('/expenses', …)` is a plain `db('expenses').insert({…}).returning('*')` (line 514-519) with no idempotency key or duplicate check, and when no categoryId is chosen it first awaits `autoCategorizeExpense(vendorName, description, amount)` (483-491) — an LLM round-trip that makes the double-tap window seconds wide. Two clicks = two rows in P&L / Schedule C.

### F0549 · P2 · Tax overview renders nothing while loading and stays blank forever if /admin/tax/dashboard fails; 15 swallowed catches on the page

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/TaxPage.jsx:4807` (reader billing-admin:BA-16)
- **Reproduction:** Block /admin/tax/dashboard and open /admin/tax.
- **Expected:** Loading without layout collapse; error with retry.
- **Actual:** Below the header the Overview tab is empty during the fetch and permanently empty on any failure; the Expenses/Rates/Taxability tabs likewise show an empty list with no message on failure.
- **Likely cause:** Early page; no-op catches.
- **Fix:** TaxPage.jsx TaxPage(): add `const [dashboardStatus, setDashboardStatus] = useState("loading")` and `const [dashboardError, setDashboardError] = useState(null)`; wrap the three overview fetches in a `loadOverview` useCallback that sets 'ready' / 'error' (message from adminFetch); render `{activeTab === "overview" && dashboardStatus === "loading" && <div style={{ padding: 40, textAlign: "center", color: D.muted }}>Loading tax overview…</div>}` and an error block with a Retry button (same shape as AccountsReceivableTab's loadError block at ~3793) ahead of the `{d && …}` block. Leave the per-tab catches to a follow-up unless the same one-line error state is added to each tab's load.
- **Test:** RTL: mock /admin/tax/dashboard to reject; render TaxPage; assert an error message + Retry button on the Overview tab; resolve on retry and assert 'Tax Collected YTD' renders. Also assert 'Loading tax overview…' appears before the promise resolves.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — client/src/pages/admin/TaxPage.jsx:4808-4813 `adminFetch("/admin/tax/dashboard").then(setDashboard).catch(() => {}); adminFetch("/admin/tax/pnl?period=mtd").then(setQuickPnl).catch(() => {}); adminFetch("/admin/tax/accounts-receivable").then((d) => setArSummary(d?.summary)).catch(() => {});` then 4881 `{d && activeTab === "overview" && (` — grep for `!d &&\|!dashboard\|dashboardError\|dashboardStatus` returns nothing, so the Overview body is empty during the fetch and forever after a failure (only the AdminCommandHeader renders). Counts hold: `grep -c 'catch(() => {})'` = 15, plus 3 bare `catch {}` (e.g. ExpensesTab load at 990-1000). Contrast: AccountsReceivableTab in the same file (3745-3763) already keeps `loading` + `loadError` with the comment 'A failed load must never render as "$0.00 / All caught up!"' — the in-file pattern to mirror.

### F0550 · P2 · Staff (TimeTracking) tabs live only in local state — no deep link, no back/forward, no usage beacon

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/TimeTrackingPage.jsx:175` (reader admin-ops-pages:OPS-008)
- **Reproduction:** Open /admin/timetracking, click Time → Approvals, press browser refresh — Dashboard renders.

Refreshing /admin/timetracking while on Approvals returns to Dashboard; nothing can link to ?tab=approvals (e.g. from an alert); the usage report records only the route. Same gap in PricingStrategyPage (tab useState at line 89, though it is hub-embedded).
- **Fix:** TimeTrackingPage.jsx: import useSearchParams + useRenderedTabBeacon; seed `useState(() => normalizeTab(searchParams.get("tab")))` where normalizeTab returns the value if it is in some group's tabs else 'dashboard'; add `selectLeaf(next)` that sets state and writes `?tab=` (delete the param for 'dashboard') with `{ replace: true }`; sync effect on [searchParams]; `useRenderedTabBeacon("/admin/timetracking", tab, [searchParams])`; add 'timetracking' to SELF_REPORTING_PAGES in lib/adminUsage.js:67 and its membership test. Guard the active-re-click like Equipment. (Separately, ApprovalsTab ignoring `weekStart` from the same cron link is outside this finding.)
- **Test:** RTL with MemoryRouter initialEntries ['/admin/timetracking?tab=approvals']: assert ApprovalsTab content renders; click 'Team' and assert the URL search becomes ?tab=team; invalid ?tab=typo renders Dashboard and the beacon receives 'dashboard'. Extend the adminUsage lib membership test for 'timetracking'.
