Route is `<Route path="timetracking" …>` (App.jsx:651). The gap already bites a real producer: server/services/time-tracking-crons.js:95-101 builds `const link = \`${portalBase}/admin/timetracking?tab=approvals&weekStart=${weekStartStr}\`` and texts it to the office weekly (`TwilioService.sendSMS(ADMIN_ALERT_PHONE, \`📋 ${pendingCount} tech timesheet… Review: ${link}\``) — that link lands on the Dashboard tab because `?tab=` is never read. Canonical pattern verified at EquipmentPage.jsx:211-258 (`useSearchParams`, `useState(() => normalizeForRole(searchParams.get("tab")))`, `selectLeaf` writing back with `{ replace: true }`, sync effect, `useRenderedTabBeacon("/admin/equipment", tab, [searchParams])`). Beacon caveat: docs/design/DECISIONS.md rounds 9/17 deliberately scope the hook to URL-driven tabs — so the beacon is owed only once ?tab= is adopted, and TT must then join SELF_REPORTING_PAGES (client/src/lib/adminUsage.js:67-84, membership locked by the lib test).

### F0558 · P2 · Turf height 'Confirm reading' silently does nothing when the PATCH fails

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/TurfHeightReviewPage.jsx:55` (reader admin-ops-pages:OPS-007)
- **Expected:** A failed action surfaces an error message; the button either recovers with feedback or the row shows why it stayed.
- **Actual:** On a 4xx/5xx the row simply stays and the button re-enables with no message — the operator cannot tell whether the click registered. A thrown network error also propagates unhandled (no catch). The `error` state that exists for load is never set here.
- **Fix:** Replace :62 with `if (!r.ok) { const b = await r.json().catch(() => ({})); setError(b.error \|\| `Could not confirm reading (HTTP ${r.status})`); return; } setError(null); setItems((prev) => prev.filter((it) => it.id !== id));` and add `catch { setError("Could not confirm reading — check your connection and try again."); }` before the `finally`. The existing `{error && <div style={{ color: M.red }}>{error}</div>}` at :80 renders it; add `role="alert"` to that div.
- **Test:** RTL: mock fetch so GET returns one item and PATCH returns {ok:false,status:500}; click "Confirm reading"; assert an alert-role message appears and the item row remains; second case: PATCH rejects → same message, no unhandled rejection.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — TurfHeightReviewPage.jsx:55-66: `async function resolve(id) { setResolving(id); try { const r = await adminFetch(`/admin/turf-height/${id}/resolve`, { method: "PATCH", … }); if (r.ok) setItems((prev) => prev.filter((it) => it.id !== id)); } finally { setResolving(null); } }` — no else branch and no catch. The `error` state declared at :41 is only written by `load` (:48-49). A 4xx/5xx therefore re-enables the button (:112-114 `disabled={resolving === it.id}` / "…") with the row still present and no message; a rejected fetch escapes the `onClick={() => resolve(it.id)}` handler at :112 as an unhandled promise rejection.

### F0570 · P2 · Negative adjusted-EBITDA renders as "$-1,234" instead of "−$1,234"

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/dashboard/EbitdaBridgeCard.jsx:46` (reader dashboard:DASH-02)
- **Reproduction:** Enter operating costs larger than month-to-date contribution (Settings → Financials → Operating Costs) or view early in a month; the 'Adjusted EBITDA' result row shows `$-N`.
- **Expected:** A negative `result` row (adjusted EBITDA or Contribution below zero — explicitly anticipated by `r.amount < 0 ? { bar: "#C8312F", text: "text-alert-fg" }` at line 26-27) prints with the same true-minus convention as the rest of the card: `−$1,234`.
- **Actual:** `fmtMoney(-1234)` returns `$-1,234` (dollar sign before the hyphen-minus) for `start`/`subtotal`/`result` rows — the one row that carries the alert tone is the one with the malformed number. MrrBridgeCard.jsx:57 already handles this correctly (`amount < 0 ? `−${fmtMoney(Math.abs(amount))}` : fmtMoney(amount)`).
- **Likely cause:** fmtMoney never handles sign; the minus branch was only written for `kind === 'minus'` rows.
- **Fix:** EbitdaBridgeCard.jsx:46 → `{r.amount < 0 ? `−${fmtMoney(Math.abs(r.amount))}` : fmtMoney(r.amount)}` (a zero-amount minus row then renders `$0` instead of `−$0`, which is correct). Do not change fmtMoney's contract.
- **Test:** RTL render `<EbitdaBridgeCard bridge={{ revenue: 1000, rows: [{key:'revenue',label:'Revenue',kind:'start',amount:1000},{key:'cogs',label:'COGS',kind:'minus',amount:-1500},{key:'ebitda',label:'Adjusted EBITDA',kind:'result',amount:-1234}] }} />`; expect text to contain `−$1,234` and `−$1,500` and never `$-`.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — EbitdaBridgeCard.jsx:46 `{r.kind === "minus" ? `−${fmtMoney(Math.abs(r.amount))}` : fmtMoney(r.amount)}`; charts.jsx:66-71 `export function fmtMoney(n, opts = {}) { const v = Number(n \|\| 0); return '$' + v.toLocaleString(…) }` has no sign handling, so `fmtMoney(-1234)` → `$-1,234`. The server emits negative non-minus rows: ebitda-bridge.js:111 `gross_profit … kind: 'subtotal'`, :116-118 `contribution … kind: overheadEntered ? 'subtotal' : 'result'`, :125 `ebitda … kind: 'result'`, and the card anticipates it at :26-27 `r.amount < 0 ? { bar: "#C8312F", text: "text-alert-fg" }`. Minus rows are emitted negative (`amount: -cogs` :110, `-marketingTotal` :112, `-overheadTotal` :124), so a unified sign test keeps them at `−$N`. MrrBridgeCard.jsx:57 already does `amount < 0 ? `−${fmtMoney(Math.abs(amount))}` : fmtMoney(amount)`.

### F0578 · P2 · Email composer sends unescaped text as HTML — '<' in a message breaks the email

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/admin/email/useEmailEditor.js:59` (reader communications:COMM-08)
- **Reproduction:** Reply to any email with the text `a < b` and view the received message.
- **Expected:** Plain text typed in the composer/reply box arrives verbatim; angle brackets and ampersands are escaped before the newline->br conversion.
- **Actual:** The client only swaps newlines for <br>; server sendMessage (gmail-client.js:265-293) wraps the body as text/html with no escaping, so 'price <100 and >50' loses the bracketed text in the recipient's mail client and any pasted markup is rendered as HTML.
- **Likely cause:** Composer treats the textarea as HTML source.
- **Fix:** In useEmailEditor.js add `const escapeHtml = (s) => String(s).replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;").replace(/"/g, "&quot;");` (or export `encodeEmailBody`) and change :59 to `body: escapeHtml(payload.body).replace(/\n/g, "<br>")`.
- **Test:** Unit test of the encoder: `'price <100 & >50\nthanks'` → `'price &lt;100 &amp; &gt;50<br>thanks'`; hook test: handleReply with that text posts the encoded body to /api/admin/email/send.
- **Verifier:** confirmed — useEmailEditor.js:55-61 posts `body: payload.body.replace(/\n/g, "<br>")` — the only transformation; admin-email.js:322-327 `router.post('/send' … const { to, subject, body, threadId, inReplyTo } = req.body; … gmailClient.sendMessage(to, subject \|\| '(no subject)', body, threadId, inReplyTo)` passes it through untouched; gmail-client.js:279 sets `'Content-Type: text/html; charset=utf-8'` and :287 concatenates the raw `body`. Both inputs are plain textareas — EmailComposer.jsx:351 `<textarea id="email-compose-body" …>` and EmailReply.jsx:29 `<textarea aria-label="Reply" …>` — and the AI draft (email-tools.js:300 `draftEmailReply` → `reply_draft: draft`) is prompt text, not HTML. So `a < b` is parsed as a tag start and a bare `&` as an entity. The fix is client-side encoding; the server contract is unchanged and no message is sent by the change itself.

### F0591 · P2 · Next Stop action row packs up to six ~34px-tall, 12px-label buttons in one non-wrapping row; the next action is not visually singular

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/tech/TechHomePage.jsx:1689` (reader tech-portal:TECH-03)
- **Reproduction:** Open /tech with a pending stop; mark En Route; observe four buttons + On Site in one row at 375px width.
- **Expected:** Field priorities: tap targets >= 44px, one obvious next action ('En Route' → 'On Site'), secondary actions demoted.
- **Actual:** Lines 725-757 render Navigate, Protocol, Quick Move, En Route (primary), plus On Site (primary, when en_route) and Sync Stop (primary, when out of sync) in a single `display:flex, gap:8` row with no wrap. On a 375px phone inside the 480px column each button is ~50px wide and ~34px tall (8px padding + 14px icon line) with a 12px label; 'Quick Move' and 'En Route' truncate/wrap awkwardly. Two or three teal primaries can be on screen at once, so the 'one primary action per view' idea is lost exactly where glanceability matters most.
- **Likely cause:** Buttons were appended one at a time (Sync Stop, On Site) to a row designed for three.
- **Fix:** Give ActionBtn minHeight: 44 and fontSize 14, add flexWrap: 'wrap' with a minWidth per button, and render the single status-advancing CTA (En Route → On Site → Sync Stop) full-width above a secondary row (Navigate / Protocol / Quick Move) so only one primary exists at a time.
- **Test:** If ruled in: render the Next Stop card at status 'en_route' and assert exactly one ActionBtn has the primary background.
- **Verifier:** downgraded — Facts hold: TechHomePage.jsx:725-757 renders Navigate, Protocol, Quick Move, En Route (primary) plus On Site (primary, `ON_SITE_ELIGIBLE = new Set(['en_route'])` :196) and Sync Stop (primary, `stopOutOfSync` :403) inside `<div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>` with no wrap; `EN_ROUTE_ELIGIBLE` (:195) excludes 'en_route', so En Route stays rendered (disabled, teal at 0.6) beside the On Site primary; ActionBtn (1689-1698) is `padding: '8px 4px' … fontSize: 12`. But the fix is a Tech Home restructure, which is a standing documented deferral: docs/design/DECISIONS.md:62-70 'Tech Home (`/tech`) will not be touched … Revisit if a tech reports specific frustration', and waves-portal-ui-redesign-spec.md:436 '`/tech` (Tech Home): DO NOT TOUCH'. The 'one primary action per view' rule cited is the admin spec (spec:51). Real ergonomics concern, no field report → P2 pending the owner's call.
- **Needs owner decision**

### F0597 · P2 · In-brief service action buttons (Report / Photos / Zone / 🚩) are ~32px tall with 12px labels; the flag button is icon-only

- **Location:** `client/src/pages/tech/VisitBriefPanel.jsx:467` (reader tech-portal:TECH-04)
- **Reproduction:** Expand any stop in Today's Services and measure the Actions row.
- **Expected:** The per-visit actions that create the customer-facing report and attach photos should be >= 44px tall with >= 14px labels, and every action should carry a visible label.
- **Actual:** 8px vertical padding + a 12px line yields ~32px buttons; '🚩' has no visible text so a tech cannot tell what it does without tapping. These are the report-creation and photo entry points for every stop.
- **Likely cause:** Moved verbatim from the old ServiceRow (per the comment at 463-465) without revisiting sizing.
- **Fix:** minHeight: 44, fontSize: 14, and label the flag button ('🚩 Opportunity'); allow flexWrap (already set).
- **Test:** If ruled in: VisitBriefPanel.test.jsx asserts the flag button has non-empty visible text and each action button's inline minHeight is 44.
- **Verifier:** downgraded — Measurements hold: VisitBriefPanel.jsx:467-471 `const btn = { padding: '8px 10px', borderRadius: 6, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600, border: … }` (≈32px tall at 12px), and :495 `<button onClick={() => onLead(service)} aria-label="Flag opportunity" style={{ ...btn, color: DARK.amber }}>🚩</button>` has no visible label. But the comment at 463-465 says these were 'moved verbatim from the old ServiceRow (labels + terminal logic + trace-eligibility guard unchanged)' — a deliberate preservation — and docs/design/DECISIONS.md:62-70 / spec:436 defer any Tech Home restyling to specific field feedback; the visible label copy is also a copy ruling. Real ergonomics gap (DECISIONS.md:65 itself names 'big tap targets'), no report → P2 pending the owner.
- **Needs owner decision**

### F0606 · P2 · `text-10` / `text-15` / `text-20` / `text-24` are not Tailwind tokens — the classes emit no CSS and silently inherit the parent size

- **Location:** `client/tailwind.config.js:67` (reader tables-lists:TL-01)
- **Reproduction:** Open /admin/estimates → any estimate → Audit; inspect the grid header row: computed font-size is inherited (not 10px) and no `.text-10` rule exists in the built stylesheet.
- **Expected:** Every `text-N` size class used in admin resolves to a theme token from the contract scale (22/18/14/13/11); table header labels render at 11px.
- **Actual:** 86 `text-10`, 22 `text-15`, 13 `text-20`, 5 `text-24` sites reference sizes absent from `theme.extend.fontSize` (no plugins, presets, safelist, or `.text-10` utility in any src CSS), so the rule is never generated. The EstimatesPageV2 audit grid header (1189), the PipelineAnalytics grid header (672), the ScheduleListView filter labels (383), DocumentRequestsPage delivery chips (316-326) and CallLogTabV2 `md:text-10` (1452) all render at whatever the parent size is (often 13-14px in a container meant for a small uppercase label, or 12px text meant to be 15px in ScheduleCustomerSidebar 329-497).
- **Likely cause:** Authors assumed the numeric fontSize scale was continuous; the theme only defines eight steps and nothing lints for unknown utilities.
- **Fix:** Map each undefined class to the nearest contract token: `text-10`→`text-11`, `text-15`→`text-14` (or `text-16` where it labels a mobile primary), `text-20`→`text-22`, `text-24`→`text-22`/`text-28`. Add an ESLint/`check:domain-rules` grep that fails on `\btext-(9\|10\|15\|17\|20\|24)\b`. Do NOT add a `10` token — 10px is below the admin floor.
- **Test:** Static test: collect every `\btext-(\d+)\b` in client/src and assert each N is a key of tailwind.config.js theme.extend.fontSize; add the same grep to check:domain-rules.
- **Verifier:** downgraded — Bug is real: client/tailwind.config.js:67-76 `fontSize` extends only '11','12','13','14','16','18','22','28', `plugins: []` (:98), no safelist/presets, postcss.config.js plain tailwindcss 3.4 (no numeric default scale), and no `.text-10\|15\|20\|24` rule in any client/src .css. Usage counts match exactly: text-10 ×86, text-15 ×22, text-20 ×13, text-24 ×5 (e.g. EstimatesPageV2.jsx:1189 `bg-zinc-50 text-10 uppercase tracking-label`, ScheduleListView.jsx:383 `text-10 uppercase`, ScheduleCustomerSidebar.jsx:329/337/351 `text-15`), so the classes emit nothing and the parent size is inherited. Downgraded to P2: it is a silent cosmetic mis-size, nothing functional. `text-10` → `text-11` is settled by the contract (admin-ui-consistency-contract.md:28 caption 11px); `text-15/20/24` have no contract step (contract 14/13/11, theme 16/18/22/28), so their mapping is a design ruling, and 126 visual changes need ui-verify, not an autofix.
- **Needs owner decision**

### F0613 · P2 · SMS-guard admin alert links to unmounted /admin/sms-templates (lands on customer login)

- **Location:** `server/services/twilio.js:153` (reader navigation-map:NAV-02)
- **Reproduction:** Trigger an SMS guard block, open the bell, click the alert.
- **Expected:** The alert link opens the SMS templates workspace (Communications → Templates, i.e. /admin/communications#tab=templates).
- **Actual:** No /admin/sms-templates route exists in App.jsx (no child route, no redirect). The URL falls through to the `/*` catch-all → ProtectedRoute → customer /login?next=/admin/sms-templates. NotificationBell navigates with window.location.href (NotificationBell.jsx:462), so the admin is hard-reloaded into the customer login screen.
- **Likely cause:** Link string never updated when the standalone templates page was folded into Communications.
- **Fix:** server/services/twilio.js:153 `link: "/admin/sms-templates",` → `link: "/admin/communications#tab=templates",`.
- **Test:** Extend server/tests/twilio-internal-alert-redirect.test.js: notifySmsGuardBlocked calls triggerNotification with `link: '/admin/communications#tab=templates'`; longer term mirror client/src/config/adminNavigation.test.js:108 ('only links to mounted admin routes') over the link literals in server/services/notification-triggers.js and twilio.js.
- **Verifier:** downgraded — server/services/twilio.js:153 `link: "/admin/sms-templates"` (inside notifySmsGuardBlocked → triggerNotification('internal_admin_alert')). grep for 'sms-templates' in client/src and server finds only API paths (`adminFetch('/admin/sms-templates…')` in CommunicationsPage/CommunicationsTabsV2/NotificationEventsTabV2) — App.jsx has no `sms-templates` child under `<Route path="/admin">` (552-676) and no admin wildcard, so the URL falls to `<Route path="/*" element={<ProtectedRoute><PortalPage/>…` (678-685) whose unauthenticated branch is `<Navigate to={`/login?next=${encodeURIComponent(next)}`} replace />` (ProtectedRoute, App.jsx ~495) — the customer login. NotificationBell.jsx:462 navigates with `window.location.href = n.link`. The target exists: CommunicationsPageV2.jsx:233 `key: "templates"` and the hash reader at 3165-3180 handles `#tab=templates`. Downgraded to P2: the alert body is still readable in the bell and the admin can navigate back; it is a broken deep link, not a blocker.

## F. Accessibility report (verified, ranked)

Ranked by severity. The census counted 0 icon-only buttons without a name on 44 of 45 admin routes (Customer 360 has 1) and 1 dialog without a role (Customer 360).

## K. Remaining work, ranked

### K.1 Verified, autofixable, queued as PR-sized units (151 findings)

151 verified P1/P2 findings carry `safe_to_autofix` after verification; 84 of them are named in the seven ranked units below, and the remaining 67 are enumerated in K.1.8 by file so each has a unit and an owner (the sweep PR for its file family).

Units are ordered by user impact × frequency; each is one PR with its own tests.

1. **Schedule & dispatch mobile fixes** — `MobileServiceEditModal` rewrites `service_type` on every save (F0195, P1: appends "— Billed Monthly" to any type without a cadence keyword); `ServiceOutlineComposerModal` at `z-[70]` under the mobile bars (F0106, P1); `InlineTechPicker` swallows a failed assignment and closes as if it succeeded (F0171); `CreateAppointmentModal` sets `saving` only after an awaited re-quote (F0166); `MarkPrepaidModal` safe-area + max-height (F0176/F0177); checkout line items rounded to whole dollars (F0186); sortable headers as buttons with `aria-sort` (F0203); DispatchPageV2 stats row loses its spaces (F0431); `EstimatesPageV2` row-actions sheet ignores the home-indicator inset (F0456).
2. **Money-adjacent hardening in Invoices / Tax / Banking** — reverse-prepaid has no busy guard (F0342, two money-moving POSTs on a double click); Tax "Add expense" double-submit (F0543); A/R "Total Outstanding" white on white when ≤ $500 (F0547, P1); overdue check in browser-local time (F0343); invoice list refetch per keystroke with no latest-request guard (F0341); the invoices toast prefixes failures with a green "OK" and has no `aria-live` (F0340); negative EBITDA rendered "$-1,234" (F0570); Tax overview blank forever on failure (F0549); pricing / revenue / CSR panels treat error bodies as data (F0508, F0127); `RecapCapture` white on white (F0532, P1).
3. **Responsive & safe-area sweep** — 12 module-level `isMobile` snapshots → `useIsMobile` (F0442, F0542); `sTableWrap` `overflow: hidden` → auto (F0405); unwrapped tables in TimeTracking ×4, ToolHealth, EmailAutomations (F0552, F0555, F0438); portal Request overlay header under the notch (F0309, P1); Geofence prompt, tech bottom sheets, recap sheet z-order (F0219, F0042, F0223, F0589, F0594); admin NotificationBell panel offsets (F0034); cookie bar (F0107/F0108); estimator 3-column form rows (F0452); Triage action row (F0557); 100vh inside padded shells (F0331, F0592).
4. **Accessibility quick wins** — Settings toggles → `role="switch"` + keyboard (F0534, P1); PayerDetailSheet / PhotoAssessmentDetailSheet close control (F0500, P1); `AdminLayoutV2` `<main>` + skip link + drawer focus trap (F0019/F0014); CallRoutingSettings switch names (F0049); IB input name + live region (F0090/F0091); PublicBooking address labels (F0319); MobileNewCustomerSheet labels (F0095); Newsletter icon button (F0492); clickable divs → buttons in Inventory / ToolHealth / CreateAppointment search (F0472, F0554, F0168); nested interactive in the invoice row (F0345); `ui/Tabs` arrow keys + `aria-controls` (F0238); pay-page surcharge live region (F0276); estimate modals Escape + scroll lock (F0270, F0273).
5. **Navigation & token hygiene** — `/admin` index redirect without `replace` traps Back (F0008; F0009 was the same report from a second reader and is folded in); `/admin/*` wildcard so unknown staff URLs stop landing on the customer login (F0013); SMS-guard alert links to unmounted `/admin/sms-templates` (F0613); TimeTracking tabs into the URL (F0550); `Badge` unknown tones (F0227/F0410 — 14 call sites render unstyled); undefined `text-10/15/20/24` classes (F0200/F0366/F0402/F0450); admin raw `<a href>` full reloads (F0565); Email search debounce (F0579); email composer sends unescaped `<` as HTML (F0578).
6. **States & silent failures** — Equipment maintenance / mileage saves (F0441), Compliance "Loading forever" (F0406), Inventory products load (F0474), Payers empty-on-error (F0502), Turf height confirm (F0558), portal property switch (F0314), Decline/Follow-up/Extend dialogs masking server errors (F0076).
7. **Typography below the caption floor (9–10px)** — the unambiguous sites only: Banking/Tax/Inventory 9–10px headers (F0373), PPC capacity cards (F0357), GBP badges (F0470), Social chart labels (F0540), palette 10px footer (F0086), ReviewVelocity 10–11px (F0517), grid chips 8–9px (F0212). The 11–13px population waits on ruling K.2-1.

### K.2 Needs an owner ruling before code moves (45 findings)

The most consequential first; the full table follows.

1. **Which text floor governs Tier 1 admin: the consistency contract's 13/12/11 scale or the 14px hard line** (F0228 and ~3,500 sites). Every reader hit this. Until ruled, sweeps stop at 10px.
2. **Customer 360 IA**: keep or delete the Health Radar (real sub-scores exist, F0062); "Add Note" — build the note composer or remove the two entries (F0065); reuse `CustomerSmsPanel` in the Comms tab (F0071); collapse the seven identical header pills (F0064); the duplicated mobile bars (F0073).
3. **Tax A/R "Send Reminder"** posts to a route that does not exist (F0544, P1): remove it, or route through the invoice follow-up sequence (which owns stop-on-payment and quiet hours).
4. **"Charge card on file" from Invoices** charges off-session with one tap, no amount, no confirmation (F0348, P1): add a quote → confirm step, or keep one-tap for field speed.
5. **Pipeline**: keep or delete the unrouted Unified Pipeline directory (2,660 lines); does "Convert to Customer" survive; which lead-close path is canonical; give the mobile estimates list the desktop actions (F0467, P1).
6. **Customer pills** after the 2026-09-04 "no status chips" ruling: hero/re-entry status pills (F0326), six V2 report folders (F0153), weather/score/TrendChip (CRF-19).
7. **Dead code deletion** (37 unreachable files, ~12,800 lines + 2 dead-import panels) and the reachability test that stops it recurring.
8. **Palette folds**: PricingLogicPanel / PushSettings / MileageTab / JobFormSection / GlobalCommandPalette slate-teal → zinc (F0102, F0080); AutonomousContentReview forest-green hero (AM-01); dispatch grid `#3B82F6` blocks; dashboard `.dashboard-blackout` override.
9. **Intelligence Bar chrome**: one implementation (F0079/F0080) and which follow-up pattern survives.
10. **ServiceOutlinePage** as a Tailwind page on a customer glass surface (F0332, P1): sanctioned third style or theme-doc migration.
11. **ContentCalendar** phone layout (F0408, P1) and the marketing sub-tab / duplicated-dashboard IA.
12. **Invented visit descriptions** in the portal Visits tab (F0288, P1): real copy from the schedule payload, or none.
13. **NewsletterPage** mixed Tier 1 + inline leaf row (F0493, P1).
14. **BrandButton secondary** = 40px choice chip or 48px action; the shared money formatter's null semantics; CardPage dark glass; the mobile cash numpad (decorative today).

Breakpoint disagreement: client/src/components/admin/Customer360ProfileV2.jsx:5917 `@media (max-width: 768px) {` (inclusive) shows `.c360-mobile-actionbar`, `.c360-header-mobile`, `.c360-mobile-footer-spacer` and hides `.c360-header-desktop` (:5922-5925); client/src/components/admin/StickyActionBar.jsx:52 `"md:hidden fixed left-0 right-0 …"` hides the bottom bar at ≥768; client/src/hooks/useIsMobile.js:5 `window.innerWidth < breakpoint` (768) is what pages/admin/CustomersPageV2.jsx:1063 uses. Duplication: the top bar :8085-8100 renders `<a href={`/admin/communications?phone=…&action=sms`}>Text</a>` and `<CallBridgeLink …>Call</CallBridgeLink>`; the bottom bar rendered at :8177 `<CustomerActionBar … standalone />` builds `{ key: "text", … href: `/admin/communications?phone=…&action=sms` }` and `{ key: "call", … callViaBridge }` (StickyActionBar.jsx:137-150). Worse than the finding states: at exactly 768px the desktop header (the only home of the Book Appt / Invoice / Prepay Invoice pills, ~:6102-6117) is hidden AND the bottom bar (Text/Call/Estimate/Book/Invoice) is hidden, while the mobile header (:6137-6260) and the ⋯ menu (:8112-8176: Edit customer / Send prepay invoice / Record collected prepay / Add note) carry none of Book/Estimate/Invoice — so those actions are unreachable at that width and the footer spacer reserves space for a bar that is not there. The inline comment :8059-8062 chose the inclusive boundary on purpose ('no Tailwind md:hidden, which would blank it at exactly 768px / iPad portrait'); its premise only holds because the desktop header is also hidden ≤768 — moving both rules to 767.98px gives the desktop header at 768, consistent with `md` and useIsMobile. Which bar owns Text/Call on phones is a design ruling, so the bundle is not autofixable. P2 stands (one exact width; the duplication itself is cosmetic). |

Real maintainability debt, P2. |

| F0175 | P2 | `client/src/components/schedule/MarkPrepaidModal.jsx:202` | Schedule/Dispatch V2 overlay family is hand-rolled without focus trap, Escape, focus return or scroll lock; most lack role=dialog | Facts hold. MarkPrepaidModal.jsx:202-211 `return createPortal(<div onClick={dismiss} className="fixed inset-0 z-[1200] flex items-end md:items-center justify-center" …><div onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()} className="w-full bg-white rounded-2xl" style={{ maxWidth: 480, padding: 20 }}>` — no role, no ref, and the file has no keydown/useModalFocus/body-lock at all; PrepaySwitchSheet.jsx:107-116 `function Shell({ children }) { return createPortal(<div className="fixed inset-0 z-[1200] …">` no role; RescheduleConfirmModal.jsx:175-178 `role="dialog" aria-modal="true"` but no useModalFocus/Escape/labelledby; RainOutSheet.jsx:645-651 role + aria-label, no trap. My per-file scan of components/schedule confirms Escape=0 useModalFocus=0 bodyLock=0 for AnnualPrepayLauncher, MarkPrepaidModal, PrepaySwitchSheet, RainOutSheet, RescheduleConfirmModal, TreatmentPlanPanel and every Mobile*Sheet except MobileCustomerDetailSheet (:75 Escape, :113 role) — CompletionPricingCard and ScheduleCustomerSidebar also have Escape+role, a minor overcount in the finding. ui/Dialog.jsx:26 `useModalFocus(open, onClose)` + :41-43 body lock and ui/Sheet.jsx:9-16 are the canonical shells; contract line 76 `Overlays: Dialog or Sheet`. Why P2 not P1: the contract is `Active for new and migrated /admin/* work` (line 3) and mandates `one page or shared component at a time` (line 7), so un-migrated hand-rolled overlays are declared migration backlog rather than a shipped defect; every overlay has an explicit 44px close control; the two operators are touch/mouse users. Real, systemic, but not a functional blocker. The fix is an 18-file design-system migration whose canonical shell (Dialog's max-sm mode vs a new MobileSheet vs Sheet) is an owner/design ruling. |

MobileAppointmentDetailSheet.jsx:415-417 `return createPortal(<div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] bg-white overflow-y-auto" …` with no role/aria-modal/keydown/focus hook (scan: Escape=0 useModalFocus=0 roleDialog=0 bodyLock=0). The detail sheet portals to document.body (z-100), while the checkout sheet renders inline in this tree (z-105) …`. ui/Sheet.jsx:9 `useModalFocus(open, onClose)` + :11-16 body lock is the canonical, unused shell; contract line 76 `Overlays: Dialog or Sheet`. The classes are no-ops. Drift is real: OpportunityActions.jsx:154 `to: "/admin/estimates?tab=estimates"` while EstimatesPageV2.jsx:2598/:3375 open editors via `?editEstimateId=`. Downgraded from P1: with no importer the tree is not in the Vite bundle and has zero runtime or user impact — it is a maintenance-cost item. Not autofixable: deleting files needs explicit instruction (rule 5), and removing server routes needs proof no native-app or ops-script consumer exists, which this repo cannot supply (rule 18); the alternative (re-mount and fix drift) is a product decision. |

But the fix is a Tech Home restructure, which is a standing documented deferral: docs/design/DECISIONS.md:62-70 'Tech Home (`/tech`) will not be touched … Revisit if a tech reports specific frustration', and waves-portal-ui-redesign-spec.md:436 '`/tech` (Tech Home): DO NOT TOUCH'. The 'one primary action per view' rule cited is the admin spec (spec:51). Real ergonomics concern, no field report → P2 pending the owner's call. |

| F0605 | P2 | `client/src/utils/admin-fetch.js:17` | Admin fetch is fragmented: 2 shared wrappers with different contracts plus 81 inline adminFetch copies | Counts reproduce: 80 files define their own `function adminFetch`/`const adminFetch` outside the two helpers, 49 import utils/admin-fetch, 27 import lib/adminFetch, 135 non-test files read `waves_admin_token`. Contracts differ as claimed: utils/admin-fetch.js:52-70 parses, redirects on 401, retries 429; lib/adminFetch.js:14-27 returns the raw Response; ScheduleListView.jsx:9-18 `.then(r => { if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`${r.status}`); return r.json(); })`. But utils/admin-fetch.js:17-19 documents this exact state as a deliberate incremental policy ('The dozens of other inline copies still work — they just don't get the 429 grace path. Migrate as touched'), so it is tech debt with a P2 symptom (a raw '429' on unmigrated pages), not a P1 defect. Which wrapper becomes canonical (fold lib's raw mode into utils, or keep both) is the ruling the code does not settle. |
| F0606 | P2 | `client/tailwind.config.js:67` | `text-10` / `text-15` / `text-20` / `text-24` are not Tailwind tokens — the classes emit no CSS and silently inherit the parent size | Bug is real: client/tailwind.config.js:67-76 `fontSize` extends only '11','12','13','14','16','18','22','28', `plugins: []` (:98), no safelist/presets, postcss.config.js plain tailwindcss 3.4 (no numeric default scale), and no `.text-10\|15\|20\|24` rule in any client/src .css. Usage counts match exactly: text-10 ×86, text-15 ×22, text-20 ×13, text-24 ×5 (e.g. EstimatesPageV2.jsx:1189 `bg-zinc-50 text-10 uppercase tracking-label`, ScheduleListView.jsx:383 `text-10 uppercase`, ScheduleCustomerSidebar.jsx:329/337/351 `text-15`), so the classes emit nothing and the parent size is inherited. Downgraded to P2: it is a silent cosmetic mis-size, nothing functional. `text-10` → `text-11` is settled by the contract (admin-ui-consistency-contract.md:28 caption 11px); `text-15/20/24` have no contract step (contract 14/13/11, theme 16/18/22/28), so their mapping is a design ruling, and 126 visual changes need ui-verify, not an autofix. |
| F0155 | P3 | `client/src/components/report/lawnV2/LawnReportV2.jsx:282` | Off-scale font sizes (14.5 / 15 / 15.5 / 16.5 / 17px) throughout the V2 report bodies and one flow page | The sizes are real — LawnReportV2.jsx:282 `fontSize: 14.5`, :330 `fontSize: 16.5`, :333 `fontSize: 14.5`; TermiteReportV2.jsx:331 `fontSize: 17`; CockroachReportV2.jsx:80 `fontSize: 15`; ReportViewPage.jsx:7300 `.sr-cell-value { … font-size: 15px }`, :6031 `font-size: 15px`; SecureAppointmentPage.jsx:628 / :676 `fontSize: 14.5`; 43 matches across the six V2 files (lawn 20, tree 8, termite 7, cockroach 6, mosquito 2, pest 0). But no repo rule bans them: the customer-doc-style-guide.md:20-36 table defines tokens and a 14px floor, not exclusivity; the glass sheet itself authors 15px (glass-theme.css:159 `[data-g-stattile] * { font-size: 15px !important }`, :504 `.waves-ask-form button { … font-size: 15px !important }`); the owner accepted 15px on the estimate seasons block (DECISIONS.md:2082 'Layout: 16px row heading, 15px description'); and batch C's gate/sweep of components/report (check-portal-brand.js:36-38, DECISIONS.md:2074-2075) left these in place. The only rule-backed subset is 14.5px running prose vs D1 'body prose is 16' (theme-doc.js:46) — whether a given card sub-line is prose (16) or meta (14) is a per-site design judgment, which is why the reader's own mapping reads '14.5→14 (or 16 where it is running prose)'. Real drift, cosmetic, above the floor. |
| F0275 | P3 | `client/src/pages/PayPageV2.jsx:652` | Pay page still renders a 'No fees' status pill inside the Zelle panel despite the no-chips ruling | The span exists exactly as cited (client/src/pages/PayPageV2.jsx:652-665: `textTransform: 'uppercase' ... borderRadius: 999 ... No fees`). But the ruling it invokes does not cover it. docs/design/DECISIONS.md:2043 (4): "**No status chips on customer pages.** ... Every status pill / badge on Secure, Receipt, Appointment, Pest (urgency + safety), Lawn, Track, Pay, Contract, reschedule and re-service is gone; state reads from the heading and copy ... Remaining estimate chip-like elements (WaveGuard tier chip, setup-fee / savings badges in `SecurePlanChoice`, the picker's "Tech nearby" pill) await a ruling." 'No fees' is not a state; it is a fee/savings badge of the class the ruling explicitly parks. git blame shows line 653 (`fontSize: FS.body`) was rewritten by e0820a9699 on 2026-09-05 — the post-ruling glass sweep touched this pill and kept it. So the finding restates a pending ruling as a violation of a settled one; what remains is a chip-shaped informational badge on a customer page, which is the open ruling itself. |
| F0278 | P3 | `client/src/pages/PortalPage.jsx:1` | Giant single-file pages: PortalPage 16,168 lines / 238 useState, Customer360ProfileV2 8,474 lines / 145 useState | Size facts verified: `wc -l` = 16168 (PortalPage.jsx), 9636 (ReportViewPage.jsx), 8474 (Customer360ProfileV2.jsx); grep counts 238 `useState(`, 50 `useEffect(`, 55 top-level `function [A-Z]` components in PortalPage; C360 145 `useState(`. client/vite.config.js:44-48 already documents it: "PortalPage remains a large mixed-responsibility module ... Split that page before adding it here". But the perf mechanism claimed is wrong: with 55 separate function components (e.g. `function ShellCloseButton` at 453, `ScheduleTab` at ~4194, `PortalPage` at 15319) state lives in the component that owns it, so a card's state change re-renders that component subtree, not "the whole portal tree". The chunk is lazy (client/src/App.jsx:358 `const PortalPage = lazyWithRetry(() => import('./pages/PortalPage'))`, comment at 355 names it as 500KB+) and loads once per PWA install. This is a documented known state and a program-level refactor (rule 10 plan-first), not a defect; maintainability P3. |
| F0371 | P3 | `client/src/pages/admin/AutonomousContentReviewPage.jsx:296` | Autopilot tab renders a TruGreen-style forest-green/lime brand hero, cream page background and iPhone mockup inside /admin | The rendered facts are accurate — client/src/pages/admin/AutonomousContentReviewPage.jsx:296 `bg-[#FAF7EF]` (non-embedded only), :298 `rounded-2xl bg-[#143D2A]`, :299 `bg-[#43B02A]/25 blur-3xl`, PhoneFrame, autonomous-content/shared.jsx:77 `bg-[#43B02A]`, :165 green/forest Tag tones — but the theme is an explicit owner directive, not a missed token pass: commit fa61fa6d5 (PR #2021) body: 'TruGreen-inspired theme (owner-directed)… NOTE: intentionally diverges from the admin monochrome-zinc convention per owner request — this page is deliberately brand-themed.' The reader's likely_cause is backwards: the Blog-hub embed landed earlier in 8808d003e (PR #1876, `<AutonomousContentReviewPage embedded />` at BlogPage.jsx:3059), so the owner directed the look on the already-embedded Autopilot tab. Whether that exception still stands beside the monochrome Blog header is an owner ruling, not a reviewer call. What remains real (P3): two `<h1>`s when embedded — AdminCommandHeader.jsx:23/:27 defaults `headingLevel = 1` → `h1` for BlogPage's header (:3018) and AutonomousContentReviewPage.jsx:316 renders its own `<h1 …>Autonomous blog activity</h1>`. Also note the decision lives only in a PR body, not docs/design/DECISIONS.md. |
| F0475 | P3 | `client/src/pages/admin/InventoryPage.jsx:2535` | Money and count columns in Tier 2 tables are left-aligned (the cited cells are monospace, so figures are already tabular) | Partly refuted. InventoryPage.jsx:2535-2551 — both cited cells set `fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace"`; monospace figures are tabular by construction, so 'no tabular figures' does not hold for the cited cells. AdminInvoicesPage.jsx:1692 wraps the total in `<div style={{ textAlign: "right" }}>`, so the 'Invoices total left-aligned' claim is wrong (only `fontVariantNumeric` is absent there, in Roboto). What survives: `thS` (131-139) is `textAlign: "left"` for 'Best Price'/'Unit Cost' (2351-2352) and the cells inherit `tdS` (140-145) left alignment; TaxPage.jsx:2782-2786 and EquipmentPage.jsx:1022-1030 are mono, left. The contract (admin-ui-consistency-contract.md 'Numbers: use tabular numerals (u-nums)') mandates tabular numerals, not right alignment — right alignment is only a convention (ui/Table.jsx:61-69 `TD align nums`, SEOPage.jsx:116/124 thR/tdR, BankingPage 14× `textAlign: "right"`). Left-aligned mono money columns on Tier 2 pages are a consistency nit, and the contract's 'Do not replace legacy inline styles globally' rule plus the alignment convention question make it a design call, not an autofix. |
| F0561 | P3 | `client/src/pages/admin/WinLossSlicesCard.jsx:32` | 148 raw <table> in admin vs 10 ui/Table consumers; Tier-1 files drop the overflow-x wrapper ui/Table provides | Duplication half holds: ui/Table.jsx:4-9 wraps `<table>` in `<div className="w-full overflow-x-auto">`; WinLossSlicesCard.jsx hand-rolls seven `<table className="w-full text-13">` (:32, :154, :191, :216, :255, :276, :328); only DocumentRequestsPage.jsx imports `Table` from the ui barrel; ~143 raw `<table` remain across pages/admin + components/admin. The responsive harm is refuted for the cited file: every WinLoss table is 2–4 short-text columns (`Clean lookup` / `62%` / `n=12`; Band/Clean/Flagged; Age/Won/Lost/Open at :191-195 of the 140-340 window) with `w-full` and no `whitespace-nowrap`, so cells wrap and nothing forces page-level horizontal scroll at 375px; SourcePerformanceCard.jsx:119/:165 are the same shape. EmailAutomationsPanelV2.jsx:106-108 (`Card className="p-0 overflow-hidden"` around a 6-column `px-4` table) is the one real clip candidate, and NewsletterPage.jsx:1504 already wraps its sibling in `overflow-x-auto`. Migrating to `<Table>/<TR>/<TH>` is not behaviour-preserving (adds `border-collapse`, `hover:bg-zinc-50` rows, uppercase 11px TH) — it is a design-system consolidation that needs a ruling on whether ui/Table becomes mandatory for Tier 1 cards. |

### K.4 Programmes (multi-PR, from sections C, G, H, I)

1. Sub-modal / sheet consolidation: every Tier 1 hand-rolled overlay onto `ui/Dialog` (with `layer`) or `ui/Sheet`; one `MobileSheetFrame` for the 12 schedule sheets; `ui/Dialog` scroll lock → `useLockBodyScroll`.
2. `ui/State` + `ui/Toast` and the retirement of 294 `alert()` outcomes and 15 toast copies.
3. Admin fetch consolidation (81 inline copies → `utils/admin-fetch`) with a domain-rules grep.
4. `ui/Table` growth (`SortableTH`, `Pagination`, `minWidth`) and the two mechanical table PRs (wrappers; `adminLegacy.js`).
5. Tier 2 palette convergence (`theme-admin.js`) and per-page Tier 1 migrations in the order of section I.
6. Glass edge collapse in the sequence of section G.
7. Customer portal local-style folding into the brand primitives; report V2 `reportStyles.js`.
8. Enforcement: `check-portal-brand` scope widening, undefined-class grep, import-graph reachability test; `eslint-plugin-jsx-a11y` as CI warnings only after Adam approves the new dependency (see H, A11y mechanisms).

### K.5 Not verified in this pass

364 P2/P3 findings that were neither P1 nor flagged autofixable were not sent through the refute pass (all are listed, with their evidence lines and proposed fixes, in the tracked inventory `docs/design/ui-consistency-audit-2026-09-07-inventory.md`). They are consistency, duplication, typography and dead-code observations; treat each as "reader-reported" until a fix PR re-reads its lines.

## Appendix: verification log

## Refuted

| uid | reader id | file | claim | why refuted |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| F0145 | glass-census:GL-05 | `client/src/components/estimate/glass/glass-components.css:115` | Glass component sheet ships 11px/12px type on customer estimate chips (gate cannot see .css) | None of the three rules renders under 14px. (1) `.gc-slot-priority` (glass-components.css:108-123, 11px) and `.gc-slot-rain` (:126-139, 12px) have no consumer: `grep -rn "gc-slot-" client/src \| grep -v .css:` returns nothing (JSX, JS and the glass engine included) — they are dead CSS, not customer type. (2) `.gc-gr-city` (:266-269, 12px) is the only live one (GlassEstimateExtras.jsx:158 `<span className="gc-gr-city">{r.location}</span>`), and the estimate walker retags by computed size, not class: EstimateGlassTheme.jsx:139 `if (!el.hasAttribute('data-gt') && !el.closest('button, a') && …` → :146 `else if (fs <= 12) el.setAttribute('data-gt', 'fine')`; glass-theme.css:143-144 `html[data-glass-theme] [data-gt="fine"] { font-size: 14px !important; …}` beats the non-!important `[data-glass-theme] .gc-gr-city` rule. The marquee card is a div (GlassEstimateExtras.jsx:154-157), so the button/anchor skip does not apply, and the walker runs on this page (EstimateViewPage.jsx:588 `<EstimateGlassTheme active />`). The gate-scope observation is accurate (check-portal-brand.js:119 `if (!/\.(jsx?\|tsx?)$/.test(entry.name)) continue;` and :31-34 exclude components/estimate) but that is tooling, not a rendered defect. Optional cleanup: delete the two dead slot rules rather than resize them. |
| F0230 | overlays:OV-03 | `client/src/components/ui/Dialog.jsx:41` | ui/Dialog and ui/Sheet lock scroll with body overflow:hidden, which the repo's own hook documents as ignored by iOS Safari | The cited code is real — client/src/components/ui/Dialog.jsx:39-44 and Sheet.jsx:11-16 set `document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden'`, and hooks/useLockBodyScroll.js:8-9 says iOS ignores that — but the claimed defect cannot occur where these primitives live. Every consumer renders inside AdminLayoutV2 (all pages/admin/*, plus dispatch/JobDrawer.jsx via DispatchBoardPage.jsx and estimates/CommercialProposalModal.jsx via EstimatesPageV2.jsx; no tech/customer importer), and in that shell the window never scrolls: client/src/index.css:267-268 'The admin window never scrolls; .admin-main (AdminLayoutV2) is the real scroll container', AdminLayoutV2.jsx:178 same note, :640-643 `height: "var(--admin-vh, 100vh)"`, `overflowY: "auto"`. The dialog portals to document.body (Dialog.jsx:109, Sheet.jsx:57), whose document is exactly one viewport tall, so a touch drag on the overlay has no ancestor scroller to bleed into — and neither body `overflow:hidden` nor useLockBodyScroll's body `position:fixed` touches `.admin-main`. The proposed swap is a no-op in every live consumer; the non-refcounted save/restore is inert for the same reason. Dialog.test.jsx:39/52 already covers the body overflow contract. |
| F0363 | agents-ib:AIB-12 | `client/src/pages/admin/AgentOpsPage.jsx:46` | Five hub tabs are Tier-2 light-D with blue/green/amber/red status chips and 700 weights, beside four Tier-1 tabs — one copy-pasted Chip in each file | The finding restates a documented repo decision as a bug. docs/design/DECISIONS.md:627-629 (2026-06-12, agents-hub-consolidation): '`AgentsHubPage`, Tier 1 V2 shell … Both pages stay in place as files (Tier 2 styles intact) and gain an `embedded` prop'. The later tabs record the same choice in their headers: AgentShadowDraftsPage.jsx:9-10 and PendingDraftsTab.jsx:15-16 'Tier 2 styling (inline + light D palette) to match the sibling …; the hub shell stays Tier 1.' The contract (docs/design/admin-ui-consistency-contract.md) says 'Migration rule: Apply this contract one page or shared component at a time', 'Existing 600/700 legacy text may remain until its page is migrated', and 'Move a page only when that page is the declared scope.' The repo fact for this audit is that two systems coexisting is not the violation — mixing inside one component is — and none of the five files mixes: AgentOpsPage.jsx:1-19, AgentDecisionsPage.jsx:1-5 and DataHygienePage.jsx:1-5 import only AdminCommandHeader/adminFetch/hooks, AgentShadowDraftsPage.jsx:12-13 and PendingDraftsTab.jsx:18-19 import only adminFetch; no `components/ui` import in any of them. Residual (P3, not a P1): the Chip helper is copy-pasted (AgentOpsPage.jsx:65-84, AgentDecisionsPage.jsx:22-46, DataHygienePage.jsx:22-46, a bg/fg variant in PendingDraftsTab.jsx:172) and `blue: "#1D4ED8"` is used as a status tone the contract does not list — both disappear with the per-page migration, whose scope is an owner declaration. |
| F0372 | billing-admin:BA-18 | `client/src/pages/admin/BankingPage.jsx:44` | Banking and Tax force a page-specific JetBrains Mono stack on every amount, against the Roboto-only contract | The claimed outcome ('switch to a monospace family… browser falls back to Menlo/Courier') cannot happen: client/src/index.css:188-193 `html.admin-app, html.admin-app *, .admin-shell-v2, .admin-shell-v2 * { font-family: "Roboto", system-ui, sans-serif !important; }` and :194-207 apply the same `!important` Roboto to `[class*="font-mono"]`, `code` and `pre`; the comment at :176-181 says this exists precisely because 'many inline-styled elements set their own font-family via the style attribute' — an `!important` stylesheet declaration beats an inline `style` in the cascade. So `fontFamily: MONO` from BankingPage.jsx:44 and TaxPage.jsx:41 (`"'JetBrains Mono', monospace"`) never renders; every amount stays Roboto, exactly what the contract line 'The admin font is scoped by `.admin-shell-v2` in client/src/index.css' describes. The behaviour is handled by the documented mechanism. Residual: the MONO constant is inert dead styling present in 46 client files (grep -rl JetBrains client/src), i.e. not a Banking/Tax-specific font stack; a repo-wide constant cleanup is a P3 outside this finding. |

So 48px padding + header + 20px margin + (100vh - 64) always exceeds 100vh on desktop and the board's bottom ~100px is below the fold until the outer scroller moves, at which point the sticky header covers its top; on phones the fixed bars add to it and raw 100vh ignores the shell's --admin-vh/100dvh (index.css:211-220). | F0133 | `client/src/components/estimate/AppShowcaseCard.jsx:134` | App card forces the two store badges side by side and a 240px copy column — overflows the card at 320px | P2 → P3 | The inputs are as cited: AppShowcaseCard.jsx:27 `width="132" height="40" … style={{ display: 'block', height: 40, width: 'auto' }}`, :39 `width="135"`, :134 `flexWrap: 'nowrap'`, glass-components.css:443-446 `.gc-av-right { flex: 1; min-width: 240px; }`, EstimateViewPage.jsx:592 `padding: '32px 20px 40px'`, cardStyles.js:21 `padding: 24` (+1px border, :19). But the arithmetic overstates the effect. At 320px the card content box is 320-40-2-48 = 230px; `.gc-app-visual` is `flex-wrap: wrap` (glass-components.css:366-371) so `.gc-av-right` wraps onto its own line at 240px, overrunning the content box by 10px — still inside the 25px card border+padding, not 'past the card edge'. The 275px badge row centred in that 240px column overflows 17.5px each side (`justify-content: center` distributes negative space equally), so the right badge ends ~2.5px past the card's border and ~17px inside the page's 20px gutter; document.scrollWidth stays 320, so the 'must never scroll horizontally' expectation is not violated. At 360px (content 270) the overflow is 2.5px per side, entirely within the card padding; at 375px+ it fits. The left pair already carries an explicit narrow-phone guard (glass-components.css:374-378 'Never wider than the card's content box — on narrow phones the pair shrinks to fit'). So: a ≤~345px-only, ~2.5px border crossing of one badge — cosmetic. The reader's proposed fix (wrap below 360) contradicts the owner directive quoted at AppShowcaseCard.jsx:130-131 ('always SIDE BY SIDE (owner 2026-07-09)'); the side-by-side form can be kept by letting the badges shrink instead. |

'3px 9px' : '6px 12px', borderRadius: 999` used at :506, :576, :696, :920 and TreeShrubReportV2.jsx:333, :402, :441, :481; TermiteReportV2.jsx:334-336 `borderRadius: 999 … ACTIVE`; CockroachReportV2.jsx:207-209 `{whatsNext.badge}` pill and :47-55 `chip` (999); PestReportV2.jsx:234-238 / MosquitoReportV2.jsx:175-179 SupportingMetric (999); PestReportV2.jsx:640 / MosquitoReportV2.jsx:421 dueLabel pill; PestReportV2.jsx:695 'Identified on-site'; GaugePrimitives.jsx:208-212 TrendChip; ReportViewPage.jsx:3110-3124, :3202-3214, :4562-4567 + :6762-6773. No functional impact; the replacement grammar per family needs the owner. Consistency debt at P2, owner decision required. |
| F0155 | `client/src/components/report/lawnV2/LawnReportV2.jsx:282` | Off-scale font sizes (14.5 / 15 / 15.5 / 16.5 / 17px) throughout the V2 report bodies and one flow page | P2 → P3 | The sizes are real — LawnReportV2.jsx:282 `fontSize: 14.5`, :330 `fontSize: 16.5`, :333 `fontSize: 14.5`; TermiteReportV2.jsx:331 `fontSize: 17`; CockroachReportV2.jsx:80 `fontSize: 15`; ReportViewPage.jsx:7300 `.sr-cell-value { … font-size: 15px }`, :6031 `font-size: 15px`; SecureAppointmentPage.jsx:628 / :676 `fontSize: 14.5`; 43 matches across the six V2 files (lawn 20, tree 8, termite 7, cockroach 6, mosquito 2, pest 0). But no repo rule bans them: the customer-doc-style-guide.md:20-36 table defines tokens and a 14px floor, not exclusivity; the glass sheet itself authors 15px (glass-theme.css:159 `[data-g-stattile] * { font-size: 15px !important }`, :504 `.waves-ask-form button { … font-size: 15px !important }`); the owner accepted 15px on the estimate seasons block (DECISIONS.md:2082 'Layout: 16px row heading, 15px description'); and batch C's gate/sweep of components/report (check-portal-brand.js:36-38, DECISIONS.md:2074-2075) left these in place. The only rule-backed subset is 14.5px running prose vs D1 'body prose is 16' (theme-doc.js:46) — whether a given card sub-line is prose (16) or meta (14) is a per-site design judgment, which is why the reader's own mapping reads '14.5→14 (or 16 where it is running prose)'. Real drift, cosmetic, above the floor. |

MarkPrepaidModal.jsx:202-211 `return createPortal(<div onClick={dismiss} className="fixed inset-0 z-[1200] flex items-end md:items-center justify-center" …><div onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()} className="w-full bg-white rounded-2xl" style={{ maxWidth: 480, padding: 20 }}>` — no role, no ref, and the file has no keydown/useModalFocus/body-lock at all; PrepaySwitchSheet.jsx:107-116 `function Shell({ children }) { return createPortal(<div className="fixed inset-0 z-[1200] …">` no role; RescheduleConfirmModal.jsx:175-178 `role="dialog" aria-modal="true"` but no useModalFocus/Escape/labelledby; RainOutSheet.jsx:645-651 role + aria-label, no trap. Why P2 not P1: the contract is `Active for new and migrated /admin/* work` (line 3) and mandates `one page or shared component at a time` (line 7), so un-migrated hand-rolled overlays are declared migration backlog rather than a shipped defect; every overlay has an explicit 44px close control; the two operators are touch/mouse users. | F0181 | `client/src/components/schedule/MobileAppointmentDetailSheet.jsx:417` | No shared sheet implementation: 10 full-screen sheets and 8 overlay modals each hand-roll their shell; none use ui/Sheet or ui/Dialog, so focus trap, Escape, aria-modal, scroll lock and z-layering are inconsistent (10 distinct z-index values) | P1 → P2 | Facts hold. Same finding as F0185 and (for the centre modals) F0175. Why P2: the contract applies to new and migrated work one component at a time (lines 3, 7), so this is declared backlog rather than a shipped regression; the stacking consequence is already handled in code; the surfaces are opaque full-screen sheets with 44px Close controls used by two touch-first operators — degraded keyboard/AT semantics, nothing blocked. Which shell becomes canonical (new MobileSheet vs Dialog's max-sm full-screen mode at Dialog.jsx:57-65 vs Sheet) and the target z-ladder are design rulings; the migration is cross-file. |

| F0193 | `client/src/components/schedule/MobilePaymentSheet.jsx:85` | Tap to Pay button stays permanently disabled after handoff; a cancelled or declined Terminal session has no retry, and in the prepay flows the only exit (Close) voids the invoice | P2 → P3 | The stuck button is real: MobilePaymentSheet.jsx:85-98 `setCharging(true); … await launchTapToPay(invoiceId); // Control is now handed off … the parent will close it` and only the catch runs `setCharging(false)`; lib/tapToPay.js:54-57 resolves the promise on `visibilityState === 'hidden'`, and the sheet has no visibilitychange/pageshow/focus handler (the only one in the dir, useDispatchReadiness.js:66-71, just refreshes readiness), so 170-174 `disabled={charging}` keeps rendering 'Opening Tap to Pay…' after the tech comes back. DispatchPageV2.jsx:2270-2274 even documents it ('a launched Tap to Pay left `charging` stuck for the next customer — codex P2 #3187 r18') and mitigated only with `key={paymentData.invoiceId}`, so a same-invoice retry is still blocked. The rest is overstated: the tender rows at 100-123/194-226 (Cash, Check, Manual Credit Card Entry, Card on File for admins) stay enabled in every flow including the prepay sheets, so a declined card can be retaken without leaving; in the checkout flow Close returns to MobileCheckoutSheet and re-tapping Charge remounts with charging=false (two taps); and PrepaySwitchSheet.jsx:224-250/325-331 `abortAndClose` re-reads the invoice and routes settled→`collected()`, processing→recovery, voiding only a genuinely uncollected prepay — the documented abort design (MobilePaymentSheet.jsx:111-115, PrepaySwitchSheet.jsx:221-223), not a defect. tapToPayReturn.js:3-8 also notes iOS usually evicts the tab so the sheet remounts anyway. Real but low-impact polish → P3. Re-enabling on return is safe: server/routes/stripe-terminal.js:172-174 refuses paid/prepaid/processing invoices and 31/297 lock concurrent attempts, so a second tap cannot double-charge. |

| F0208 | `client/src/components/schedule/TimeGridDay.jsx:387` | Time grids are pointer-only: appointment blocks are announced as buttons but Enter/Space does nothing, and no keyboard sensor or focusable slot exists | P1 → P2 | Pointer-only is proven: TimeGridDay.jsx:387-398 `<div ref={setNodeRef} {...attributes} {...listeners} onClick={(e) => { if (isDragging) return; … onEdit?.(service); }}` and TimeGridDays.jsx:235-243 identical; sensors are TimeGridDay.jsx:1156 / TimeGridDays.jsx:752 `useSensors(useSensor(PointerSensor, { activationConstraint: { distance: 4 } }))` only, so dnd-kit `listeners` carry just onPointerDown while `attributes` still add role='button'/tabIndex=0/aria-roledescription='draggable' (node_modules/@dnd-kit/core/dist/core.esm.js:3406-3439); a div with role=button gets no synthetic click on Enter/Space, and grep for onKeyDown\|KeyboardSensor\|sortableKeyboardCoordinates in both grids = 0; SlotDroppable (TimeGridDay.jsx:580) has only `onPointerDown`. The only keyboard handler in the schedule views is MonthDayCell (CalendarViewsV2.jsx:347-353). Enter/Space→onEdit is a trivial local addition, but keyboard drag/create (KeyboardSensor with a time-grid coordinate getter and announcements vs a 'Move to…' action) is a design ruling, so the finding as scoped is not autofixable. |

| F0275 | `client/src/pages/PayPageV2.jsx:652` | Pay page still renders a 'No fees' status pill inside the Zelle panel despite the no-chips ruling | P2 → P3 | The span exists exactly as cited (client/src/pages/PayPageV2.jsx:652-665: `textTransform: 'uppercase' ... borderRadius: 999 ... No fees`). But the ruling it invokes does not cover it. docs/design/DECISIONS.md:2043 (4): "**No status chips on customer pages.** ... Every status pill / badge on Secure, Receipt, Appointment, Pest (urgency + safety), Lawn, Track, Pay, Contract, reschedule and re-service is gone; state reads from the heading and copy ... Remaining estimate chip-like elements (WaveGuard tier chip, setup-fee / savings badges in `SecurePlanChoice`, the picker's "Tech nearby" pill) await a ruling." 'No fees' is not a state; it is a fee/savings badge of the class the ruling explicitly parks. git blame shows line 653 (`fontSize: FS.body`) was rewritten by e0820a9699 on 2026-09-05 — the post-ruling glass sweep touched this pill and kept it. So the finding restates a pending ruling as a violation of a settled one; what remains is a chip-shaped informational badge on a customer page, which is the open ruling itself. |
| F0278 | `client/src/pages/PortalPage.jsx:1` | Giant single-file pages: PortalPage 16,168 lines / 238 useState, Customer360ProfileV2 8,474 lines / 145 useState | P1 → P3 | Size facts verified: `wc -l` = 16168 (PortalPage.jsx), 9636 (ReportViewPage.jsx), 8474 (Customer360ProfileV2.jsx); grep counts 238 `useState(`, 50 `useEffect(`, 55 top-level `function [A-Z]` components in PortalPage; C360 145 `useState(`. client/vite.config.js:44-48 already documents it: "PortalPage remains a large mixed-responsibility module ... Split that page before adding it here". But the perf mechanism claimed is wrong: with 55 separate function components (e.g. `function ShellCloseButton` at 453, `ScheduleTab` at ~4194, `PortalPage` at 15319) state lives in the component that owns it, so a card's state change re-renders that component subtree, not "the whole portal tree". The chunk is lazy (client/src/App.jsx:358 `const PortalPage = lazyWithRetry(() => import('./pages/PortalPage'))`, comment at 355 names it as 500KB+) and loads once per PWA install. This is a documented known state and a program-level refactor (rule 10 plan-first), not a defect; maintainability P3. |
| F0286 | `client/src/pages/PortalPage.jsx:3510` | PortalPage defines 13 local `card` objects; 10 are byte-identical and contradict PORTAL_CARD_STYLE (radius 8 vs 16, hard-coded #E7E2D7 vs PORTAL_SHELL tokens) | P2 → P3 | Duplication is real: the 10 objects at client/src/pages/PortalPage.jsx:3510,4489,5844,7623,8473,8892,10580,12096,12767,14985 hash identically (`background: B.white, border: '1px solid #E7E2D7', borderRadius: 8, boxShadow: '0 1px 2px rgba(15,23,42,0.04)', position: 'relative'`), 13822/14838 use tokens with radius 8, and 479-484 `PORTAL_CARD_STYLE` uses radius 16. But the visual claim is refuted: every consumer is a glass card (PortalPage.jsx:10643 and 10659 `<section ... data-glass="card" style={{ ...card, ...}}>`, 12257 same) and PortalPage.jsx:15507 `useGlassSurface(true)` is unconditional, so client/src/glass/glass-theme.css overrides all four properties with !important: 180-188 `html[data-glass-theme] [data-glass] { ... border: 1px solid var(--glass-border) !important; box-shadow: ... }`, 455 `[data-glass]:not([data-glass="modal"]) { border-radius: 12px !important; }`, 479-481 `[data-glass="card"] { background: ... !important; }`. All cards render r12 with the glass border — there are not "three card shapes" and Referrals/Billing corners are not tighter. The token contradiction is also refuted: client/src/theme-customer.js:19 `border: '#E7E2D7'` is what `PORTAL_SHELL.border` resolves to (PortalPage.jsx:395,412). Only `position: 'relative'` survives from these objects. Code-hygiene P3. |
| F0319 | `client/src/pages/PublicBookingPage.jsx:1067` | Public booking address + unit inputs have no accessible label | P1 → P2 | client/src/pages/PublicBookingPage.jsx:1067-1097 renders `<AddressAutocomplete autoFocus value={address.line1} ... placeholder="Start typing your address" className="waves-focus-ring" style={inputStyle} />` and 1106-1127 `<input type="text" value={address.line2} ... placeholder="Apt / Unit # (optional)" ... />`; an awk over 1040-1130 finds no `aria-label`, `<label`, `aria-labelledby` or `id=` in the address step, while the file's own comment at 1346-1348 records the htmlFor/id fix on the contact step. client/src/components/AddressAutocomplete.jsx:42 documents "Other native input props (maxLength, aria-label, disabled) are forwarded" and 186-187 spreads `{...inputProps}`, so nothing stops the fix. Downgraded because the 'announced as unnamed' claim is overstated: per HTML-AAM a text input's accessible name falls back to `placeholder`, so VoiceOver reads 'Start typing your address' / 'Apt / Unit # (optional)' — weak, disappearing-on-input names rather than none. Real a11y gap, not a P1 blocker. |
| F0326 | `client/src/pages/ReportViewPage.jsx:2530` | Service report hero and re-entry card still render status pills ('Ready now', 'Service complete', 'Follow-up recommended') | P1 → P2 | Both pills exist as cited: client/src/pages/ReportViewPage.jsx:2530-2534 `<div className={`status-badge status-${smartStatus.statusTone \|\| (readinessBadge?.ready ? 'ready' : 'pending')}`}>{smartStatus.status \|\| readinessBadge.label}</div>`, 2743 `<div className="readiness-status-chip">{readiness.status}</div>`, CSS 6205-6235 (`border-radius: 999px ... .status-badge.status-warning { ... color: #B45309 }`), and a repo grep confirms no other file (glass-theme.css included) targets those classes. But the readiness chip is a documented, deliberate hold, not an unswept leftover: docs/design/DECISIONS.md batch C (2026-09-05) (6) "Left alone on purpose: ... the report's re-entry 'Ready now' readiness indicator still renders as a chip because it carries a safety state and its own tests — flagged for a ruling under the no-chips rule" (client/src/pages/ReportViewPage.test.jsx:22,99 imports `readinessStatusBadge`). The no-chips commit 79fc585eb did touch ReportViewPage and removed only the `.floating-ask-pills` marquee. What is genuinely unflagged is the hero chip's non-safety values (`smartStatus.status` such as 'Service complete' / 'Follow-up recommended' with pending/warning/neutral tones), but its fallback branch renders the same readiness label, so it is part of the same pending ruling. Not a P1 regression against a settled ruling; a P2 pending owner decision. |
| F0342 | `client/src/pages/admin/AdminInvoicesPage.jsx:1161` | Reverse prepaid invoice has no pending state — double-click fires two money-moving POSTs | P1 → P2 | Client side is as claimed: AdminInvoicesPage.jsx:1161-1180 `handleReversePrepaid` never calls setRowActionBusy, and the button at :1975-1981 has no `disabled`/opacity, unlike handleVoid :1126-1139 and its button :1965-1972 — a double-click does send two POSTs. But the money side is guarded on the server: admin-invoices.js:2354-2364 locks the row `trx('invoices').where({ id }).forUpdate().first()` inside a transaction, :2395-2397 throws 400 'No applied credit to restore' when `restore <= 0`, and :2413-2421 / :2440-2443 write `credit_applied: 0` (and the restored status) in that same transaction — the second request finds nothing to restore and is refused, so credit cannot be returned twice. The real defect is the missing busy state plus a spurious 'Reverse failed: …' toast (:1178) after the success toast — P2, not a P1 double money movement. |
| F0353 | `client/src/pages/admin/AdminInvoicesPage.jsx:7126` | Follow-up panel shows 'Loading follow-up…' forever when the fetch fails | P2 → P3 | AdminInvoicesPage.jsx:7126-7131 `const d = await adminFetch(\`/admin/invoices/${invoiceId}/followup\`).catch(() => null); setData(d);` and :7152-7157 `if (!data) return (<div …>Loading follow-up…</div>)` — null is both the initial and the failed state, so a 401/500 leaves the line permanently. Real, but it is a secondary panel under an expanded row: nothing about money or invoice data is misrepresented and every row action still works (the panel's own `act` at :7134-7150 reports failures via toast). A stuck loading line with no retry is a P3 states defect, not P2. |
| F0359 | `client/src/pages/admin/AgentDecisionsPage.jsx:584` | AgentDecisionsPage form fields have no associated <label>; notice/error banners have no role | P2 → P3 | Facts hold: client/src/pages/admin/AgentDecisionsPage.jsx:584 `<div style={{ fontSize: 11, … }}>Actual Human Reply</div>` and :593 `Final / Rewrite Reply` are plain divs, not `<label htmlFor>`; :585-591 and :594-600 textareas carry only placeholders; :605-616 the two inputs have only placeholders (`"scenario, e.g. scheduling"`, `"What should the agent learn from this reply?"`); :685-690 the correctedActions textarea has neither label nor placeholder (no accessible name at all); :280-290 the notice/error banner is a styled div with no role. Over-rated at P2: this is a Tier 2, single-operator admin page (DECISIONS.md:627-629 keeps it Tier 2 until migrated), four of the five controls still receive a placeholder-derived accessible name, and the contract's 'accessible names' item is a per-page migration checklist entry, not a hard line. P3 a11y hygiene. |

The reader's likely_cause is backwards: the Blog-hub embed landed earlier in 8808d003e (PR #1876, `<AutonomousContentReviewPage embedded />` at BlogPage.jsx:3059), so the owner directed the look on the already-embedded Autopilot tab. | F0379 | `client/src/pages/admin/BlogPage.jsx:112` | Module-level `isMobile` constants (Blog, Reviews, ReviewVelocityEngine) never update; RVE imports useIsMobile but never calls it; Social/KB define their own hooks | P2 → P3 | Partly right, partly wrong. Right: client/src/pages/admin/BlogPage.jsx:112 `const isMobile = typeof window !== "undefined" && window.innerWidth < 640;` consumed inside components at :141 (Card padding), :742, :934, :2556, :2579; ReviewsPage.jsx:23 same constant consumed in StatCard (:122-124), LocationCard (:221-223) and :1508/:1681/:1873/:1978; ReviewVelocityEngine.jsx:507 same constant consumed in `Dashboard` (:968) at :1057-1321. Those branches are frozen at bundle evaluation. Wrong: ReviewVelocityEngine.jsx:2 is NOT an unused import — `CustomerDrawer` (:2006) calls `const isMobile = useIsMobile(640);` at :2016 and `BatchModal` (:2512) at :2522, each with the comment 'Reactive (rotation-safe) — the module-level snapshot never recomputes'; the reader's grep for the literal `useIsMobile()` missed the `(640)` argument, so 'the RVE CustomerDrawer full-screen branch is fixed at bundle-evaluation time' is false. SocialMediaPage.jsx:161-171 and KnowledgeBasePage.jsx:52-63 are functional local hooks (resize / matchMedia listeners), so 'never update' does not apply to them — they are duplicates of hooks/useIsMobile.js, not bugs. Severity: a rotate/split-view edge case on admin phone use, with the two overlay components that matter most already fixed → P3. |
| F0405 | `client/src/pages/admin/CompliancePage.jsx:77` | `sTableWrap` uses `overflow: hidden` — Compliance and Discounts tables clip their right-hand columns on narrow screens instead of scrolling | P1 → P2 | CompliancePage.jsx:77-82 `const sTableWrap = { background: D.card, border: \`1px solid ${D.border}\`, borderRadius: 12, overflow: "hidden" };` wraps `<table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse" }}>` directly at :217, :564, :643 and :751 with no inner scroller — so the clip is real on the wide nitrogen (:643, Customer/City/County/Lawn Type/… 7+ columns) and licenses (:751) tables at 375px. But the reader's supporting facts are wrong: the tables at 566/645 do NOT lack a wrapper — they are inside sTableWrap (:564, :643), i.e. they clip rather than page-scroll; and the file already has the correct pattern at :355-357 `<div style={sTableWrap}><div style={{ overflowX: "auto" }}><table` (Applications tab) which the finding missed. DiscountsTabs.jsx:46-51 is an identical constant (`overflow: "hidden"`) around the :429 table; its :384/:396/:418 spreads are text placeholders; :1079's table sits in `sCard` (:1077) with no overflow rule (bare). Real defect, over-rated: content-dependent (the 4-column :217 and :564 tables wrap and may fit), a Tier 2 compliance/discounts surface rather than a daily driver, the desktop view and the DACS export are unaffected, and the site count was inflated. |

| F0425 | `client/src/pages/admin/DiscountsTabs.jsx:636` | Money fields as type="number" without step — cents flagged invalid, spinner/scroll-wheel mutates dollar amounts | P2 → P3 | client/src/pages/admin/DiscountsTabs.jsx:630-634 (Amount), :638-642 (`Max Discount ($)`), :717-721 (`Min Subtotal ($)`) are `<input type="number" style={sInput} value=… onChange={(e) => upd(…, e.target.value)} />` with no `step`, `min`, or `inputMode`; a grep for `<form`, `step=`, `inputMode` in the file returns nothing. Because no `<form>` wraps them, the "blocks submit" path cannot happen and `e.target.value` still carries "12.50" to `upd`; Chrome draws no default `:invalid` outline (only Firefox's `:user-invalid` glow), so the stated Chrome repro is inaccurate. What remains real: the default step=1 leaves the control in stepMismatch and Chrome mutates a focused number input on scroll-wheel. In-repo precedent client/src/components/schedule/CreateAppointmentModal.jsx:2769 `step="0.01"`. Cosmetic/footgun only on a Tier 2 admin form → P3. |
| F0441 | `client/src/pages/admin/EquipmentMaintenancePage.jsx:1477` | Equipment maintenance record and mileage log saves fail silently (console.error only, no error UI in the file) | P1 → P2 | client/src/pages/admin/EquipmentMaintenancePage.jsx:29-40 `function af(...)` → `.then((r) => { if (!r.ok) throw new Error(\`HTTP ${r.status}\`); return r.json(); })`; :1440-1479 record modal `submit` → `try { await af(…, { method: "POST", … }); onDone(); } catch (e) { console.error(e); } setSaving(false);`; :1799-1822 mileage `submit` identical. Real. Over-rated: the claim "no error surface anywhere on the page" is wrong (:303), the modal stays open with the operator's form intact (no data loss), and it is a single-operator ops page → P2. |

| F0475 | `client/src/pages/admin/InventoryPage.jsx:2535` | Money and count columns in Tier 2 tables are left-aligned (the cited cells are monospace, so figures are already tabular) | P2 → P3 | Partly refuted. Left-aligned mono money columns on Tier 2 pages are a consistency nit, and the contract's 'Do not replace legacy inline styles globally' rule plus the alignment convention question make it a design call, not an autofix. |
