Siblings use `<Link>` (AgentActivityTab.jsx:183, AgentOpsPage.jsx:177). Over-rated: the link still lands on the right page with the right query; the cost is a bundle reload and lost hub state, no data or auth loss — P3 polish/consistency, not P2. |
| F0511 | `client/src/pages/admin/ProjectsPage.jsx:3776` | ProjectsPage mixes Tier 1 primitives + Tailwind with 157 inline style blocks and hand-rolled buttons/inputs | P1 → P2 | The mix is real inside single components, not just the file: client/src/pages/admin/ProjectsPage.jsx:5 imports `Badge, Button, Dialog, DialogBody, DialogFooter, Select`; useConfirmDialog (:46-109) renders the ui `<Dialog>` (:80) and `<Button>` (:96,:99) around an input styled `style={{ ...inputStyle, marginTop: 12 }}` (:91); the detail-pane component renders the ui `<Badge tone={status.tone}>` at :2439 beside hand-rolled `style={{ ...btnPrimary … }}` buttons at :3187/:3200/:3221 and `inputStyle` inputs at :2604/:2628/:2840; counts 157 `style={{` vs 19 `className=`; constants at :3762-3797. Over-rated: :111-114 documents the owner-directed C1/C2 restyle — 'the detail pane's chrome constants now carry the V2 zinc ramp (whites/greys/black — owner direction). Names kept so the ~200 consumer sites need no churn' — so both systems paint the same zinc palette (btnPrimary #18181B, ESTIMATE_* zinc hexes) and there is no user-visible inconsistency. A rule-consistency debt with a documented interim decision is P2, not P1; finishing the migration or swapping the controls is an owner-scoped choice. |
| F0516 | `client/src/pages/admin/RevenuePage.jsx:202` | ~68 admin data effects fetch without an AbortController or mounted/sequence guard (stale-response races) | P2 → P3 | Cited effects are unguarded as claimed: client/src/pages/admin/RevenuePage.jsx:202-206 `useEffect(() => { adminFetch(\`/admin/ads/revenue-attribution?period=${period}\`).then((d) => setAttr(d)).catch(() => {}); }, [period]);`; AdsPage.jsx:714-722 same shape keyed on period; KnowledgePage.jsx:306-313 keyed on articleId; CustomerHealthTabs.jsx:680-686 keyed on customerId — no AbortController, no cancelled/sequence flag, no cleanup. Census over client/src/pages/admin + components/admin (non-test): 11 files with AbortController, ~34 with a `let cancelled/alive/mounted = …` guard, ~121 fetching inside useEffect — so roughly 70 unguarded, matching the '~68'. Over-rated: the wrong-data outcome needs a slow network plus rapid re-toggling of the same control, is transient, and self-corrects on the next change; mount-only effects only risk a no-op setState (React 18 dropped the unmounted-setState warning). Systemic-but-rare stale-response race → P3. |
| F0524 | `client/src/pages/admin/SEOPage.jsx:920` | Geo-grid scan poller (setInterval every 20s, up to 18 ticks) is never cleared on unmount or tab change | P2 → P3 | Leak is real: client/src/pages/admin/SEOPage.jsx:919-929 inside runScan: `let n = 0; const t = setInterval(async () => { n += 1; const s = await adminFetch("/admin/seo/geo-grid").catch(() => null); if (!s?.scanning \|\| n > 18) { clearInterval(t); setRunning(false); … loadHeat(); } }, 20000);` — `t` is a handler-local const; the only `clearInterval` in the file is :924, GeoGridTab (:864) has no timer ref or useEffect cleanup (grep timerRef/intervalRef/pollRef = 0). Over-rated: :898-905 `selRef` plus the :926-927 guard already stop a stale poll from writing heat for a changed selection, React 18 silently ignores setRunning/setHeat after unmount, and the loop self-terminates at `n > 18` — worst case is 18 extra authenticated GETs over 6 minutes after leaving the tab, with no wrong data and no crash. Resource nit → P3. |
| F0542 | `client/src/pages/admin/TaxPage.jsx:171` | TaxPage `isMobile` is a module-level constant evaluated once — layout never responds to resize/rotation | P1 → P2 | Real: client/src/pages/admin/TaxPage.jsx:171 `const isMobile = typeof window !== "undefined" && window.innerWidth < 640;` is module scope, read at 95-97 (StatCard), 262, 2649, 2959, 3663, 3891, 5046, and TaxPage imports no hook (lines 1-19). client/src/hooks/useIsMobile.js exists and registers a resize listener (lines 8-12). But the premise 'Every other page derives the breakpoint from a hook' is false: the identical module-level constant exists in client/src/components/equipment/MileageTab.jsx:26, pages/admin/EquipmentMaintenancePage.jsx:117, ReviewsPage.jsx:23, LawnAssessmentPanel.jsx:39, CommunicationsPage.jsx:57, PricingStrategyPage.jsx:83 — a repo-wide legacy idiom, not a TaxPage regression. Impact is limited to a resize or rotation AFTER the lazy chunk (App.jsx lazyWithRetry) first evaluates: a phone that loads the page in portrait renders the mobile layout correctly. Post-load reflow on a tablet rotation / desktop narrow is a degradation, not a broken surface → P2, not P1. |
| F0552 | `client/src/pages/admin/TimeTrackingPage.jsx:3059` | Four TimeTracking tables render with no horizontal-scroll wrapper — the page scrolls sideways on phones | P1 → P2 | Real, and larger than reported: client/src/pages/admin/TimeTrackingPage.jsx has nine `<table` (888, 1031, 1354, 1908, 2000, 2072, 2282, 3059, 5226) and only three `overflowX: "auto"` wrappers (886, 1352, 5224), so SIX are unwrapped — the cited 1031 (parent: expanded-day block, `fontSize: 11`), 1908, 2282 (`OvertimeTable` returns a bare `<table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", fontSize: 12 }}>`), 3059 (`<div style={sCard}> <table … fontSize: 13>` with Photo/Name/Phone/Email/Status/Actions at 3066), plus 2000 and 2072 (same `<div style={sCard}>` shape). `sCard` (71-78) sets no overflow; no global CSS wraps raw tables (index.css:245-246 only decorates wrappers that already exist). Related claims spot-checked: GBPManagement.jsx:1806 and BankingPage.jsx:1340 are bare tables; WinLossSlicesCard 7 tables/0 wrappers; SourcePerformanceCard 2/0; EmailAutomationsPanelV2.jsx:106-108 `Card p-0 overflow-hidden` + `table w-full` clips. Over-rated because 'the whole admin page gains a horizontal scrollbar' is not what happens: AdminLayoutV2.jsx:626-648 makes `.admin-main` (`minWidth: 0, maxWidth: "100%", overflowY: "auto"`) the scroll container, so the tab content pans sideways inside the shell while header/tab bar stay put — every cell remains reachable. That is the same defect class the audit itself rates P2 at F0555 (ToolHealthPage:473); P2 is the consistent rating. |
| F0557 | `client/src/pages/admin/TriageInboxTabV2.jsx:656` | Triage card action row squeezes the caller name to nothing on phones | P1 → P2 | Structure is as claimed: TriageInboxTabV2.jsx:623 `<div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">` (no wrap), :624 `<div className="min-w-0 flex-1">`, :626 name `text-14 font-medium text-zinc-900 truncate`, actions :656 `<div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">` holding up to three `<Button size="sm">` (Dismiss/Deny/Accept, each with a 13px icon + `mr-1`). Button.jsx SIZES.sm = `h-11 sm:h-7 px-3 text-11` with BASE `uppercase … tracking-label`, so on a phone each is a 44px-tall uppercase pill ≈75-95px wide; three plus two `gap-2` ≈ 270px in a `shrink-0` group. Usable row width at 375px: shell 16×2 (AdminLayoutV2.jsx:638-639) + Triage `px-3` 12×2 (:502) + CardBody `p-4` 16×2 (ui/Card.jsx:49) + `border-l-[3px] pl-3` (:611-616) ≈ 270px, so the `flex-1 min-w-0` column is squeezed toward zero and `truncate` hides the caller name; on the 2-button auto-routed view it gets ~85px. This is a class-list fact, not a runtime one. Downgraded from P1: exact pixels depend on font metrics, and the card stays actionable — the synopsis (:697), Badge and buttons still render — so it is a degraded phone layout, not a blocked flow. |
| F0561 | `client/src/pages/admin/WinLossSlicesCard.jsx:32` | 148 raw <table> in admin vs 10 ui/Table consumers; Tier-1 files drop the overflow-x wrapper ui/Table provides | P2 → P3 | Duplication half holds: ui/Table.jsx:4-9 wraps `<table>` in `<div className="w-full overflow-x-auto">`; WinLossSlicesCard.jsx hand-rolls seven `<table className="w-full text-13">` (:32, :154, :191, :216, :255, :276, :328); only DocumentRequestsPage.jsx imports `Table` from the ui barrel; ~143 raw `<table` remain across pages/admin + components/admin. The responsive harm is refuted for the cited file: every WinLoss table is 2–4 short-text columns (`Clean lookup` / `62%` / `n=12`; Band/Clean/Flagged; Age/Won/Lost/Open at :191-195 of the 140-340 window) with `w-full` and no `whitespace-nowrap`, so cells wrap and nothing forces page-level horizontal scroll at 375px; SourcePerformanceCard.jsx:119/:165 are the same shape. EmailAutomationsPanelV2.jsx:106-108 (`Card className="p-0 overflow-hidden"` around a 6-column `px-4` table) is the one real clip candidate, and NewsletterPage.jsx:1504 already wraps its sibling in `overflow-x-auto`. Migrating to `<Table>/<TR>/<TH>` is not behaviour-preserving (adds `border-collapse`, `hover:bg-zinc-50` rows, uppercase 11px TH) — it is a design-system consolidation that needs a ruling on whether ui/Table becomes mandatory for Tier 1 cards. |
